*sva-savitrī-lasaj-jyotiḥ-*  
*kaṁsa-trāsa-viṣāda-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vasudeva*—in Vasudeva; *ullasat*—who exhibited; *śakte*—Your potency; *devakī*—of Devakī; *aṣṭama*—as the eighth (child); *garbha-ga*—who entered the womb; *sva-savitrī*—in Your own mother; *lasat*—luminous; *jyotiḥ*—by the effulgence; *kaṁsa*—for Kaṁsa; *trāsa*—fear; *viṣāda*—and discouragement; *kṛt*—who created.

**Перевод:**

**You shone with potency within the body of Vasudeva, entered Devakī’s womb as her eighth child, and imparted to Your mother’s body a luminous effulgence that frightened and disheartened Kaṁsa.**

### Text 44

*sadā-kaṁsa-mano-vartin*  
*brahma-rudrādy-abhiṣṭuta*  
*satyātmaka jagan-nātha*  
*śuddha-sāttvika-rūpa-bhṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sadā*—constantly; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *manaḥ*—in the mind; *vartin*—who were present; *brahma-rudra-ādi*—by Brahmā, Rudra, and others; *abhiṣṭuta*—who were praised; *satya-ātmaka*—O embodiment of truth; *jagat*-n*ātha*—O Lord of the universe; *śuddha-sāttvika*—of pure goodness; *rūpa-bhṛt*—who assume forms.

**Перевод:**

**You lived always in Kaṁsa’s mind. And Brahmā, Rudra, and other demigods offered You prayers: O embodiment of truth, O Lord of the universe, who appear in forms of pure goodness!**

**Комментарий:**

The second half of this verse begins a summary of the prayers recited by the demigods to Kṛṣṇa when He lay in Devakī’s womb. Kṛṣṇa appears in various incarnations, all of them free from any effect of Māyā’s illusion.

### Text 45

*bhaktaika-labhya-sarvasva*  
*sarva-sarvārtha-kṛd-vapuḥ*  
*rūpa-nāmāśritāviṣṭa*  
*janma-mātra-dharārti-hṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhakta*—by Your devotees; *eka*—only; *labhya*—achievable; *sarva-sva*—Your everything; *sarva*—for everyone; *sarva*—all; *artha*—goals; *kṛt*—creating; *vapuḥ*—whose body; *rūpa*—of Your beauty; *nāma*—and names; *āśrita*—of those who take shelter; *āviṣṭa*—who enter the hearts; *janma-mātra*—just by taking birth; *dharā*—of the earth; *ārti*—of the travail; *hṛt*—O reliever.

**Перевод:**

**You give Yourself and all that is Yours to Your devotees alone. It is from Your body that everyone achieves all goals. You enter the hearts of those who take shelter of Your beauty and Your names. And just by Your birth You relieve the earth of her travail.**

### Text 46

*svar-bhūr-bhūṣaṇa-pādābja*  
*vinodaikārtha-jāta he*  
*jaya bhū-bhāra-haraṇa*  
*devāśvāsita-mātṛka*  
*(namaḥ 12)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*svaḥ*—of heaven; *bhūḥ*—and of earth; *bhūṣaṇa*—the ornament; *pāda-abja*—whose lotus feet; *vinoda*—of enjoyment; *eka*—only; *artha*—with the purpose; *jāta*—born; *he*—O; *jaya*—victory to You; *bhū*—of the earth; *bhāra*—of the burden; *haraṇa*—O remover; *deva*—by the demigods; *āśvāsita*—consoled; *mātṛka*—whose mother; *namaḥ 12*—obeisance 12.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord whose lotus feet are the ornament of earth and heaven, You have taken birth just to enjoy Your pastimes. Victory to You, remover of the earth’s burden, who had the demigods console Your mother! (Obeisance 12)**

**Комментарий:**

Just as Kṛṣṇa protected the demigods and the residents of the three worlds by descending in the past in *avatāras* such as fish, horse, and tortoise, the demigods pray that He now kindly protect them by coming to remove the burden of the earth.

*iti daśama-skandhe dvitīyo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the second chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 3

### Text 47

*bhādra-kṛṣṇāṣṭamī-jāta*  
*prājāpatyarkṣa-sambhava*  
*mahī-maṅgala-vistārin*  
*sādhu-citta-prasādaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhādra*—of the month of Bhādrapada; *kṛṣṇa*—of the dark fortnight; *aṣṭamī*—on the eighth day; *jāta*—who were born; *prājāpatya*-*ṛkṣa*—under the lunar mansion of Prajāpati; *sambhava*—whose appearance; *mahī*—of the earth; *maṅgala*—of the good fortune; *vistārin*—O expander; *sādhu*—of saintly persons; *citta*—to the hearts; *prasādaka*—O bringer of satisfaction.

**Перевод:**

**You were born in the dark fortnight of Bhādra, on the eighth day, under the lunar mansion of Prajāpati. Thus You expanded the good fortune of the earth and brought satisfaction to the hearts of the saintly.**

**Комментарий:**

Of the twenty-seven constellations of the lunar zodiac, Kṛṣṇa was born while the moon was passing through Rohiṇī, which is ruled by Prajāpati.

### Text 48

*maharṣi-mānasollāsa*  
*santoṣita-sura-vraja*  
*niśītha-samayodbhūta*  
*vasudeva-priyātmaja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mahā-ṛṣi*—of the great sages; *mānasa*—the minds; *ullāsa*—O delighter; *santoṣita*—who fully satisfied; *sura-vraja*—all the demigods; *niśītha-samaya*—in the middle of the night; *udbhūta*—who appeared; *vasudeva-priya*—of the beloved wife of Vasudeva; *ātmaja*—O son.

**Перевод:**

**O son of Vasudeva’s beloved wife, when You appeared in the middle of the night You thrilled the minds of the great sages and fully satisfied all the demigods.**

**Комментарий:**

Because Kṛṣṇa was born from the body of Devakī, she cannot be a material person. Śukadeva Gosvāmī confirms this in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (10.3.8):

*devakyāṁ deva-rūpiṇyāṁ*  
*viṣṇuḥ sarva-guhā-śayaḥ*  
*āvirāsīd yathā prācyāṁ*  
*diśīndur iva puṣkalaḥ*

**“Then the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Viṣṇu, who is situated in the core of everyone’s heart, appeared from the heart of Devakī in the dense darkness of night, like the full moon rising on the eastern horizon, because Devakī was of the same category as Śrī Kṛṣṇa.”**

According to the *Mahopaniṣad, so ’nutpattir alaya eva hariḥ:* “Lord Hari has no material birth and no material home.”

### Text 49

*devakī-garbha-sad-ratna*  
*balabhadra-priyānuja*  
*gadāgraja prasīdāśu*  
*subhadrā-pūrva-jāva mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*devakī*—of Devakī; *garbha*—of the womb; *sat-ratna*—O precious jewel; *balabhadra*—of Balabhadra; *priya*—dear; *anuja*—O younger brother; *gada-agraja*—O elder brother of Gada; *prasīda*—please become satisfied; *āśu*—quickly; *subhadrā*—of Subhadrā; *pūrva-ja*—O elder brother; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**O precious jewel of Devakī’s womb, dear younger brother of Balabhadra! Elder brother of Gada and Subhadrā! Please quickly be satisfied with me. Please protect me.**

**Комментарий:**

The protection Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī requests from Śrī Kṛṣṇa is to be allowed to remember the Lord’s pastimes always.

### Text 50

*āścarya-bāla māṁ pāhi*  
*divya-rūpa-pradarśaka*  
*kārāgārāndhakāra-ghna*  
*sūtikā-gṛha-bhūṣaṇa*  
*(namaḥ 13)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*āścarya*—wondrous; *bāla*—O child; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *divya*—radiant; *rūpa*—of Your form; *pradarśaka*—O displayer; *kārāgāra*—of the prison; *andhakāra*—of the darkness; *ghna*—O destroyer; *sūtikā-gṛha*—of the delivery chamber; *bhūṣaṇa*—O ornament; *namaḥ 13*—obeisance 13.

**Перевод:**

**O wondrous child, displaying Your radiant form of Godhead, destroying the prison’s darkness, O ornament of the delivery chamber, please protect me! (Obeisance 13)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa at first appeared before Vasudeva and Devakī with all the magnificence of the Supreme Lord. He had lotus eyes, divine ornaments such as Śrīvatsa and Kaustubha, and four arms, holding conch, disc, club, and lotus. He shone brilliantly with the glow of His priceless *vaidūrya* gems and His jewel-studded crown, earrings, and other ornaments. The radiance from His jewelry made the dark dungeon as bright as daylight outdoors.

### Text 51

*vasudeva-stutaṁ sākṣād-*  
*adṛśyātma-pradarśakam*  
*sat-praviṣṭāpraviṣṭaṁ tvāṁ*  
*vande kāraṇa-kāraṇam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vasudeva-stutam*—praised by Vasudeva; *sākṣāt*—directly; *adṛśya*—not visible; *ātma*—Your personal form; *pradarśakam*—who made visible; *sat*—Your real creation; *praviṣṭa*—who have entered; *apraviṣṭam*—and not entered; *tvām*—to You; *vande*—I offer respects; *kāraṇa-kāraṇam*—to the cause of all causes.

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respects to You, who received the prayers of Vasudeva. Though Your personal form is not readily visible, You sometimes choose to reveal Yourself. Your creation, which is real, You have entered and yet not entered. You are the cause of all causes.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse and the next summarize Vasudeva’s prayers to newborn Kṛṣṇa. Forgetting that Kṛṣṇa was his son, Vasudeva glorified Him as the Supreme Lord. Ordinary eyes cannot see the Supreme Person, but the eyes of those who have ecstatic love for Him can see Him everywhere. And even to the ignorant He shows Himself in the form of His material creation. This creation, produced by His *prakṛti,* is real, and He personally enters it as the witnessing Supersoul, although He actually has no contact with the material world. The common meaning of the word *a*-*praviṣṭa* is “not entered.” But the prefix *a-* can sometimes be read as “similar to.” In that case, *a-praviṣṭa* would mean “appearing to have entered.” Whatever or whoever might be considered the cause of creation, Kṛṣṇa was present before all. Thus He alone is the original cause.

### Text 52

*siddhākartṛtva-kartṛtvaṁ*  
*jagat-kṣema-karodayam*  
*daitya-mukti-da-kāruṇyaṁ*  
*sva-jana-prema-vardhanam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*siddha*—who have managed; *akartṛtva*—being inactive; *kartṛtvam—*and being active; *jagat*—of the whole universe; *kṣema*—the welfare; *kara*—creating; *udayam*—whose appearance; *daitya*—to the demons; *mukti-da*—giving liberation; *kāruṇyam*—whose mercy; *sva-jana*—of Your servants; *prema*—of the pure love; *vardhanam*—to the increaser.

**Перевод:**

**Managing to be both active and still, You appear for the welfare of the universe. You are so merciful that You give liberation to the demons. And You increase your servants’ pure love for You.**

**Комментарий:**

Although the idea that the Supreme Lord acts and does not act seems contradictory, it is verified by the Vedic scriptures. He has no material desires and no material instruments of action, but as the Lord of the universe He does all the work of creating and maintaining it. Material nature and her modes assist Him in this work. And He has His own free play, His *līlā,* which mundane scholars can never understand.

When Kṛṣṇa appears on earth, He delivers His pure devotees by killing wicked demons who act under the guise of kings and by doing whatever else is needed for the benefit of His devotees. The enemies He kills also benefit, because they obtain liberation. Simply remembering this causeless mercy increases a devotee’s love for Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 53

*devakī-nayanānanda*  
*jaya bhīta-prasū-stuta*  
*nirguṇādhyātma-dīpāti-*  
*laya-kāraka kāla-sṛk*

**Пословный перевод:**

*devakī*—of Devakī; *nayana*—for the eyes; *ānanda*—O ecstasy; *jaya*—victory to You; *bhīta*—filled with fear; *prasū*—by Your mother; *stuta*—who were prayed to; *nirguṇa*—who are beyond the modes of nature; *adhyātma*—of consciousness; *dīpa*—O lamp; *ati-laya*—the final annihilation; *kāraka*—who bring about; *kāla-sṛk*—who send forth the potency of time.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, O ecstasy for the eyes of Devakī! Your frightened mother offered You prayers: You are beyond the modes of nature, yet You are the lamp who lights the consciousness of the living beings. It is You who finally dissolve this world, having sent forth the potency of time.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse and the next summarize Mother Devakī’s prayers to Kṛṣṇa. But first Śrīla Sanātana asks Kṛṣṇa to be victorious by showing His supreme excellences and releasing His parents from their imprisonment.

When Devakī saw the marks of the Supreme Lord on the body of her son, she put aside her motherly affection and worshiped Him with reverential prayers.

### Text 54

*sva-pādāśrita-mṛtyu-ghna*  
*māṁsa-dṛg-dṛṣṭy-ayogya he*  
*lokopahāsa-bhītāmbā-*  
*vṛta-divyāṅga-saṁvṛte*  
*(namaḥ 14)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-pāda*—at Your feet; *āśrita*—of those who take shelter; *mṛtyu*—of death; *ghna*—O killer; *māṁsa*—of flesh; *dṛk*—with eyes; *dṛṣṭi*—whose being seen; *ayogya*—not possible; *he*—O; *loka*—of the ordinary people; *upahāsa*—of the ridicule; *bhīta*—fearful; *ambā*—by Your mother; *vṛta*—requested; *divya*—divine; *aṅga*—of Your body; *saṁvṛte*—the covering up; *namaḥ 14*—obeisance 14.

**Перевод:**

**O killer of death for those who take shelter at Your feet, mere eyes of flesh can never see You. At the request of Your mother, who feared public ridicule, You covered Your appearance as the Supreme Godhead. (Obeisance 14)**

**Комментарий:**

Mother Devakī was concerned that people would think it preposterous that the same Supreme Godhead who keeps all the planets in His body at the time of universal annihilation had taken shelter in her womb. She therefore entreated Him to change His appearance with four arms, holding weapons, to that of an ordinary infant.

### Text 55

*pitṛ-prāg-janma-kathaka*  
*sva-datta-vara-yantrita*  
*mahārādhana-santoṣa*  
*tri-janmātmajatā-gata*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pitṛ*—of Your parents; *prāk*—previous; *janma*—about the births; *kathaka*—O teller; *sva-datta*—given by Yourself; *vara*—by the boons; *yantrita*—who were compelled; *mahā-ārādhana*—by the excellent worship; *santoṣa*—who were fully satisfied; *tri-janma*—in three births; *ātmajatā*—the role of their son; *gata*—who assumed.

**Перевод:**

**You described to Your parents their previous births. Compelled by the boons You had given them and fully satisfied by their excellent worship, for three births You became their son.**

**Комментарий:**

In texts 55 through 57 Kṛṣṇa responds to His parents’ prayers.

### Text 56

*mahānanda-prasū-tāta*  
*līlā-mānuṣa-bālaka*  
*narākṛti-para-brahman*  
*prakṛṣṭākāra sundara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mahā-ānanda*—great ecstasy; *prasū*—for Your mother; *tāta*—and father; *līlā*—as Your pastime; *mānuṣa*—human; *bālaka*—O child; *nara*—of a human being; *ākṛti*—in the form; *para-brahman*—O Supreme Absolute Truth; *prakṛṣṭa*—perfect; *ākāra*—whose bodily form; *sundara*—O beautiful one.

**Перевод:**

**Your mother and father felt great ecstasy, and then for Your pastimes You became like a human baby. O Supreme Absolute Truth in human form, perfect in Your appearance, O beautiful Lord!**

### Text 57

*janakopāya-nirdeṣṭar*  
*yaśodā-jāta-māya he*  
*śāyita-dvāḥstha-paurāder*  
*mohitāgāra-rakṣaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*janaka*—to Your father; *upāya*—of what was to be done; *nirdeṣṭaḥ*—O teller; *yaśodā—*from Yaśodā; *jāta-māya*—who made Māyā take birth; *he*—O; *śāyita*—who made fall asleep; *dvāḥ-stha*—the doorkeepers; *paura*—residents of the fortress; *ādeḥ*—and others; *mohita*—who made lose consciousness; *āgāra*—of the prison; *rakṣaka*—the guards.

**Перевод:**

**You then told Your father what to do. Now that the illusory energy Māyā had been born of Mother Yaśodā, the doorkeepers and everyone else in the fortress fell asleep. Even the prison guards lost consciousness.**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa in His two-armed baby form advised His father to carry Him at once to Gokula, on the other side of the river Yamunā. By the influence of Yogamāyā, who had also just been born, Vasudeva, unnoticed, was able to remove Kṛṣṇa from the prison.

### Text 58

*sva-śakty-udghāṭitāśeṣa-*  
*kavāṭa pitṛ-vāhaka*  
*śeṣoraga-phaṇā-chatra*  
*yamunā-datta-sat-patha*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-śakti*—by Your personal potency; *udghāṭita*—who made to open; *aśeṣa*—all; *kavāṭa*—the doorways; *pitṛ*—Your father; *vāhaka*—whose carrier; *śeṣa-uraga*—of the serpent Ananta Śeṣa; *phaṇā*—the hoods; *chatra*—whose umbrella; *yamunā*—by the Yamunā; *datta*—given; *sat*—suitable; *patha*—whose path.

**Перевод:**

**By Your potency all the doors opened wide. And as Your father carried You, the hoods of the serpent Ananta Śeṣa served as Your umbrella, and the Yamunā gave You a suitable path.**

**Комментарий:**

While a terrible storm raged and the Yamunā tossed about with huge waves, Vasudeva approached the river not knowing how he would be able to cross. But Śrī Yamunā subsided just for Vasudeva’s convenience, giving Him a dry path by which he easily reached the other side.

### Text 59

*vraja-mūrta-mahā-bhāgya*  
*yaśodā-talpa-śāyita*  
*nidrā-mohita-nandādi-*  
*yaśodāviditehita*  
*(namaḥ 15)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vraja*—of Vraja; *mūrta*—personified; *mahā-bhāgya*—O good fortune; *yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *talpa*—on the bed; *śāyita*—who were laid to sleep; *nidrā*—in sleep; *mohita*—who made oblivious; *nanda-ādi*—Nanda and the others; *yaśodā*—Yaśodā; *avidita*—not knowing; *īhita*—what You had done; *namaḥ 15*—obeisance 15.

**Перевод:**

**O personified good fortune of Vraja, You were laid to sleep on Yaśodā’s bed. And Nanda, Yaśodā, and the others, oblivious in deep slumber, knew nothing of what You had done. (Obeisance 15)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe tṛtīyo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the third chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 4

### Text 60

*kaṁsa-ghātita-durgaṁ tvāṁ*  
*vande durgoditodbhavam*  
*kaṁsa-vismāpakaṁ tāta-*  
*mātṛ-bandha-vimocakam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kaṁsa*—by Kaṁsa; *ghātita*—attacked with the intent to kill; *durgam*—Durgā; *tvām*—to You; *vande*—I offer homage; *durgā*—by Durgā; *udita*—told; *udbhavam*—about whose appearance; *kaṁsa*—to Kaṁsa; *vismāpakam*—astonishing; *tāta-mātṛ*—of Your father and mother; *bandha*—from bondage; *vimocakam*—O releaser.

**Перевод:**

**I offer my homage to You, who made Kaṁsa try to kill Durgā and who then astonished him by having her tell him about Your birth. You then made him release Your father and mother from bondage.**

**Комментарий:**

Counting Yogamāyā as the eighth child of Devakī, Kaṁsa seized her from her mother’s arms and threw her to the ground to kill her. But she rose into the sky, assumed her Durgā form, and rebuked Kaṁsa for his foolishness, much to his amazement. Bewildered by the apparent falsity of the prediction a voice from the sky had spoken earlier, during Devakī’s wedding procession, Kaṁsa let Vasudeva and Devakī leave the prison.

### Text 61

*sabhaya-smṛti-saṁśuddha-*  
*citta-kaṁsa-viveka-dam*  
*kaṁsātma-jñāna-saṁślāghi-*  
*pitṛ-mātṛ-kṣamā-pradam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sa-bhaya*—fearful; *smṛti*—from the remembrance; *saṁśuddha*—cleansed; *citta*—whose heart; *kaṁsa*—to Kaṁsa; *viveka*—good judgment; *dam*—who gave; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *ātma-jñāna*—the spiritual realization; *saṁślāghi*—congratulating; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—to Your father and mother; *kṣamā*—forbearance; *pradam*—giving.

**Перевод:**

**You gave good judgment to Kaṁsa, whose heart was cleansed by fear when he recognized the evil of his acts. And You gave Your father and mother the forbearance to congratulate him for his spiritual realization.**

### Text 62

*durmantri-gaṇa-vāg-jāla-*  
*kaṁsa-durmāna-vardhanam*  
*sad-atikrama-durmantra-*  
*kṣayitāsura-jīvitam*  
*(namaḥ 16)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*durmantri-gaṇa*—of all his bad advisors; *vāk*—of the words; *jāla*—by the network; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *durmāna*—the evil intentions; *vardhanam*—stirring up; *sat*—of faultless persons; *atikrama—*for abuse; *durmantra*—by their bad advice; *kṣayita*—who diminished; *asura*—of the demon; *jīvitam*—the life span; *namaḥ 16*—obeisance 16.

**Перевод:**

**Through the devious words of all of Kaṁsa’s bad advisors, You again stirred up his evil intentions. And by his ill-advised abuse of faultless persons, You cut short the length of his demonic life. (Obeisance 16)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe caturtho ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the fourth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 5

### Text 63

*pradatta-pūrva-sva-padābja-sauhṛda-*  
*pradāna-dīkṣocita-deśa-saṅgata*  
*sva-sevaka-brahma-sukhādhikotsava*  
*premākara krīḍana-kṛn namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pradatta*—having placed; *pūrva*—previously; *sva*—Your; *pada-abja*—lotus feet; *sauhṛda*—of affection; *pradāna*—for giving; *dīkṣā*—Your intention; *ucita*—suitable; *deśa*—in the place; *saṅgata*—whose proper situation; *sva-sevaka*—for Your servants; *brahma-sukha*—than the happiness of Brahman; *adhika*—greater; *utsava*—O festival; *prema*—of pure love; *ākara*—O reservoir; *krīḍana-kṛt*—O performer of sporting pastimes; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You.

**Перевод:**

**Now that You had put Your lotus feet in Vraja, the most suitable place, You vowed to give those who reside there Your wholehearted affection, awarding them Your company. O reservoir of pure love, performer of sporting pastimes, festival of joy for Your servants—joy greater than the happiness of Brahman—I bow down to You.**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa’s firm intention, His *dīkṣā,* was to always give the greatest possible pleasure to the residents of Vraja:

*tasmān mac-charaṇaṁ goṣṭhaṁ*  
*man-nāthaṁ mat-parigraham*  
*gopāye svātma-yogena*  
*so ’yaṁ me vrata āhitaḥ*

**“I must therefore protect the cowherd community by My transcendental potency, for I am their shelter, I am their master, and indeed they are My own family. After all, I have taken a vow to protect My devotees.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.25.18)**

Now Kṛṣṇa was located in the most suitable place to delight and protect His devotees—Śrī Gokula.

### Text 64

*nanda-nandana sañjāta-*  
*jāta-karma-mahotsava*  
*nānā-dānaugha-kṛt-tāta*  
*śrīmad-gokula-maṅgala*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nanda-nandana*—O darling son of Nanda; *sañjāta*—happened; *jāta-karma*—at whose birth ceremony; *mahā-utsava*—a grand festival; *nānā*—various; *dāna*—of gifts; *ogha*—heaps; *kṛt*—who caused to make; *tāta*—Your father; *śrīmat*—blessed; *gokula*—for Gokula; *maṅgala*—O source of auspiciousness.

**Перевод:**

**O source of all good fortune for blessed Gokula, O darling son of Nanda, at the grand festival of Your birth ceremony You moved Your father to give heaps of gifts in charity.**

**Комментарий:**

In Kaṁsa’s prison when Kṛṣṇa was born, Vasudeva could give charity only in his mind. Now Nanda Mahārāja gave away many cows, mountains of grain, and other gifts. Kṛṣṇa’s presence brought all splendor and good fortune to Gokula.

### Text 65

*kṛtālaṅkāra-gopāla-*  
*gopī-gaṇa-kṛtotsava*  
*gopī-prema-mud-āśīr-bhāk*  
*vraja-go-rasa-kīrṇa he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kṛta-alaṅkāra*—who decorated themselves; *gopāla*—by the cowherds; *gopī-gaṇa*—and the cowherd women; *kṛta*—performed; *utsava*—the celebration; *gopī*—by the cowherd women; *prema*—with love; *mut*—and with joy; *āśīḥ*—of blessings; *bhāk*—O receiver; *vraja*—of Vraja; *go-rasa*—with the milk products; *kīrṇa*—who were smeared; *he*—O.

**Перевод:**

**During this celebration, the cowherds and their women festively decorated themselves. The women offered You blessings with happiness and love and smeared You with the milk products of Vraja.**

**Комментарий:**

For newborn Kṛṣṇa’s good luck, the grown-up *gopīs* rubbed yogurt, milk, ghee, and butter on His body, together with turmeric and various fragrant powders and oils.

### Text 66

*nanda-vraja-janānandin*  
*nanda-sammānita-vraja*  
*datta-vraja-mahā-bhūte*  
*śrī-yaśodā-stanan-dhaya*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nanda-vraja*—of Nanda’s cowherd village; *jana*—on the people; *ānandin*—O bestower of bliss; *nanda*—through Nanda; *sammānita*—who honored; *vraja*—the people of Vraja; *datta*—who gave as a gift; *vraja*—Vraja; *mahā-bhūte*—with the greatest opulence; *śrī-yaśodā*—of Śrī Yaśodā; *stanam*—from the breast; *dhaya*—O drinker.

**Перевод:**

**O bliss of the people of Nanda’s Vraja! Through Nanda Mahārāja You properly honored the Vraja-vāsīs, bestowing on Vraja the gift of the greatest opulence as You drank from the breast of Śrī Yaśodā.**

**Комментарий:**

The Vraja-vāsīs, both men and women, derived great pleasure from throwing yogurt, ghee, milk, and butter at one another during the festival celebrating Kṛṣṇa’s birth. Inspired by Kṛṣṇa as the Supersoul, Nanda Mahārāja honored the bards, chroniclers, laudators, and others with presents of clothing, jewelry, and cows. With Kṛṣṇa now present, Vraja became the playground of the goddess of fortune and so was filled with all riches.

### Text 67

*prāpta-putra-mahā-ratna-*  
*rakṣā-vyākula-tāta he*  
*kara-dānārtha-mathurā-*  
*gata-nanda-gṛhāvita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prāpta*—obtained; *putra*—of a son; *mahā-ratna*—the rare jewel; *rakṣā*—about protecting; *vyākula*—anxious; *tāta*—whose father; *he*—O; *kara*—taxes; *dāna-artha*—for paying; *mathurā-gata*—gone to Mathurā; *nanda*—of Nanda; *gṛha*—the household; *avita*—who protected.

**Перевод:**

**Your father Nanda was always anxious over how to protect this rare jewel of a son he had obtained. Yet when Nanda went to Mathurā to pay taxes, You saw to protecting his household.**

### Text 68

*vasudeva-śubha-praśna-*  
*samānandita-nanda me*  
*prasīda nanda-sad-vākya-*  
*vasudevāti-nandaka*  
*(namaḥ 17)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vasudeva*—of Vasudeva; *śubha*—auspicious; *praśna*—by the questions; *samānandita*—delighted; *nanda*—Nanda; *me*—with me; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *nanda*—of Nanda; *sat-vākya*—with the perfect replies; *vasudeva*—to Vasudeva; *ati-nandaka*—O giver of great pleasure; *namaḥ 17*—obeisance 17.

**Перевод:**

**Nanda, because of You, was delighted by the virtuous questions of Vasudeva, whom You greatly delighted through Nanda’s perfect replies. Please be satisfied with me. (Obeisance 17)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe pañcamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the fifth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 6

### Text 69

*vasudevoditotpāta-*  
*śaṅkā-nanda-śubhāśrita*  
*vraja-mohana-sad-veṣa-*  
*viṣa-stana-bakīkṣita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vasudeva*—by Vasudeva; *udita*—told about; *utpāta*—of calamities; *śaṅkā*—having suspicion; *nanda*—Nanda; *śubha*—for welfare; *āśrita*—taken shelter of; *vraja*—the residents of Vraja; *mohana*—bewildering; *sat*—respectable; *veṣa*—whose dress; *viṣa*—of poison; *stana*—whose breast; *bakī*—by Pūtanā, the sister of Baka; *īkṣita*—who were seen.

**Перевод:**

**When Vasudeva told Nanda he suspected danger, Nanda for good fortune took shelter of You. And indeed You were seen by Baka’s sister, poison on her breast and disguised like a fine woman to bewilder the Vraja-vāsīs.**

**Комментарий:**

To warn his guest Nanda Mahārāja, Vasudeva said,

*neha stheyaṁ bahu-tithaṁ*  
*santy upātāś ca gokule*

**“You had better not stay in this place for many days, since I know there may be some disturbances in Gokula.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.5.31)**

Nanda responded by returning home, but in his mind he also took shelter of the Supreme Lord:

*nandaḥ pathi vacaḥ śaurer*  
*na mṛṣeti vicintayan*  
*hariṁ jagāma śaraṇam*  
*utpātāgama-śaṅkitaḥ*

**“While Nanda Mahārāja was on the way home, he considered that what Vasudeva had said could not be false or useless. There must have been some danger of disturbances in Gokula. As Nanda Mahārāja thought about the danger for his beautiful son Kṛṣṇa, he was afraid, and he took shelter at the lotus feet of the supreme controller.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.6.1)**

### Text 70

*lajjā-mīlita-netrābja*  
*pūtanāṅkādhiropita*  
*bakī-prāṇa-payaḥ-pāyin*  
*pūtanā-stana-pīḍana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*lajjā*—out of shyness; *mīlita*—who closed; *netra-abja*—Your lotus eyes; *pūtanā*—of Pūtanā; *aṅka*—on the lap; *adhiropita*—who were taken; *bakī*—of Pūtanā; *prāṇa*—of the life air; *payaḥ*—and milk; *pāyin*—O drinker; *pūtanā*—of Pūtanā; *stana*—to the breast; *pīḍana*—O giver of great pain.

**Перевод:**

**Out of shyness You closed Your lotus eyes, and Pūtanā, that sister of Baka, took You upon her lap. And along with her milk You drank her life air, O torment to her breast.**

### Text 71

*pūtanākrośa-janaka*  
*pūtanā-prāṇa-śoṣaṇa*  
*ṣaṭ-krośī-vyāpi-bhī-dāyi-*  
*pūtanā-deha-pātana15–17*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pūtanā*—Pūtanā; *ākrośa*—of the loud screaming; *janaka*—who caused; *pūtanā*—of Pūtanā; *prāṇa*—the life air; *śoṣaṇa*—who sucked up; *ṣaṭ-krośī*—six *krośas* (twelve miles); *vyāpi*—covering; *bhī-dāyi*—giving fear; *pūtanā*—of Pūtanā; *deha*—the body; *pātana*—who made fall.

**Перевод:**

**Making Pūtanā scream as You sucked out her life air, You felled her body, which stretched for twelve miles and caused panic.**

### Text 72

*nānā-rakṣā-vidhāna-jña-*  
*gopa-strī-kṛta-rakṣaṇa*  
*vinyasta-rakṣā-go-dhūle*  
*go-mūtra-śakṛd-āpluta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—various; *rakṣā*—for protection; *vidhāna*—of arrangements; *jña*—who knew; *gopa-strī*—by the cowherd women; *kṛta*—performed; *rakṣaṇa*—rituals for whose protection; *vinyasta*—put; *rakṣā—*for whose security; *go*—from the cows; *dhūle*—dust; *go*—of the cows; *mūtra*—with urine; *śakṛt*—and dung; *āpluta*—who were covered.

**Перевод:**

**Cowherd women who knew various means of assuring safety did rituals for Your protection. For Your security they covered You with dust raised by the cows and bathed You in cow urine and cow dung.**

### Text 73

*gopikā-vihitājādi-*  
*bīja-nyāsābhimantrita*  
*dahyamāna-bakī-deha-*  
*saurabhya-vyāpita-kṣite*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopikā*—by the *gopīs; vihita*—applied; *aja-ādi*—of Lord Aja and others; *bīja-nyāsa*—by touching with the seed vibrations; *abhimantrita*—who were sanctified with *mantras; dahyamāna*—burning; *bakī*—of the sister of Baka; *deha*—of the body; *saurabhya*—the fragrance; *vyāpita*—who made spread; *kṣite*—through the countryside.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs sanctified You by applying the seed mantras of Aja and other forms of God to different parts of Your body. And You made Pūtanā’s burning body spread a wonderful fragrance throughout the countryside.**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa enjoyed Pūtanā’s breast milk, freeing her of all sins. Her burning body therefore created an auspicious fragrance.

### Text 74

*pūtanā-mocana dveṣṭṛ-*  
*rākṣasī-sad-gati-prada*  
*nandāghrāta-śiro-madhya*  
*jaya vismāpita-vraja*  
*(namaḥ 18)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pūtanā*—of Pūtanā; *mocana*—O deliverer; *dveṣṭṛ*—enemy; *rākṣasī—*for the demoness; *sat-gati*—of a transcendental destination; *prada*—O giver; *nanda*—by Nanda; *āghrāta*—smelled; *śiraḥ-madhya*—the top of whose head; *jaya*—victory to You; *vismāpita*—who caused great amazement; *vraja*—to the residents of Vraja; *namaḥ 18*—obeisance 18.

**Перевод:**

**O deliverer of Pūtanā, You bestowed a blessed goal upon that demoness who hated You. Nanda smelled the top of Your head with affection. Victory to You, who amazed everyone in Vraja! (Obeisance 18)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe ṣaṣṭho ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the sixth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 7

### Text 75

*autthānikotsavāmbābhi-*  
*ṣiktasañjāta-nidra-dṛk*  
*mahocca-śakaṭādhaḥ-stha-*  
*bāla-paryaṅka-śāyita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*autthānika*—of the time when a child first stands; *utsava*—at the celebration; *ambā*—by Your mother; *abhiṣikta*—who were bathed; *sañjāta*—which developed; *nidra*—fatigue; *dṛk*—whose eyes; *mahā*—large; *ucca*—and tall; *śakaṭa*—a cart; *adhaḥ*—underneath; *stha*—situated; *bāla-paryaṅka*—in a cradle; *śāyita*—who were put to rest.

**Перевод:**

**At the celebration of Your first standing up, Your mother performed Your ritual bath. Your eyes then showed signs of sleep, and she put You to rest in a cradle beneath a large, tall cart.**

### Text 76

*añjana-snigdha-nayana*  
*paryāyāṅkurita-smita*  
*līlākṣa-taralāloka*  
*mukhārpita-padāṅgule*

**Пословный перевод:**

*añjana*—with a cosmetic ointment; *snigdha*—smeared; *nayana*—whose eyes; *paryāya*—from time to time; *aṅkurita*—arising; *smita*—whose smile; *līlā*—playful; *akṣa*—with Your eyes; *tarala*—unsteady; *āloka*—whose glance; *mukha*—to Your mouth; *arpita*—put; *pada-aṅgule*—whose toe.

**Перевод:**

**Your eyes smeared with kajjala ointment, from time to time a smile appearing on Your face, You glanced about, Your playful eyes moving to and fro, and put Your toe in Your mouth.**

### Text 77

*jayotsava-kriyāsakta-*  
*dhātrī-stanyārtha-rodana*  
*utkṣipta-caraṇāmbhoja*  
*he ’no-viparivartaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya-utsava*—of the auspicious ceremony; *kriyā*—in the rituals; *āsakta*—absorbed; *dhātrī*—of Your mother; *stanya-artha*—for the breast milk; *rodana*—whose crying; *utkṣipta*—kicked upward; *caraṇa-ambhoja*—whose lotus feet; *he*—O; *anaḥ*—of the cart; *viparivartaka*—O overturner.

**Перевод:**

**You cried for breast milk from Your mother, who was caught up in the rituals of the auspicious ceremony for You. You then kicked Your lotus feet upward, O Kṛṣṇa, overturner of the cart.**

### Text 78

*vrajānirṇeya-carita*  
*śakaṭāsura-bhañjana*  
*dvijodita-svasty-ayana*  
*mantra-pūta-jalāpluta*  
*(namaḥ 19)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vraja*—to the people of Vraja; *anirṇeya*—incomprehensible; *carita*—what had happened; *śakaṭa-asura*—of the cart demon; *bhañjana*—O destroyer; *dvija*—by the learned *brāhmaṇas; udita*—spoken; *svasti-ayana*—auspicious words; *mantra*—by *mantras; pūta*—purified; *jala*—with water; *āpluta*—who were washed; *namaḥ 19*—obeisance 19.

**Перевод:**

**O destroyer of the cart demon, the people of Vraja couldn’t understand what had happened. The learned brāhmaṇas chanted mantras for Your well-being, and You were bathed in water purified by mantras. (Obeisance 19)**

### Text 79

*yaśodotsaṅga-paryaṅkaṁ*  
*līlāviṣkṛta-gauravam*  
*mātṛ-vismaya-kartāraṁ*  
*tṛṇāvartāpavāhitam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *utsaṅga*—the lap; *paryaṅkam*—whose bed; *līlā*—as a sport; *āviṣkṛta*—revealed; *gauravam*—whose heaviness; *mātṛ*—of Your mother; *vismaya*—of the astonishment; *kartāram—*the creator; *tṛṇāvarta*—by the whirlwind demon; *apavāhitam*—who were carried away.

**Перевод:**

**You accepted Mother Yaśodā’s lap as Your bed, and as a sport You began to seem heavy, astonishing her. Then the whirlwind Tṛṇāvarta came and carried You away.**

### Text 80

*jananī-mārgita-gatiṁ*  
*tṛṇāvartāti-durvaham*  
*gala-grahaṇa-niśceṣṭa-*  
*tṛṇāvarta-nipātanam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jananī*—by Your mother; *mārgita*—searched for; *gatim—*whose movement; *tṛṇāvarta*—for Tṛṇāvarta; *ati-durvaham*—who were too heavy to carry; *gala*—by his neck; *grahaṇa*—by Your holding on; *niśceṣṭa*—immobile; *tṛṇāvarta*—Tṛṇāvarta; *nipātanam*—who made fall.

**Перевод:**

**While Your mother searched for You, You became too heavy for Tṛṇāvarta to carry. Grasping his neck, You made him unable to keep moving and made him fall to the ground.**

**Комментарий:**

The whirlwind demon raised such a cloud of dust as he flew away with Kṛṣṇa that the Vraja-vāsīs couldn’t see anything. In this fearful situation, Kṛṣṇa’s mother ran after Him to see where He was being carried.

### Text 81

*tvāṁ vrajānanda-dāyakam*

*tṛṇī-kṛta*—who made a weakling; *tṛṇāvartam*—Tṛṇāvarta; *rudat*—crying; *gopa-aṅganā*—by the cowherd ladies; *īkṣitam*—who were seen; *gopī*—by the *gopīs; dhātrī*—to Your mother; *arpitam*—who were presented; *vande*—I offer homage; *tvām*—to You; *vraja*—to Vraja; *ānanda*—of delight; *dāyakam*—O giver; *namaḥ 20*—obeisance 20.

**Перевод:**

**The crying cowherd ladies discovered You and returned You to Your mother. O delight of all Vraja, I offer My homage to You, who reduced the mighty Tṛṇāvarta to a weakling. (Obeisance 20)**

### Text 82

*yaśodā-stanya-mudita*  
*yaśodā-mukha-vīkṣaka*  
*yaśodā-nandanāhaṁ te*  
*yaśodā-lālitāva mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *stanya*—by the breast milk; *mudita*—who were pleased; *yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *mukha*—at the face; *vīkṣaka*—who looked; *yaśodā-nandana*—O darling son of Yaśodā; *aham*—I; *te*—Yours; *yaśodā*—by Yaśodā; *lālita*—who were caressed; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**Delighted with Yaśodā’s breast milk, You gazed at her face, and she caressed You. O Yaśodā’s darling son, I am Yours. Please protect me.**

### Text 83

*jananī-cumbyamānāsya-*  
*madhya-darśita-viśva me*  
*prasīda paramāścarya-*  
*darśin vismita-mātṛka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jananī*—by Your mother; *cumbyamāna*—kissed; *āsya*—Your mouth; *madhya*—inside; *darśita*—who showed; *viśva*—the universe; *me*—with me; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *parama-āścarya*—of most amazing wonders; *darśin*—O displayer; *vismita*—who astonished; *mātṛka*—Your mother.

**Перевод:**

**As Your mother kissed Your face, You showed her the entire universe within Your mouth. O Lord who displayed the most amazing wonders, astonishing Your mother, please be satisfied with me.**

### Text 84

*pūtanādi-vadhāloki-*  
*mātṛ-śaṅkā-śata-prada*  
*svabhāva-vividhāścarya-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pūtanā-ādi*—of Pūtanā and others; *vadha*—the killing; *āloki*—by showing; *mātṛ*—to Your mother; *śaṅkā*—of apprehensions; *śata*—of hundreds; *prada*—O giver; *svabhāva*—natural; *vividha*—various; *āścarya-mayatā*—by Your being full of wonders; *tat*—of that (anxiety); *nirāsaka*—O dispeller; *namaḥ 21*—obeisance 21.

**Перевод:**

**By showing the killing of such demons as Pūtanā, You gave Your mother hundreds of reasons for worry. And by Your natural power to show every sort of wonder, You dispelled that worry. (Obeisance 21)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe saptamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the seventh chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 8

### Text 85

*garga-vāk-cāturī-hṛṣṭa-*  
*nanda-nīta-rahaḥ-sthalam*  
*praśasta-nāma-karaṇaṁ*  
*garga-sūcita-vaibhavam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*garga-vāk*—of the words of Garga; *cāturī*—by the cleverness; *hṛṣṭa*—who were delighted; *nanda*—by Nanda; *nīta*—who were brought; *rahaḥ*—secluded; *sthalam*—to a place; *praśasta*—excellent; *nāma-karaṇam—*whose name-giving ceremony; *garga-sūcita*—hinted at by Garga; *vaibhavam*—whose opulence.

**Перевод:**

**Enlivened by the skillful words of Garga Muni, Nanda Mahārāja brought You to a secluded place. Garga there gave You excellent names and spoke some hints of Your opulence.**

### Text 86

*sādhu-rakṣā-karaṁ duṣṭa-*  
*mārakaṁ bhakta-vatsalam*  
*mahā-nārāyaṇaṁ vande*  
*nandānanda-vivardhanam*  
*(namaḥ 22)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sādhu*—of the pious; *rakṣā-karam*—the protector; *duṣṭa*—of the wicked; *mārakam*—the killer; *bhakta-vatsalam*—who are kind to Your devotees; *mahā-nārāyaṇam—*to the Supreme Lord Nārāyaṇa; *vande*—I offer homage; *nanda*—of Nanda; *ānanda*—the bliss; *vivardhanam*—who expand; *namaḥ 22*—obeisance 22.

**Перевод:**

**O protector of the pious, killer of the wicked, kind caretaker of Your devotees! O Supreme Lord Nārāyaṇa, expander of the bliss of Nanda Mahārāja, I offer my homage to You! (Obeisance 22)**

### Text 87

*jaya riṅgaṇa-līlāḍhya*  
*jānu-caṅkramaṇotsuka*  
*ghṛṣṭa-jānu-kara-dvandva*  
*maugdhya-līlā-manohara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *riṅgaṇa*—of crawling; *līlā*—by the bsport; *āḍhya*—who were glorified; *jānu*—on Your knees; *caṅkramaṇa*—in moving about; *utsuka*—who were eagerly engaged; *ghṛṣṭa*—rubbed; *jānu*—whose knees; *kara-dvandva*—and pair of hands; *maugdhya*—innocent; *līlā*—by Your pastimes; *manohara*—who were captivating to the mind.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, who fully enjoyed Your sport of crawling about. You eagerly moved on Your hands and knees, which rubbed against the ground. Your innocent pastimes captivate the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

“Victory to You.” In other words, “Please make me happy by revealing Your pastimes.”

### Text 88

*kiṅkiṇī-nāda-saṁhṛṣṭa*  
*vraja-kardama-vibhrama*  
*vyālambi-cūlikā-ratna-*  
*grīvā-vyāghra-nakhojjvala*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kiṅkiṇī*—of tiny bells; *nāda*—by the sound; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—who were very pleased; *vraja*—of Vraja; *kardama*—in the dirt; *vibhrama*—whose play; *vyālambi*—tall; *cūlikā*—on whose topknot; *ratna*—with a jewel; *grīvā*—around Your neck; *vyāghra-nakha*—and with tiger nails; *ujjvala*—who were splendid.

**Перевод:**

**You were thoroughly pleased by the sound of tiny waist and ankle bells as You played in the dirt of Vraja. With a jewel in Your topknot, tiger nails around Your neck, You appeared splendid.**

### Text 89

*paṅkānulepa-rucira*  
*māṁsaloru-kaṭī-taṭa*  
*sva-mukha-pratibimbārthin*  
*pratibimbānukāraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*paṅka*—with mud; *anulepa*—by being smeared; *rucira*—who were attractive; *māṁsala*—chubby; *ūru*—whose thighs; *kaṭī-taṭa*—and waist; *sva-mukha*—of whose own face; *pratibimba*—for the reflection; *arthin*—who hankered; *pratibimba*—the reflection; *anukāraka*—who imitated.

**Перевод:**

**You looked charmingly attractive, smeared with mud, and chubby in Your waist and thighs. You took a fancy to the reflection of Your own face and imitated the movements of Your reflection.**

### Text 90

*avyakta-valgu-vāg-vṛtte*  
*smita-lakṣya-radodgama*  
*dhātrī-kara-samālambin*  
*praskhalac-citra-caṅkrama*  
*(namaḥ 23)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*avyakta*—inarticulate; *valgu*—sweet; *vāk*—of whose speech; *vṛtte*—the manner; *smita*—in whose smile; *lakṣya*—noticed; *rada*—of teeth; *udgama*—the appearance; *dhātrī*—of Your mother; *kara*—to the hand; *samālambin*—who held on; *praskhalat*—stumbling; *citra*—wonderful; *caṅkrama*—whose walking; *namaḥ 23*—obeisance 23.

**Перевод:**

**Your broken speech was sweet, and Your smile showed new teeth coming in. As You held Your mother’s hand, You wonderfully tried to walk with toddling steps. (Obeisance 23)**

### Text 91

*jayāṅganā-gaṇa-prekṣya-*  
*bālya-līlānukāraka*  
*āviṣkṛtālpa-sāmarthya*  
*pāda-vikṣepa-sundara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *aṅganā-gaṇa*—by all the women; *prekṣya*—worth being witnessed; *bālya-līlā*—of the pastimes of infancy; *anukāraka*—O imitator; *āviṣkṛta*—displayed; *alpa*—small; *sāmarthya*—whose abilities; *pāda*—of Your feet; *vikṣepa*—with the kicking; *sundara*—who were beautiful.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! All the women enjoyed seeing You imitate the pastimes of an infant as You displayed Your small abilities and kicked Your feet about in a lovely way.**

**Комментарий:**

The victory hoped for is that Kṛṣṇa’s fame become known to everyone in the world.

### Text 92

*vatsa-puccha-samākṛṣṭa*  
*vatsa-puccha-vikarṣaṇa*  
*vismāritānya-vyāpāra-*  
*gopa-gopī-pramodana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vatsa*—of the calves; *puccha*—by the tails; *samākṛṣṭa*—who were dragged; *vatsa*—of the calves; *puccha*—of the tails; *vikarṣaṇa*—O puller; *vismārita*—made to forget; *anya*—other; *vyāpāra*—business; *gopa*—of the cowherd men; *gopī*—and cowherd women; *pramodana*—O delighter.

**Перевод:**

**O puller of the calves’ tails, the calves dragged You about by their tails. You delighted the cowherd men and women, making them forget all other business.**

### Text 93

*gṛha-kṛtya-samāsakta-*  
*mātṛ-vaiyagrya-kāraka*  
*brahmādi-kāmya-lālitya*  
*jagad-āścarya-śaiśava*  
*(namaḥ 24)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gṛha-kṛtya*—in household duties; *samāsakta*—engrossed; *mātṛ*—of Your mother; *vaiyagrya-kāraka*—O distracter; *brahma-ādi*—to Brahmā and others; *kāmya*—desirable; *lālitya*—whose sweetness; *jagat*—to the worlds; *āścarya*—amazing; *śaiśava*—whose childhood; *namaḥ 24*—obeisance 24.

**Перевод:**

**When Your mother was engrossed in household chores You distracted her. Your sweetness enchants demigods like Brahmā. Your childhood amazes all the worlds. (Obeisance 24)**

### Text 94

*prasīda bāla-gopāla*  
*gopī-gaṇa-mud-āvaha*  
*anurūpa-vayasyāpta*  
*cāru-kaumara-cāpala*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—be pleased; *bāla-gopāla*—O child Gopāla; *gopī-gaṇa*—to the cowherd ladies; *mut*—of pleasure; *āvaha*—O giver; *anurūpa*—suitable; *vayasya*—with contemporary friends; *āpta*—supplied; *cāru*—charming; *kaumara*—like a toddler; *cāpala*—who were naughty.

**Перевод:**

**Be pleased with me, O child Gopāla, joy of the cowherd women! You had suitable friends Your age, and just as a toddler should be, You were charmingly naughty.**

**Комментарий:**

“Be pleased with me,” Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī asks, “and let these pastimes be visible in my heart.”

### Text 95

*akāla-vatsa-nirmoktar*  
*vraja-vyākrośa-susmita*  
*navanīta-mahā-cora*  
*vānarāhāra-dāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*akāla*—untimely; *vatsa*—of the calves; *nirmoktaḥ*—O releaser; *vraja*—of the Vraja-vāsīs; *vyākrośa*—by the angry complaints; *su-smita*—who smiled sweetly; *nava-nīta*—of the fresh butter; *mahā-cora*—O great thief; *vānara*—to the monkeys; *āhāra*—of food; *dāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**You released the calves untimely and smiled sweetly at the Vraja-vāsīs’ angry complaints. O great butter thief! O generous feeder of the monkeys!**

### Text 96

*pīṭholūkhala-sopāna*  
*kṣīra-bhāṇḍa-vibhedaka*  
*śikya-bhāṇḍa-samākarṣin*  
*dhvāntāgāra-praveśa-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pīṭha*—from stools; *ulūkhala*—and mortars; *sopāna*—whose ladders; *kṣīra*—for milk; *bhāṇḍa*—of the pots; *vibhedaka*—O breaker; *śikya*—on rope swings; *bhāṇḍa*—the pots; *samākarṣin*—who reached; *dhvānta*—dark; *āgāra*—the storerooms; *praveśa-kṛt*—who entered.

**Перевод:**

**You found Your way into dark storerooms and broke the yogurt pots. You built ladders from chairs and grinding mortars to reach even the pots hung from the ceilings by ropes.**

### Text 97

*svāṅga-ratna-pradīpāḍhya*  
*gopī-dhārṣṭyāti-vādaka*  
*gopī-vrātokti-bhī-bhrāmyan-*  
*netra mātṛ-praharṣaṇa*  
*(namaḥ 25)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva*—Your own; *aṅga*—on the body; *ratna*—with jewels; *pradīpa*—as lamps; *āḍhya*—who were amply supplied; *gopī*—to the cowherd women; *dhārṣṭya*—audacities; *ati-vādaka*—who spoke boldly; *gopī*—of the cowherd women; *vrāta-ukti*—by threatening speech; *bhī*—in fear; *bhrāmyat*—moving about; *netra*—whose eyes; *mātṛ*—to Your mother; *praharṣaṇa*—O giver of pleasure; *namaḥ 25*—obeisance 25.

**Перевод:**

**The jewels on Your body gave ample light for such theft. You audaciously taunted the cowherd women, but when they threatened You, Your eyes would dart about in fear. Thus You gave great pleasure to Your mother. (Obeisance 25)**

**Комментарий:**

If a housewife in a neighboring house caught Kṛṣṇa stealing, He would defend Himself by speaking boldly: “What do you mean? I own this house, and you’re the thief!” Or if we accept the subject of the words *ati-vādaka* (“speaking boldly”) to be the *gopīs* rather than Kṛṣṇa, the idea is that the ladies would lodge vociferous complaints with Mother Yaśodā:

*evaṁ dhārṣṭyāny uśati kurute mehanādīni vāstau*  
*steyopāyair viracita-kṛtiḥ su-pratīko yathāste*

**“Your boy is sitting here now as a model of good behavior, but He comes to our clean homes and does the most outrageous things—peeing in our houses, making elaborate arrangements for stealing, and what all.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.8.31)**

### Text 98

*bhaktopālambhanānanda*  
*vāñchā-bhakṣita-mṛttika*  
*rāmādi-prokta-mṛd-vārta*  
*hitaiṣy-ambāti-bhartsita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhakta*—by the devotees; *upālambhana*—reproach; *ānanda*—whose great pleasure; *vāñchā*—by Your desire; *bhakṣita*—who ate; *mṛttika*—dirt; *rāma*—by Balarāma; *ādi*—and others; *prokta*—told; *mṛt—*about dirt; *vārta*—the news; *hita-aiṣī*—desiring the best welfare; *ambā*—by Your mother; *ati-bhartsita*—rebuked severely.

**Перевод:**

**Because You enjoy being rebuked by Your devotees, You purposely ate some dirt. And when Balarāma and other boys told Your mother what You had done, because she wanted the best for You she scolded You severely.**

### Text 99

*kṛtaka-trāsa-lolākṣa*  
*mitrāntar-gūḍha-vigraha*  
*balādi-vacanākṣeptar*  
*jananī-pratyayāvaha*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kṛtaka*—contrived; *trāsa*—with fear; *lola*—rolling; *akṣa*—whose eyes; *mitra-antaḥ*—among Your friends; *gūḍha*—hidden; *vigraha*—whose body; *bala-ādi*—of Balarāma and the others; *vacana*—of the statements; *ākṣeptaḥ*—O denier; *jananī*—of Your mother; *pratyaya*—belief; *āvaha*—who created.

**Перевод:**

**Pretending to be afraid, You then rolled Your eyes and hid among Your friends. You denied the statements of Balarāma and the others and tried to make Your mother believe Yours.**

### Text 100

*vyātta-svalpānanābjāntar-*  
*mātṛ-darśita-viśva he*  
*yaśodā-viditaiśvarya*  
*jaya svācchandya-mohana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vyātta*—wide-opened; *su-alpa*—very small; *ānana-abja*—whose lotus mouth; *antaḥ*—inside; *mātṛ*—to Your mother; *darśita*—who showed; *viśva*—the universe; *he*—O; *yaśodā*—by Yaśodā; *vidita*—understood; *aiśvarya*—whose majestic power; *jaya*—victory to You; *svācchandya*—by Your sweet will; *mohana*—O creator of bewilderment.

**Перевод:**

**You showed Your mother the entire universe within Your little wide-open lotus mouth. O You who bewilder at Your whim, Mother Yaśodā then understood Your divine majestic power. Victory to You!**

**Комментарий:**

Here Kṛṣṇa’s victory lies in His revealing His supreme opulence and the sweetness of His being scolded.

### Text 101

*savitrī-sneha-saṁśliṣṭa*  
*yaśodā-sneha-vardhana*  
*sva-bhakta-brahma-sandatta-*  
*dharā-droṇa-varārtha-kṛt*  
*(namaḥ 26)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*savitrī*—of Your mother; *sneha*—by the affection; *saṁśliṣṭa*—who were embraced; *yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *sneha*—of the affection; *vardhana*—O increaser; *sva-bhakta*—by Your devotee; *brahma*—Brahmā; *sandatta*—bestowed; *dharā*-*droṇa*—to Dharā and Droṇa; *vara*—of the benediction; *artha-kṛt*—O fulfiller; *namaḥ 26*—obeisance 26.

**Перевод:**

**Mother Yaśodā embraced You with her love, which You constantly increased. And thus You fulfilled the benediction given to Dharā and Droṇa by Your devotee Brahmā. (Obeisance 26)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe ’ṣṭamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the eighth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 9

### Text 102

*dadhi-nirmanthanārambhi-*  
*savitrī-stanya-lolupa*  
*jananī-gīta-carita*  
*dadhi-manthana-daṇḍa-dhṛk*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dadhi*—of yogurt; *nirmanthana*—the churning; *ārambhi*—who was beginning; *savitrī*—of Your mother; *stanya*—for breast milk; *lolupa*—who were greedy; *jananī*—by Your mother; *gīta*—sung; *carita*—whose activities; *dadhi-manthana*—meant for churning yogurt; *daṇḍa*—the rod; *dhṛk*—who held.

**Перевод:**

**Once when Your mother was just starting to churn yogurt and was singing about Your pastimes, You grabbed hold of the yogurt-churning rod, greedy for her breast milk.**

### Text 103

*mātṛ-stanyāmṛtātṛpta*  
*kṣīrottāra-gatāmbika*  
*mṛṣā-kopa-prakampauṣṭha*  
*dadhi-bhājana-bhañjana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mātṛ*—of Your mother; *stanya*—of the breast; *amṛta*—by the nectar; *atṛpta*—who were not yet satisfied; *kṣīra*—the milk; *uttāra*—for taking off; *gata*—gone; *ambika*—whose mother; *mṛṣā*—false; *kopa*—with anger; *prakampa*—trembling; *oṣṭha*—whose lips; *dadhi*—of yogurt; *bhājana*—of the pot; *bhañjana*—O breaker.

**Перевод:**

**Before You had drunk Your fill at Your mother’s breast, she went away to take milk off the stove. Then, Your lips pouting in false anger, You broke the yogurt pot.**

### Text 104

*śikya-haiyaṅgava-stena*  
*navanīta-mahāśana*  
*haiyaṅgavīna-rasika*  
*navanītāvakīrṇaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śikya*—on the rope swings; *haiyam-gava*—of the fresh-churned butter; *stena*—O thief; *nava-nīta*—fresh butter; *mahā-aśana*—whose favorite food; *haiyam-gavīna*—of fresh butter; *rasika*—who enjoyed the taste; *nava-nīta*—fresh butter; *avakīrṇaka*—who threw around.

**Перевод:**

**O You whose favorite food is newly churned butter, You stole the fresh butter hanging from the ceiling, enjoyed its taste, and scattered the butter all about.**

### Text 105

*navanīta-viliptāṅga*  
*kiṅkiṇī-kvaṇa-sūcita*  
*navanīta-mahā-dātar*  
*mṛṣāśro caurya-śaṅkita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nava-nīta*—with fresh butter; *vilipta*—smeared; *aṅga*—whose body; *kiṅkiṇī*—of Your small bells; *kvaṇa*—by the sound; *sūcita*—revealed; *nava-nīta*—of fresh butter; *mahā-dātaḥ*—O great giver of charity; *mṛṣā*—false; *aśro*—whose tears; *caurya*—because of Your theft; *śaṅkita*—who were afraid.

**Перевод:**

**Your body became smeared with butter. And when the sound of Your tiny bells gave You away, great giver of butter in charity, You shed false tears, scared of punishment for Your theft.**

### Text 106

*mātṛ-bhī-dhāvana-para*  
*goṣṭhāṅgana-vinodana*  
*jananī-śrama-vijñātar*  
*dāmodara namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mātṛ*—of Your mother; *bhī*—in fear; *dhāvana*—on fleeing; *para*—who were intent; *goṣṭha*—of the village; *aṅgana*—in the courtyard; *vinodana*—O entertainer; *jananī*—of Your mother; *śrama*—of the fatigue; *vijñātaḥ*—who recognized; *dāma*—a rope; *udara*—on whose abdomen; *namaḥ*—bowing down; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**Перевод:**

**By Your fear of Your mother and Your anxiety to flee, You put on a delightful show in the village courtyard. But then You understood how fatigued Your mother had become. O Dāmodara, I bow down to You!**

### Text 107

*dāmākalpa calāpāṅga*  
*gāḍholūkhala-bandhana*  
*yaśodā-vatsalānanta*  
*dāma-bandha-niyantrita*  
*(namaḥ 27)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dāma*—the rope; *ākalpa*—whose ornament; *cala*—unsteady; *apāṅga*—whose sidelong glances; *gāḍha*—tightly; *ulūkhala*—to the mortar; *bandhana*—whose being bound; *yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *vatsala*—O object of motherly affection; *ananta*—O unlimited one; *dāma*—with a rope; *bandha*—by being bound; *niyantrita*—who were brought under control; *namaḥ 27*—obeisance 27.

**Перевод:**

**The rope Your new ornament, Your eyes darting here and there, You were firmly tied to the mortar. O object of Yaśodā’s motherly love, O unlimited one, You thus became bound by a rope. (Obeisance 27)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe navamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the ninth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 10

### Text 108

*dṛṣṭārjuna-taru-dvandva*  
*kuvera-suta-śāpa-bhit*  
*aparādhi-samuddhāra-*  
*dayā-nārada-gīta-vit*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dṛṣṭa*—who saw; *arjuna-taru*—of *arjuna* trees; *dvandva*—the pair; *kuvera-suta*—of the sons of Kuvera; *śāpa*—of the curse; *bhit*—O breaker; *aparādhi*—of the offenders; *samuddhāra*—for the deliverance; *dayā*—the mercy; *nārada*—by Nārada; *gīta*—what was sung; *vit*—who knew.

**Перевод:**

**You saw the twin arjuna trees and broke the curse upon the sons of Kuvera, for You knew what Nārada had mercifully told them to deliver them from their offenses.**

### Text 109

*akiñcana-jana-prāpya*  
*śrī-madāndhādy-agocara*  
*ākṛṣṭolūkhalālāna*  
*jaya śrī-nārada-priya*

**Пословный перевод:**

*akiñcana*—with no material possessions; *jana*—by persons; *prāpya*—who are obtainable; *śrī*—of wealth; *mada*—by the intoxication; *andha*—to those who are blinded; *ādi*—and so on; *agocara*—who are inaccessible; *ākṛṣṭa*—who pulled; *ulūkhala*—of the mortar; *ālāna*—the rope; *jaya*—victory to You; *śrī-nārada*—to Nārada; *priya*—who are dear.

**Перевод:**

**Though gained by persons who have nothing, You stand beyond the reach of those blind and drunk with wealth or whatever they may have. O Lord, dear to Nārada, victory to You! You pulled the rope tied to the mortar.**

**Комментарий:**

May Kṛṣṇa be victorious; may He prove true the words of His devotees and thus expand His pastimes.

### Text 110

*kṛta-devarṣi-gītārtha-*  
*yamalārjuna-bhañjana*  
*dhanadātmaja-sat-stotra-*  
*stuta sarveśvareśvara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kṛta*—fulfilling; *deva-ṛṣi*—of Nārada, the sage of the demigods; *gīta*—of the words uttered; *artha*—the meaning; *yamala-arjuna*—of the twin *arjuna* trees; *bhañjana*—O breaker; *dhanada*—of Kuvera; *ātmaja*—of the sons; *sat*—excellent; *stotra*—by the prayers; *stuta*—who were praised; *sarva-īśvara*—of all lords; *īśvara*—O Lord.

**Перевод:**

**To fulfill the words of that sage of the demigods, You broke the twin arjuna trees. Then the sons of Kuvera praised You with excellent prayers, O Lord of all lords.**

**Комментарий:**

The last phrase of this verse, *sarveśvareśvara,* begins the summary of Nalakūvara and Maṇigrīva’s prayer.

### Text 111

*jīva-durjñeya-mahiman*  
*sadā bhaktaika-citta-bhāk*  
*asādhāraṇa-līlohya*  
*viśva-maṅgala-maṅgala*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jīva*—by the finite living entities; *durjñeya*—not easily understandable; *mahiman*—whose glories; *sadā*—always; *bhakta*—of Your devotees; *eka*—only; *citta*—to the mentality; *bhāk*—who respond; *asādhāraṇa*—exceptional; *līlā*—by Your pastimes; *ūhya*—who are guessed at; *viśva*—of the universe; *maṅgala*—of what is auspicious; *maṅgala*—O source of auspiciousness.

**Перевод:**

**The finite living beings cannot easily understand Your glories, for You always respond only to the mind of Your devotee, and no one else. Your exceptional pastimes hint at Your greatness. Of all things good in the universe, You are where the goodness has its source.**

### Text 112

*sva-dāsa-dāsatā-prīta*  
*bhakta-bhaktāti-vatsala*  
*guhyakārthita-sarvāṅga-*  
*hṛṣīka-bhajanāmṛta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva*—Your own; *dāsa*—of the servant; *dāsatā*—the state of being a servant; *prīta*—who love; *bhakta-bhakta*—of Your devotee’s devotee; *ati-vatsala*—who take very special care; *guhyaka*—by the Guhyakas; *arthita*—prayed for; *sarva*—all; *aṅga*—with their limbs; *hṛṣīka*—and senses; *bhajana*—of worshiping; *amṛta*—the nectar.

**Перевод:**

**You love to act as the servant of Your servant. You take special care for Your devotee’s devotee. The two Guhyakas—Nalakūvara and Maṇigrīva—prayed that with their every limb and every sense they might taste the nectar of worshiping You.**

### Text 113

*śiva-mitra-suta-stotra-*  
*santoṣāmṛta-varṣi-vāk*  
*sva-bhakta-vīkṣā-māhātmya-*  
*vādin prema-vara-prada*  
*(namaḥ 28)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śiva-mitra*—of Lord Śiva’s friend (Kuvera); *suta*—of the sons; *stotra*—by the prayers; *santoṣa*—who were satisfied; *amṛta*—nectar; *varṣi*—showing; *vāk*—whose words; *sva-bhakta*—Your devotee; *vīkṣā*—of seeing; *māhātmya*—the glory; *vādin*—who described; *prema*—of pure love; *vara*—of the blessing; *prada*—O giver; *namaḥ 28*—obeisance 28.

**Перевод:**

**Satisfied with the praises from those sons of Kuvera, Lord Śiva’s friend, You showered those two sons with the immortal nectar of Your words. You told them the glories of seeing Your devotee and then granted them the blessing of pure love. (Obeisance 28)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe daśamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the tenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 11

### Text 114

*gopa-vismāpana-krīḍa*  
*bāla-saṅkathitehita*  
*sambhrānta-nanda-sandṛṣṭa*  
*smita-bhinnauṣṭha-sampuṭa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopa*—the cowherds; *vismāpana*—which astonished; *krīḍa*—whose pastime; *bāla*—by the young boys; *saṅkathita*—reported; *īhita*—whose deed; *sambhrānta*—with surprise; *nanda*—by Nanda; *sandṛṣṭa*—who were beheld; *smita*—smiling; *bhinna*—parted; *oṣṭha*—of whose lips; *sampuṭa*—the round opening.

**Перевод:**

**When Your young friends reported what You had done, the cowherds were astonished by Your pastime. Nanda looked at You with great surprise, and Your lips parted in a smile.**

### Text 115

*patitārjuna-madhya-stha*  
*maholūkhala-karṣaka*  
*go-pāśāli-lasan-madhya*  
*nanda-mocita-bandhana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*patita*—fallen; *arjuna*—of the *arjuna* trees; *madhya-stha*—in the midst; *mahā-ulūkhala*—of the big mortar; *karṣaka*—O puller; *go*—for cows; *pāśa*—of binding ropes; *āli*—with a series; *lasat*—resplendent; *madhya*—whose waist; *nanda*—by Nanda; *mocita*—who were released; *bandhana*—from being tied.

**Перевод:**

**You moved about in the midst of the fallen arjuna trees, still pulling the big mortar. All those ropes for tying cows were still to be seen on Your waist, and Nanda released You from their knots.**

### Text 116

*sva-bhakta-vaśyatā-darśin*  
*ballavī-stobha-nartita*  
*bālakodgīti-nirata*  
*bāhu-kṣepa-manorama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-bhakta*—of Your devotees; *vaśyatā*—of Your coming under control; *darśin*—who showed; *ballavī*—of the cowherd women; *stobha*—by the praise; *nartita*—who were encouraged to dance; *bālaka*—for babies; *udgīti*—with songs; *nirata*—who were entranced; *bāhu-kṣepa*—with the waving of Your arms; *manorama*—who were enchanting.

**Перевод:**

**When the joyful sounds of the cowherd women got You dancing, You showed how You come under the control of Your devotees. Entranced by baby songs, You enchanted everyone by waving Your arms.**

### Text 117

*gopy-ājñā-dhṛta-pīṭhāde*  
*navanītārthanā-paṭo*  
*vraja-moha-kara-krīḍā-*  
*sudhā-sindho namo ’stu te*  
*(namaḥ 29)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī-ājñā*—on the orders of the *gopīs; dhṛta*—who carried; *pīṭha-āde*—the footstool and so on; *nava-nīta*—for butter; *arthanā*—in begging; *paṭo*—O expert; *vraja*—of Vraja; *moha-kara*—causing the enchantment; *krīḍā*—of whose play; *sudhā*—of nectar; *sindho*—O ocean; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You; *namaḥ 29*—obeisance 29.

**Перевод:**

**On the gopīs’ orders, You carried such things as Your father’s footstool. O expert in begging butter, O sweet nectarean ocean of childhood play that fascinated all of Vraja, I bow down to You. (Obeisance 29)**

### Text 118

*upanandāhita-prīte*  
*vṛndāvana-rasotsuka*  
*prasthāna-śakaṭārūḍha*  
*gopikā-gīta-ceṣṭita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*upananda*—by Upananda; *āhita*—showed concern for; *prīte*— whose happiness; *vṛndāvana*—of Vṛndāvana; *rasa*—to get the taste of the pastimes; *utsuka*—who were eager; *prasthāna*—which took You away; *śakaṭa*—on the cart; *ārūḍha*—who climbed; *gopikā*—by the *gopīs; gīta*—sung about; *ceṣṭita*—whose activities.

**Перевод:**

**Because Upananda showed concern for Your happiness and You were eager to enjoy the tastes of Your Vṛndāvana sports, You climbed onto the cart that took You away, while the gopīs sang of Your pastimes.**

**Комментарий:**

This begins the description of Kṛṣṇa’s moving from Mahāvana to Vṛndāvana.

### Text 119

*hṛdya-vṛndāvanāvāsa*  
*śrī-vṛndāvana-candra he*  
*vṛndāvana-priya śrīmad-*  
*vṛndāvana-vibhūṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hṛdya*—pleasing to the heart; *vṛndāvana*—in Vṛndāvana; *āvāsa*—whose residing; *śrī-vṛndāvana*—of Śrī Vṛndāvana; *candra he*—O moon; *vṛndāvana*—of Vṛndāvana; *priya*—O dear friend; *śrīmat-vṛndāvana*—of blessed Vṛndāvana; *vibhūṣaṇa*—O ornament.

**Перевод:**

**Residing in Vṛndāvana greatly pleased Your heart. O moon of Śrī Vṛndāvana, dear friend of Vṛndāvana, ornament of Vṛndāvana!**

**Комментарий:**

Like the moon in the sky, Kṛṣṇa created happiness for every creature in Vṛndāvana, including the humans, animals, and plants. He was dear to Vṛndāvana, and Vṛndāvana dear to Him, because there He found so many inspirations (*uddīpanas*) for His all-enchanting pastimes. Vṛndāvana was rich with splendid opulence, but the most precious jewel of all was Kṛṣṇa Himself. Thus when Kṛṣṇa left Vṛndāvana it lost all its beauty and charm:

*sthāvarāś cāntar uttaptāḥ*  
*sadyaḥ śuṣkā ivābhavan*  
*bahunoktena kiṁ sarve*  
*mṛtā iva carācarāḥ*

**“And the immobile creatures, in great pain within, seemed suddenly to dry up. What more is there to say? All beings, moving and nonmoving, were on the verge of death.” (*****Bṛhad-bhāgavatāmṛta*** **2.6.70)**

### Text 120

*vyāghrādi-hiṁsra-sahaja-*  
*vaira-hartaḥ prasīda me*  
*śrī-govardhana-kālindī-*  
*pulināloka-harṣita*  
*(namaḥ 30)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vyāghra-ādi*—of the tigers and others; *hiṁsra*—violent; *sahaja*—and natural; *vaira*—of the enmity; *hartaḥ*—O remover; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *me*—with me; *śrī-govardhana*—Śrī Govardhana; *kālindī*—of the Yamunā; *pulina*—and the banks; *āloka*—by seeing; *harṣita*—who were delighted; *namaḥ 30*—obeisance 30.

**Перевод:**

**O You who take away the natural enmity of the tigers and all such violent beasts, please be satisfied with me. You were filled with joy to see Śrī Govardhana and the banks of the Yamunā. (Obeisance 30)**

### Text 121

*vrajānandākara-krīḍa*  
*manojña-kala-bhāṣaṇa*  
*vatsa-pālana-sañcārin*  
*vrajādūra-dharā-cara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vraja*—to the Vraja-vāsīs; *ānanda*—bliss; *ākara*—who distributes; *krīḍa*—whose pastimes; *manojña*—charming; *kala-bhāṣaṇa*—whose indistinct speech; *vatsa-pālana*—with herding calves; *sañcārin*—who moved about; *vraja*—from Vraja; *adūra*—not far; *dharā*—the land; *cara*—who traveled.

**Перевод:**

**Your playful pastimes enlarged the bliss of the Vraja-vāsīs, and Your broken speech charmed everyone. You started going out here and there, roaming nearby in the countryside of Vraja to herd the calves.**

### Text 122

*rāmādi-bālakārāma*  
*nānā-krīḍā-paricchada*  
*vaṁśī-vādana-saṁsakta*  
*veṇu-citra-svanākara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*rāma*—of Rāma; *ādi*—and the other; *bālaka*—boys; *ārāma*—O delight; *nānā*—various; *krīḍā*—for sports; *paricchada*—whose paraphernalia; *vaṁśī*—of Your flute; *vādana*—in the playing; *saṁsakta*—who were engrossed; *veṇu*—from Your flute; *citra*—wonderful; *svana*—of sounds; *ākara*—O mine.

**Перевод:**

**With various playthings for Your various sports, You delighted Rāma and the other boys. Engrossed in playing Your flute, You brought forth a treasure-trove of wonderful sounds.**

### Text 123

*muralī-vadana śrīmat-*  
*tri-bhaṅgī-madhurākṛte*  
*kṣepaṇī-kṣepaṇa-prīta*  
*kanduka-krīḍanotsuka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*muralī*—Your flute; *vadana*—at whose mouth; *śrīmat*—beautiful; *tri-bhaṅgī*—threefold-bending; *madhura*—attractive; *ākṛte*—whose form; *kṣepaṇī*—of throwable objects; *kṣepaṇa*—with the throwing; *prīta*—who were pleased; *kanduka*—with balls; *krīḍana*—for playing; *utsuka*—who were eager.

**Перевод:**

**Your flute to Your lips, You assumed Your beautifully attractive threefold-bending form. You liked to toss around all sorts of things, and You were keen to play with bouncing balls.**

**Комментарий:**

Sometimes Kṛṣṇa and His friends would throw stones or fruits like *bilva, āmalaka,* and *āmla,* and sometimes they would play games with bouncing balls.

### Text 124

*vṛṣa-vatsānukaraṇa*  
*vṛṣa-dhvāna-viḍambana*  
*jayānyonya-raṇa-prīta*  
*sarva-jantu-rutānukṛt*  
*(namaḥ 31)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vṛṣa*—of the bulls; *vatsa*—and of the calves; *anukaraṇa*—O imitator; *vṛṣa*—of the bulls; *dhvāna*—of the sounds; *viḍambana*—O mimicker; *jaya*—victory to You; *anyonya*—with one other; *raṇa*—fighting; *prīta*—who enjoyed; *sarva*—all; *jantu*—of the animals; *ruta*—of the calls; *anukṛt*—O imitator; *namaḥ 31*—obeisance 31.

**Перевод:**

**O imitator of the bulls and calves, mimicker of the bull sounds, victory to You! You enjoyed fighting among Your friends and copying the calls of all sorts of creatures. (Obeisance 31)**

### Text 125

*jaya vatsāsura-dhvaṁsin*  
*kapittha-vrāta-pātana*  
*bāla-praśaṁsā-saṁhṛṣṭa*  
*puṣpa-varṣy-amarārcita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *vatsa-asura*—of the calf demon; *dhvaṁsin*—O destroyer; *kapittha*—of *kapittha* trees; *vrāta*—a multitude; *pātana*—O You who felled; *bāla*—of the boys; *praśaṁsā*—by the praise; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—who were enlivened; *puṣpa*—of flowers; *varṣi*—with a shower; *amara*—by the demigods; *arcita*—who were worshiped.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, destroyer of the calf demon! After You killed the demon, knocking down many kapittha trees, You were enlivened by the boys’ cheers and worshiped by the demigods with a rain of flowers.**

### Text 126

*go-vatsa-pālanaikāgrya*  
*bāla-vṛndādbhutāvaha*  
*vikālāgāra-gāmin māṁ*  
*pāhi go-dhūli-dhūsara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*go-vatsa*—of the calves; *pālana*—in taking care; *eka-agrya*—whose attention was concentrated; *bāla-vṛnda*—to all the boys; *adbhuta*—amazement; *āvaha*—who brought; *vikāla*—at the end of the day; *āgāra*—home; *gāmin*—who went; *mām—*me; *pāhi*—please protect; *go*—from the cows; *dhūli*—with the dust; *dhūsara*—turned gray.

**Перевод:**

**While intent on caring for the calves, You would amaze Your crowd of young friends and at the end of the day come back home, gray from the dust the calves raised.**

### Text 127

*sumano-’laṅkṛta-śiro*  
*guñjā-prālambanāvṛta*  
*puṣpa-kuṇḍala barha-srak*  
*patra-vādya-vinodaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sumanaḥ*—with flowers; *alaṅkṛta*—decorated; *śiraḥ*—whose head; *guñjā*—made of *guñjā* berries; *prālambana*—with hanging chains; *āvṛta*—covered; *puṣpa*—of flowers; *kuṇḍala*—whose earrings; *barha*—of peacock feathers; *srak*—whose garland; *patra*—made from leaves; *vādya*—with musical instruments; *vinodaka*—O enjoyer.

**Перевод:**

**You wore flowers decorating Your head, long chains of guñjā berries hanging from Your neck, and flower earrings, and a garland of peacock feathers. You amused Yourself by playing tunes on pipes made from leaves.**

### Text 128

*manojña-pallavottaṁsa*  
*vana-mālā-vibhūṣita*  
*vana-dhātu-vicitrāṅga*  
*barhi-barhāvataṁsaka*  
*(namaḥ 32)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*manojña*—attractive; *pallava*—made of new leaves; *uttaṁsa*—whose headdress; *vana-mālā*—with garlands of forest flowers; *vibhūṣita*—who were adorned; *vana*—of the forest; *dhātu*—with minerals; *vicitra*—decorated; *aṅga*—whose body; *barhi*—of a peacock; *barha*—a feather; *avataṁsaka*—on whose head; *namaḥ 32*—obeisance 32.

**Перевод:**

**You wore a lovely new-leaf crown. Flower garlands adorned You, patterns made from all sorts of forest minerals decorated Your body, and a peacock feather topped Your head. (Obeisance 32)**

**Комментарий:**

The word *vana-mālā* can be understood to mean simply “a garland made from flowers,” but it also has a more technical definition:

*ā-jānu-lambinī mālā*  
*sarvartu-kusumojjvalā*  
*madhye sthūla-kadambāḍhyā*  
*vana-māleti kīrtitā*

**“A garland that hangs down to one’s knees, shines brilliantly with flowers from all the seasons, and has a large** ***kadamba*** **flower in the middle is renowned by the name** ***vana-mālā.*****”**

### Text 129

*prātar-bhojana-saṁyukta*  
*vatsa-vrāta-puraḥ-sara*  
*giri-śṛṅga-mahā-kāya-*  
*bakāsura-gatekṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prātaḥ-bhojana*—with Your morning meal; *saṁyukta*—equipped; *vatsa*—of calves; *vrāta*—the herd; *puraḥ-sara*—keeping in front; *giri-śṛṅga*—like a mountain peak; *mahā*—huge; *kāya*—whose body; *baka-asura*—upon the crane demon; *gata*—which came; *īkṣaṇa*—whose sight.

**Перевод:**

**Taking Your morning meal with You, You went out with all the calves in the lead. Then You saw the crane demon, his huge body like a mountain peak.**

### Text 130

*tīkṣṇa-tuṇḍa-baka-grasta-*  
*mūrcchāviṣṭa-suhṛd-gaṇa*  
*mahā-baka-mukhākrīḍa*  
*baka-tālu-pradāhaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tīkṣṇa*—sharp; *tuṇḍa*—with his beak; *baka*—by the crane; *grasta*—who were swallowed; *mūrcchā-āviṣṭa*—fallen unconscious; *suhṛt-gaṇa*—whose friends; *mahā-baka*—of the huge crane; *mukha*—the mouth; *ākrīḍa*—whose playground; *baka*—of the crane; *tālu*—of the palate; *pradāhaka*—who caused a sensation of burning.

**Перевод:**

**When the crane swallowed You with its sharp beak and Your friends fell unconscious, You made a playground of that huge crane’s mouth and scorched its palate.**

### Text 131

*jaya duṣṭa-bakodgīrṇa*  
*baka-cañcu-vidāraṇa*  
*balādi-bālakāśliṣṭa*  
*puṣpa-varṣi-sureḍita*  
*(namaḥ 33)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *duṣṭa*—wicked; *baka*—by the crane; *udgīrṇa*—who were ejected; *baka*—of the crane; *cañcu*—the beak; *vidāraṇa*—who tore apart; *bala*—by Baladeva; *ādi*—and the other; *bālaka*—boys; *āśliṣṭa*—who were embraced; *puṣpa*—of flowers; *varṣi*—with a shower; *sura*—by the demigods; *īḍita*—who were worshiped; *namaḥ 33*—obeisance 33.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! After that wicked crane spit You out and You split his beak apart, Baladeva and the other boys embraced You, and the demigods worshiped You with a downpour of flowers. (Obeisance 33)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe ekā-daśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the eleventh chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 12

### Text 132

*prātar-vanyāśanākāṅkṣin*  
*śṛṅgākārita-vatsapa*  
*asaṅkhya-vatsa-sañcārin*  
*asaṅkhyārbhaka-saṅgata*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prātaḥ*—early in the morning; *vanya*—in the forest; *aśana*—to eating; *ākāṅkṣin*—who looked forward; *śṛṅga*—by Your horn; *ākārita*—called; *vatsapa*—the cowherd boys; *asaṅkhya*—countless; *vatsa*—with the calves; *sañcārin*—who went; *asaṅkhya*—countless; *arbhaka*—by boys; *saṅgata*—who were accompanied.

**Перевод:**

**Early in the morning, looking forward to a picnic in the woods, You called the calf-herding boys with Your buffalo horn. Then You went to herd Your innumerable calves, together with Your innumerable young friends.**

### Text 133

*śikya-cauryādi-vividha-*  
*bāla-krīḍāti-toṣita*  
*sva-pāda-sparśana-krīḍā-*  
*paṭu-bālaka-harṣita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śikya*—of lunch bags hanging from the sticks; *caurya*—of stealing; *ādi*—and so on; *vividha*—various; *bāla-krīḍā*—with children’s games; *ati-toṣita*—who were extremely satisfied; *sva*—Your; *pāda*—of the feet; *sparśana*—of touching; *krīḍā*—in the sport; *paṭu*—clever; *bālaka*—by the boys; *harṣita*—whose were pleased.

**Перевод:**

**You got grand satisfaction from children’s games of every kind, like stealing one another’s lunch bags, and the clever boys pleased You with the sport of running to touch Your feet.**

### Text 134

*vayasyāśakya-sahana-*  
*kṣaṇa-mātrāvilokana*  
*śuka-gīta-mahā-bhāgya-*  
*vraja-bālaka-veṣṭita*  
*(namaḥ 34)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vayasya*—of Your friends; *aśakya*—impossible; *sahana*—whose being tolerant; *kṣaṇa-mātra*—for even for a moment; *avilokana*—whose not being seen; *śuka-gīta*—sung by Śukadeva; *mahā-bhāgya*—their great fortune; *vraja-bālaka*—by the boys of Vraja; *veṣṭita*—who were surrounded; *namaḥ 34*—obeisance 34.

**Перевод:**

**Your friends couldn’t bear to lose sight of You even for a moment. Those boys of Vraja, whose great fortune Śukadeva praised in song, surrounded You on all sides. (Obeisance 34)**

### Text 135

*durbuddhi-supta-pīnāhī-*  
*tarathotprekṣakānuga*  
*duśceṣṭāghāsurābhijña*  
*mugdhārbhaka-rirakṣiṣo*

**Пословный перевод:**

*durbuddhi*—evil-minded; *supta*—sleeping; *pīna*—fat; *ahi*—the snake; *itarathā*—to be something else; *utprekṣaka*—presuming; *anuga*—whose friends; *duśceṣṭa*—malevolent; *agha-asura*—the demon Agha; *abhijña*—who understood; *mugdha*—innocent; *arbhaka*—the boys; *rirakṣiṣo*—who were anxious to protect.

**Перевод:**

**Your faithful friends saw the fat, evil-minded snake sleeping on the road and took him to be something else. But You understood that this was the malevolent demon Agha, and so You were anxious to protect the innocent boys.**

**Комментарий:**

Aghāsura’s mentality was completely evil. Because Kṛṣṇa had killed his brother and sister (Baka and Pūtanā), Agha couldn’t tolerate seeing Kṛṣṇa and His friends enjoying their pastimes. Thus the demon wanted to kill them all.

### Text 136

*kṛtya-cintā-mahā-līla*  
*sarpasyāntaḥ-praveśa-kṛt*  
*agha-dānava-saṁhartar*  
*vatsa-vatsapa-jīvana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kṛtya*—about what was to be done; *cintā*—of pondering; *mahā-līla*—whose wonderful pastime; *sarpasya*—of the serpent; *antaḥ*—inside; *praveśa-kṛt*—who entered; *agha-dānava*—of the demon Agha; *saṁhartaḥ*—O killer; *vatsa*—the calves; *vatsapa*—and the cowherd boys; *jīvana*—who brought back to life.

**Перевод:**

**You acted out Your astonishing pastime of wondering what to do, and then entered the serpent’s body. You killed the demon Agha and brought the calves and their herders back to life.**

### Text 137

*amarānanda-vistārin*  
*nindya-dānava-mukti-da*  
*vismāpitāgata-brahmann*  
*āścaryābdhe namo ’stu te*  
*(namaḥ 35)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*amara*—of the demigods; *ānanda*—of the bliss; *vistārin*—O increaser; *nindya*—contemptible; *dānava*—to a demon; *mukti-da*—O giver of liberation; *vismāpita*—who amazed; *āgata*—arrived; *brahman*—Brahmā; *āścarya*—of marvels; *abdhe*—O ocean; *namaḥ*—obeisance; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You; *namaḥ 35*—obeisance 35.

**Перевод:**

**Thus You swelled the bliss of the immortal demigods, gave liberation to a contemptible demon, and astounded Brahmā, who came to see for himself. O ocean of marvels, I bow down to You. (Obeisance 35)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśamo-skandhe dvā-daśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twelfth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 13

### Text 138

*mahāścarya-caritra he*  
*parīkṣic-chukadevāti-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*paugaṇḍa*—as Your childhood after five years; *ākhyāta*—described; *kaumāra*—Your childhood in the stage before that; *mahā-āścarya*—most amazing; *caritra*—whose story; *he*—O; *parīkṣit*-*śukadeva*—for Parīkṣit and Śukadeva; *ati-vimohana*—most entrancing; *kathā*—of whose narrations; *amṛta*—the nectar.

**Перевод:**

**What You did in Your kaumāra age was told as if it happened in paugaṇḍa. The story of Your life is most amazing. Both Parīkṣit and Śukadeva were thoroughly entranced by the sweet narration of Your topics.**

*stuta-ramya-saras-tīrā-*

*stuta*—glorified; *ramya*—attractive; *saraḥ*—of the river; *tīra*—on the shore; *ādṛta*—splendid; *śādvala*—on the grass; *jemana*—whose eating; *saraḥ*—of the river; *su-pulina*—on the nice bank; *āsīna*—who were seated; *bāla*—of boys; *maṇḍala*—by a circle; *maṇḍita*—who were decorated.

**Перевод:**

**Your eating on the splendid grass of the river shore is celebrated and all-attractive. You sat on that wonderful riverbank, surrounded by a circle of boys.**

*pīta-vastrodara-nyasta-*

*sakhi-śreṇī*—of all Your friends; *antarā*—in the midst; *sthātaḥ*—who were situated; *vraja-arbhaka*—with the boys of Vraja; *saha*—together; *aśana*—whose eating; *pīta*—yellow; *vastra*—between Your cloth; *udara*—and Your belly; *nyasta*—placed; *veṇo*—whose flute; *vanya*—consisting of forest items; *vibhūṣaṇa*—whose ornaments.

**Your flute tucked between Your yellow dhotī and Your waist, You sat adorned by the items of the forest and ate in the midst of all Your friends, the young boys of Vraja.**

*kavalāśana-sundara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vāma*—left; *kakṣa*—Your armpit; *antara*—within; *nyasta*—held; *śṛṅga*—whose horn; *vetra*—and stick; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *me*—with me; *vāma*—left; *pāṇi*—in Your hand; *stha*—located; *dadhi*—with yogurt; *anna*—of rice; *kavala*—this morsel; *aśana*—with eating; *sundara*—who were beautiful.

**Under Your left arm You held Your horn and stick, and in Your left hand a mixture of yogurt and rice. So beautiful while eating this morsel, please be satisfied with me.**

### Text 142

*aṅgulī-sandhi-vinyasta-*  
*phala bālāli-citta-hṛt*

*svargy-āścarya-karāśana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*aṅgulī*—of whose fingers; *sandhi*—between the joints; *vinyasta*—held; *phala*—fruits; *bāla-āli*—of all the boys present; *citta*—of the hearts; *hṛt*—O stealer; *sva*—Your; *narma*—by whose humorous comments; *hāsyamāna*—made to laugh; *arbha*—the boys; *svargi*—the residents of heaven; *āścarya-kara*—astonishing; *aśana*—whose eating; *namaḥ 36*—obeisance 36.

**Holding pieces of fruit in Your cupped fingers, You made funny comments that stole the hearts of all the boys and made them laugh. The residents of heaven were astonished to see how You took Your meal. (Obeisance 36)**

### Text 143

*adṛśya-tarṇakānveṣin*
