*adṛṣṭa-vatsapa-vrāta*  
*vatsa-vatsapa-mārga-ga*

**Пословный перевод:**

*adṛśya*—not seen; *tarṇaka*—for the calves; *anveṣin*—who searched; *ballava-arbhaka*—of the cowherd boys; *bhīti*—of the fear; *han*—O destroyer; *adṛṣṭa*—who could not see; *vatsapa-vrāta*—all the calf herders; *vatsa*—of the calves; *vatsapa*—and the tenders of the calves; *mārga-ga*—who went by the road.

**Перевод:**

**Not seeing the calves, You went to look for them to allay the fears of the cowherd boys. But then You lost sight of the boys as well and began looking for both the calves and the boys.**

*vidita*—who were aware of; *brahma-carita*—what Brahmā was doing; *vatsa*—of the calves; *vatsapa*—and calf herders; *rūpa-dhṛk*—who assumed the forms; *vatsa*—of the calves; *pāla*—and the herders; *hara*—to the thief; *brahma*—Brahmā; *tat-tat*—of each one; *mātṛ*—and to the mothers; *mut*—pleasure; *icchaka*—who wanted to give.

**Aware of what Brahmā was up to, You assumed the forms of the calves and the calf-herd boys because You wanted to please their mothers and please Brahmā, the thief of the calves and herders.**

*go*—of the cows; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; stanya-pa*—of those who drank the breasts; *ahanta*—whose self-identification; *go*—of the cows; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; prīti*—of the love; *vardhana*—O increaser; *balarāma*—by Balarāma; *ūhita*—guessed; *udanta*—Your news; *pitāmaha*—of the grandfather (Brahmā); *vimohana*—O bewilderer.

**You took on the role of those who drank the milk of the cows and gopīs, and thus You deepened the love the cows and gopīs had for You. Balarāma guessed what You were doing, but Brahmā, the grandfather of all creatures, was completely bewildered.**

*śuddha-sattva-ghana-svīya-*

*śuddha-sattva*—of uncontaminated goodness; *ghana*—full; *svīya*—identical to Yourself; *bahu-rūpa*—of many forms; *pradarśaka*—O displayer; *ati-āścarya*—most amazing; *īkṣaṇa*—by the sight; *aśakta*—who was powerless; *brahma*—by Brahmā; *vyutthāna*—of recognition; *kāraka*—O causer.

**Then You wonderfully displayed many forms the same as Your own, all of them full in pure goodness. By showing Brahmā this most dazzling sight, beyond his power to fathom, You shocked Him into seeing the truth.**

*sva-antaḥ*—in himself; *dṛṣṭi*—by seeing; *ati-dīna*—very wretched; *aja*—of Brahmā; *bahiḥ*—external; *dṛṣṭi*—to the vision; *sukha*—of happiness; *prada*—O giver; *gopa-arbha*—of the cowherd boys; *vaśa*—who submitted to the control; *rucira*—who were charming; *sa-pāṇi*—having in Your hand; *kavala*—a morsel; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**As Brahmā looked within himself and saw a very wretched person, You gave him an outward vision full of joy. Yielding Yourself to the cowherd boys, a morsel of food in Your hand, O most charming, please protect me.**

**You withdrew the cows and boys You had created and made Brahmā feel ashamed. Brahmā the creator, who now saw the truth, drenched Your feet with tears of ecstasy as You received his prayers. (Obeisance 38)**

## Chapter 14

### Text 150

*vidhi-vākyāmṛtābdhīndo*  
*gopa-bālaka-veśa he*  
*brahmāvatāra divyāṅgā-*  
*cintya-māhātmya-rūpa-bhṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vidhi*—of Vidhi (Brahmā); *vākya*—of the words; *amṛta*—of the nectar; *abdhi*—from the ocean; *indo*—O moon; *gopa-bālaka*—of a cowherd boy; *veśa*—whose dress; *he*—O; *brahma*—for the sake of Brahmā; *avatāra*—whose descent; *divya*—transcendental; *aṅga*—by Your body; *acintya*—inconceivable; *māhātmya*—with glories; *rūpa*—of a form; *bhṛt*—O assumer.

**Перевод:**

**O moon rising from the ocean of Brahmā’s nectarean words! For the sake of Brahmā You descend to earth in Your transcendental body, dressed as a cowherd boy, assuming an appearance that displays inconceivable glories.**

**Комментарий:**

Texts 150 through 164 summarize the prayers Brahmā offered to child Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 151

*mṛṣā-jñāna-śramāsparśi-*  
*bhakty-eka-sukha-nirjita*  
*śreyaḥ-sārāty-udāsīna-*  
*durbuddhi-kleśa-śeṣaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mṛṣā*—vain; *jñāna*—for knowledge; *śrama*—by the labor; *asparśi*—which cannot not be touched; *bhakti*—by devotion; *eka*—only; *sukha*—easily; *nirjita*—who are conquered; *śreyaḥ*—of the highest good; *sāra*—in the essence; *ati-udāsīna*—completely uninterested; *durbuddhi*—for foolish people; *kleśa*—in trouble; *śeṣaka*—the culmination.

**Перевод:**

**You are easily conquered only by pure devotion, which the vain cultivation of knowledge cannot touch. You make sure that fools with no interest in the essence of what is best for them finally achieve only the trouble they have taken.**

### Text 152

*pūrva-pūrva-vimuktaughā-*  
*śrita-bhakti-sumārga he*  
*nairguṇyādhika-durjñeyā-*  
*ścaryānanta-mahā-guṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pūrva-pūrva*—at various times in the past; *vimukta*—of liberated souls; *ogha*—by a multitude; *āśrita*—taken shelter of; *bhakti*—of devotional service; *su-mārga*—whose auspicious path; *he*—O; *nairguṇya*—to Your featureless aspect; *adhika*—superior; *durjñeya*—and more difficult to understand; *āścarya*—amazing; *ananta*—and unlimited; *mahā-guṇa*—whose exalted qualities.

**Перевод:**

**Many liberated saints of bygone days took shelter of the auspicious path of Your devotional service. Your unlimited qualities are more difficult to understand than Your formless feature, and more exalted and amazing.**

### Text 153

*kevalātma-kṛpāpāṅga-*  
*vīkṣāpekṣaka-mocaka*  
*niveditāparādhāti-*  
*bhīta-putrārthita-kṣama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kevala*—only; *ātma*—Your; *kṛpā*—of mercy; *apāṅga-vīkṣā*—the sidelong glance; *apekṣaka*—of those who expect; *mocaka*—O liberator; *nivedita*—confessed; *aparādha*—whose offense; *ati-bhīta*—very fearful; *putra*—by Your son; *arthita*—begged; *kṣama*—whose forgiveness.

**Перевод:**

**You liberate only those who patiently expect Your sidelong glance of mercy. Praying thus, Your son Brahmā confessed His offense and very fearfully begged Your forgiveness.**

### Text 154

*roma-kūpa-bhramat-koṭi-*  
*koṭi-brahmāṇḍa-maṇḍala*  
*prasū-vad-āgaḥ-sahana*  
*jagan-mātar jagat-pitaḥ*

**Пословный перевод:**

*roma-kūpa*—in Your hair holes; *bhramat*—wandering; *koṭi-koṭi*—millions upon millions; *brahma-aṇḍa-maṇḍala*—spherical universes; *prasū-vat*—like a mother; *āgaḥ*—of offenses; *sahana*—O tolerator; *jagat-mātaḥ*—O mother of the universe; *jagat-pitaḥ*—O father of the universe.

**Перевод:**

**In the pores of Your body wander the universes, millions and millions of spheres. You tolerate offenses just like a mother. O mother of the universe! O father of the universe!**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa bears all the universes within Himself, so just as a mother tolerates the kicks of her unborn child in the womb, Kṛṣṇa could tolerate Brahmā’s mistake.

### Text 155

*nābhy-abja-janita-brahman*  
*nārāyaṇa nirāvṛte*  
*sva-garbhāmbā-prapañcekṣā-*  
*tad-asatyatva-darśaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nābhi-abja*—from Your lotus navel; *janita*—born; *brahman*—Brahmā; *nārāyaṇa*—O Nārāyaṇa; *nirāvṛte*—O unbounded one; *sva-garbha*—in Your abdomen; *ambā*—to Your mother; *prapañca*—the material creation; *īkṣā*—by her seeing; *tat*—of it (the creation); *asatyatva*—the unreality; *darśaka*—who showed.

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā was born from Your lotus navel, O Nārāyaṇa, unbounded one! You showed Your mother the unreality of the material creation when she saw it all within Your abdomen.**

### Text 156

*satya-līlāvatāraughā-*  
*cintya-līlāti-vaibhava*  
*mithyā-satyatva-sampādin*  
*sadā-parama-satya he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*satya*—real; *līlā*—for pastimes; *avatāra*—of Your incarnations; *ogha*—of the multitude; *acintya*—incomprehensible; *līlā*—the pastimes; *ati-vaibhava*—whose extreme potency; *mithyā*—of falseness; *satyatva*—and reality; *sampādin*—O creator; *sadā*—constant; *parama-satya he*—O supreme reality.

**Перевод:**

**All of Your many pastime incarnations are real, the power and splendor of Your pastimes beyond comprehension. O creator of both the real and the unreal! Throughout all time, You are the supreme reality.**

### Text 157

*guru-prasāda-sandṛśya*  
*prapañca-janakāsmṛte*  
*bandha-mokṣādi-mithyātva-*  
*kṛd-vicāraṇa-mātraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*guru*—of the spiritual master; *prasāda*—by the mercy; *sandṛśya*—who can be realized; *prapañca*—of material creation; *janaka*—the generator; *asmṛte*—forgetfulness of whom; *bandha*—of bondage; *mokṣa-ādi*—and liberation and so on; *mithyātva*—the unrealness; *kṛt*—that which produces; *vicāraṇa*—becoming conscious of whom; *mātraka*—simply.

**Перевод:**

**You are beheld by the mercy of the spiritual master. And forgetfulness of You is the mother from whom Your material creation is born. Simply turning conscious of You makes both bondage and liberation unreal.**

### Text 158

*asat-tyāgi-sva-bhaktāntar-*  
*bahir-ātmādhika-sphuṭa*  
*sva-pāda-mahima-jñāpi-*  
*sva-pādābja-prasāda he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*asat*—the unreal; *tyāgi*—rejecting; *sva-bhakta*—to Your devotee; *antaḥ*—internally; *bahiḥ*—and externally; *ātma*—as the dearmost Self; *adhika*—abundantly; *sphuṭa*—manifested; *sva*—Your; *pāda*—of the feet; *mahima*—glories; *jñāpi*—which reveals; *sva*—Your; *pāda-abja*—of the lotus feet; *prasāda*—the mercy; *he*—O.

**Перевод:**

**To Your devotee who rejects the unreal, You manifest Yourself abundantly, both within and without. The mercy of Your lotus feet reveals the glories of Your lotus feet.**

### Text 159

*vidhātṛ-bhūri-bhāgyaika-*  
*prārthya-dāsānudāsyaka*  
*catur-mukha-muhur-gīta-*  
*bhakti-māhātmya pāhi mām*  
*(namaḥ 39)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vidhātṛ*—by the creator (Brahmā); *bhūri*—greatest; *bhāgya*—as the good fortune; *eka*—exclusively; *prārthya*—prayed for; *dāsa*—to Your servants; *anudāsyaka*—becoming a servant; *catuḥ-mukha*—by four-headed Brahmā; *muhuḥ*—repeatedly; *gīta*—sung; *bhakti*—of whose devotional service; *māhātmya*—the glories; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me; *namaḥ 39*—obeisance 39.

**Перевод:**

**The creator thus prayed for service to Your servants as the most wonderful thing he could possibly achieve. Please protect me, O Lord. Four-headed Brahmā sang the glories of Your devotional service again and again. (Obeisance 39)**

**Комментарий:**

Here Śrīla Sanātana submits his own prayer: “Please protect me. Please make my life successful by granting me the privilege of becoming Your servant.”

### Text 160

*dhanya-dhanya-vraja-vadhū-*  
*dhenu-tarpita-modita*  
*nitya-pūrṇa-mahā-bhāgya*  
*vrajauko-mitratāṁ gata*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dhanya-dhanya*—most fortunate; *vraja*—of Vraja; *vadhū*—of the women; *dhenu*—and the cows; *tarpita*—who were satisfied; *modita*—and pleased; *nitya*—always; *pūrṇa*—complete; *mahā-bhāgya*—whose great auspiciousness; *vraja-okaḥ*—with those residing in Vraja; *mitratām*—friendship; *gata*—who have established.

**Перевод:**

**You were pleased and satisfied by the offerings of the most fortunate women and cows of Vraja. Ever complete in all that is auspicious, You have become the friend of the Vraja-vāsīs.**

**Комментарий:**

The cows and women of Vraja offered Kṛṣṇa their own milk, an offering that satisfied His hunger and also His desire to reciprocate with His devotees.

### Text 161

*vraja-vāsi-prasaṅgāntar-*  
*devatā-bahu-saukhya-da*  
*vraja-jātāṅghri-reṇu-spṛk-*  
*tṛṇa-janmepsu-padmaja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vraja-vāsi*—the Vraja-vāsīs; *prasaṅga*—circumstantially; *antaḥ*—within; *devatā*—to the presiding demigods; *bahu*—much; *saukhya*—of joy; *da*—O giver; *vraja*—in Vraja; *jāta*—being born; *aṅghri*—from the feet; *reṇu*—by the dust; *spṛk*—touched; *tṛṇa*—as a blade of grass; *janma*—of a birth; *īpsu*—desirous; *padmaja*—lotus-born Brahmā.

**Перевод:**

**You give great joy to the presiding demigods who have the good fortune to live within the senses of the Vraja-vāsīs. Those people of Vraja—lotus-born Brahmā could only hope for birth as a blade of grass touched by the dust from their feet.**

### Text 162

*prema-bhaktārpitāśeṣa*  
*ghoṣa-vāsi-mahā-ṛṇin*  
*sad-veśa-mātra-saṁjñāta-*  
*pūtanātma-pradāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prema-bhakta*—to the devotees who possess pure love; *arpita*—who have given; *aśeṣa*—everything; *ghoṣa*—of the cowherd pastures; *vāsi*—to the inhabitants; *mahā-ṛṇin*—who are in great debt; *sat*—as a saintly devotee; *veśa*—by the disguise; *mātra*—only; *saṁjñāta*—recognized; *pūtanā*—to Pūtanā; *ātma*—of Yourself; *pradāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord who give everything to Your devotees possessed of pure love, You feel utterly indebted to the dwellers of these cow pastures. You gave Yourself even to Pūtanā, only because she disguised herself as one of Your saintly devotees.**

### Text 163

*virakta-prāpya-dānānu-*  
*raktāparyāpti-yantrita*  
*putratvādy-anukārāti-*  
*suhṛd-ānṛṇya-lajjita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*virakta*—by the renounced; *prāpya*—obtainable; *dāna*—by the gift; *anurakta*—for those who are attached; *aparyāpti*—by inadequacy; *yantrita*—who are bound; *putratva-ādi*—in the roles of a son and so on; *anukāra*—acknowledging; *ati*—extreme; *suhṛt*—to Your well-wishing friends; *ānṛṇya*—by Your inability to pay Your debt; *lajjita*—who are ashamed.

**Перевод:**

**Since You give Yourself to those who are merely detached from this world, You feel bound by insolvency to those attached to You. And You feel ashamed of the debt You owe the well-wishing friends who accept You in roles like that of son.**

### Text 164

*avidvan-māni-sac-citta-*  
*vāg-agocara-vaibhava*  
*aty-ānanda-muhur-nāma-*  
*kīrtana-brahma-vandita*  
*(namaḥ 40)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*avidvan*—not in knowledge; *māni*—but imagining themselves so; *sat*—of saints; *citta*—to the hearts; *vāk*—and words; *agocara*—incomprehensible; *vaibhava*—whose glories; *ati-ānanda*—in great ecstasy; *muhuḥ*—again and again; *nāma-kīrtana*—by chanting Your holy names; *brahma*—by Brahmā; *vandita*—who were worshiped; *namaḥ 40*—obeisance 40.

**Перевод:**

**The hearts and words of professional saints who imagine they know You can never comprehend Your glories. And to You Brahmā in this way offered homage, chanting Your names in great ecstasy again and again. (Obeisance 40)**

**Комментарий:**

By offering prayers, Lord Brahmā became free from the last traces of false pride. Thus in the end he engaged in pure *nāma-saṅkīrtana.*

### Text 165

*brahma-prasāda-sumukha*  
*bhakta-vatsala vāk-priya*  
*smitekṣā-harṣita-brahman*  
*brahmānujñā-pradāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brahma*—to Brahmā; *prasāda*—to showing favor; *su-mukha*—who were favorably disposed; *bhakta-vatsala*—O friend of Your devotees; *vāk*—by his words; *priya*—who were pleased; *smita*—smiling; *īkṣā*—by Your glance; *harṣita*—encouraged; *brahman*—Brahmā; *brahma*—to Brahmā; *anujñā*—of permission to leave; *pradāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**Well disposed to show Brahmā Your favor and pleased with his words, O kind friend of Your devotees, You encouraged him with Your smiling glance and gave him permission to go.**

### Text 166

*vatsa-vatsapa-moha-ghna*  
*yathā-pūrvārbha-tarṇaka*  
*pulinānīta-vatsaugha*  
*namas te ’dbhuta-karmaṇe*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vatsa*—of calves; *vatsapa*—and calf tenders; *moha*—of the trance; *ghna*—O breaker; *yathā-pūrva*—who made as before; *arbha*—the boys; *tarṇaka*—and calves; *pulina*—to the bank; *ānīta*—who brought; *vatsa-ogha*—the whole herd of calves; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *te*—to You; *adbhuta*—wonderful; *karmaṇe*—whose deeds.

**Перевод:**

**You broke the trance of the calves and calf-herds, restored them to their previous normal state, and brought Your whole herd of calves to the riverside. I bow down to You, performer of marvelous deeds.**

### Text 167

*mugdha-bālāli-vāg-jāta-*  
*hāsa vraja-gṛhotsava*  
*vicitra-veśa-carita*  
*gopī-hṛdaya-modana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mugdha*—innocent; *bāla-āli*—of all the boys; *vāk*—by the words; *jāta*—created; *hāsa*—whose smile; *vraja*—of Vraja; *gṛha*—for the homes; *utsava*—O festival; *vicitra*—various; *veśa*—whose dress; *carita*—and pastimes; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; hṛdaya*—of the hearts; *modana*—O delighter.

**Перевод:**

**The words of the innocent boys made You smile. You created a festival in the homes of Vraja. With every sort of dress and pastime, You delighted the hearts of the gopīs.**

**Комментарий:**

Waking up from a year’s sleep, Kṛṣṇa’s friends told Him, “Come eat lunch. You haven’t touched anything!” Hearing this made Kṛṣṇa smile. When Kṛṣṇa and the boys and calves returned to Nanda’s village in the late afternoon, all the villagers celebrated Kṛṣṇa’s return. Both the older generation of *gopīs* led by Śrī Yaśodā and the younger generation led by Śrī Rādhā felt their own special joy.

### Text 168

*ātmādhika-priyatama*  
*sarva-bhūta-suhṛd-vara*  
*parīkṣic-chuka-saṁvāda-*  
*niścita-prema-sāgara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ātma-adhika*—than their own selves; *priya-tama*—who were more dear; *sarva-bhūta*—of all living beings; *suhṛt-vara*—O best friend; *parīkṣit*—of Parīkṣit; *śuka*—and of Śukadeva; *saṁvāda*—by the conversation; *niścita*—established; *prema*—of pure love; *sāgara*—whose ocean.

**Перевод:**

**O best friend of all living beings, dearer to them than their own selves! Parīkṣit and Śukadeva proved by their talks that You are the ocean of pure love.**

### Text 169

*vicitra-līla māṁ pāhi*  
*nilāyana-vihāra-vit*  
*krīḍā-setu-vidhāna-jña*  
*plavaṅga-plavanoddhata*  
*(namaḥ 41)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vicitra*—wonderful; *līla*—whose pastimes; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *nilāyana*—of hide and seek; *vihāra*—in the game; *vit*—O expert; *krīḍā*—for play; *setu*—of bridges; *vidhāna*—of the construction; *jña*—O knower; *plavaṅga*—like the monkeys; *plavana*—with jumping; *uddhata*—who were bold; *namaḥ 41*—obeisance 41.

**Перевод:**

**Please protect me, O performer of wonderful pastimes, knower of hide and seek, expert builder of play bridges, bold jumper like the monkeys! (Obeisance 41)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe catur-daśamo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the fourteenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 15

### Text 170

*paugaṇḍāgama gopāla*  
*vṛndā-vipina-maṅgala*  
*vṛndāvanāntaḥ-sañcārin*  
*sammānita-nijāgraja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*paugaṇḍa*—to the *paugaṇḍa* age; *āgama*—whose coming; *gopāla*—O cowherd; *vṛndā-vipina*—of the Vṛndāvana forest; *maṅgala*—O good fortune; *vṛndāvana-antaḥ*—in the Vṛndāvana forest; *sañcārin*—O wanderer; *sammānita*—who honored; *nija*—Your; *agra-ja*—elder brother.

**Перевод:**

**As You reached Your sixth year, O cowherd, good fortune of the forest of Vṛndāvana, You wandered in the forest and honored Your elder brother.**

### Text 171

*vṛndāvana-guṇākhyāna-*  
*miṣa-datta-mahā-vara*

*nānā-rati-vicakṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vṛndāvana*—of Vṛndāvana; *guṇa*—the good qualities; *ākhyāna*—of describing; *miṣa*—on the pretext; *datta*—who gave; *mahā-vara*—a wonderful benediction; *ati*—very much; *vṛndāvana*—by Vṛndāvana; *prīta*—who were pleased; *nānā*—of various kinds; *rati*—in recreation; *vicakṣaṇa*—O expert.

**Перевод:**

**On the pretext of describing the good qualities of Vṛndāvana, You gave it Your superlative blessing. You were so pleased by the Vṛndāvana forest, in which You showed Your skill in many kinds of amusements.**

### Text 172

*bhṛṅgānukārin māṁ pāhi*  
*kūja-nirjita-kokila*  
*upātta-haṁsa-gamana*  
*śikhi-nṛtyānukāraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhṛṅga*—of the bees; *anukārin*—O imitator; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *kūja*—by Your cooing; *nirjita*—who put to shame; *kokila*—the cuckoos; *upātta*—who took up; *haṁsa*—of the swans; *gamana*—the movement; *śikhi*—of the peacocks; *nṛtya*—of the dancing; *anukāraka*—O emulator.

**Перевод:**

**You mimicked the bees, put the cooing of the cuckoos to shame, glided just like the swans, and danced like the peacocks. Please protect me!**

### Text 173

*pratidhvāna-pramudita*  
*śākhā-kūrdana-kovida*  
*nāmākārita-go-vṛnda*  
*rajju-yajñopavīta-bhṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pratidhvāna*—by the echoes; *pramudita*—who were delighted; *śākhā*—on the branches; *kūrdana*—in jumping; *kovida*—O expert; *nāma*—by their names; *ākārita*—who called near; *go-vṛnda*—all the cows; *rajju*—Your rope; *yajña-upavīta*—like a *brāhmaṇa’s* thread; *bhṛt*—who wore.

**Перевод:**

**You had fun hearing echoes and expertly jumped from one tree branch to the next. You called all Your cows by name and wore like a brāhmaṇa’s thread the rope You used to tie them.**

### Text 174

*niyuddha-līlā-saṁhṛṣṭa*  
*balabhadra-śramāpanut*  
*gopa-praśaṁsā-nipuṇa*  
*vṛkṣa-cchāyā-hṛta-śrama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*niyuddha-līlā*—by mock fighting; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—who were delighted; *balabhadra*—of Balabhadra; *śrama*—of the fatigue; *apanut*—O reliever; *gopa*—the cowherd boys; *praśaṁsā*—in praising; *nipuṇa*—O expert; *vṛkṣa*—of a tree; *chāyā*—in the shade; *hṛta*—being removed; *śrama*—whose fatigue.

**Перевод:**

**You thrilled in the sport of mock fighting, and sometimes You relieved Balarāma of His fatigue. You expertly praised the cowherd boys, and You relieved Your own fatigue in the shade of a tree.**

### Text 175

*puṣpa-pallava-talpāḍhya*  
*gopotsaṅgopabarhaṇa*  
*gopa-saṁvāhita-pada*  
*gopa-vyajana-vījita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*puṣpa*—made of flowers; *pallava*—and new leaves; *talpa*—with a bed; *āḍhya*—who were graced; *gopa*—of a cowherd boy; *utsaṅga*—the lap; *upabarhaṇa*—whose pillow; *gopa*—by a cowherd boy; *saṁvāhita*—who were massaged; *pada*—whose feet; *gopa*—by a cowherd boy; *vyajana*—with a fan; *vījita*—who were fanned.

**Перевод:**

**You were graced with a bed of flowers and new leaves, and a cowherd boy’s lap for a pillow, and one boy massaging Your feet, and another fanning You.**

### Text 176

*gopa-gāna-sukha-svapna*  
*jitaiśya grāmya-ceṣṭita*  
*ramā-lālita-pādābjā-*  
*ṅkita-vṛndāvana-sthala*  
*(namaḥ 42)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopa*—of the cowherd boys; *gāna*—with the singing; *sukha*—comfortable; *svapna*—whose sleep; *jita*—suppressed; *aiśya*—whose status as God; *grāmya*—of a villager; *ceṣṭita*—whose behavior; *ramā*—by Ramā, the goddess of fortune; *lālita*—tended; *pāda-abja*—by whose lotus feet; *aṅkita*—marked; *vṛndāvana*—of Vṛndāvana; *sthala*—the ground; *namaḥ 42*—obeisance 42.

**Перевод:**

**Sleeping comfortably while the cowherd boys sang for You, You held back Your status as God and acted like an ordinary villager. Your lotus feet, which are tended by the goddess of fortune, marked the ground of Vṛndāvana. (Obeisance 42)**

### Text 177

*jaya śrīdāma-subala-*  
*stokakṛṣṇaika-bāndhava*  
*vṛṣāla-vṛṣabhaujasvi-*  
*devaprastha-vayasya he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *śrīdāma-subala-stokakṛṣṇa*—of Śrīdāmā, Subala, and Stokakṛṣṇa; *eka-bāndhava*—O best friend; *vṛṣāla-vṛṣabha-ojasvi-devaprastha*—of Vṛṣāla, Vṛṣabha, Ojasvī, and Devaprastha; *vayasya he*—O companion.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, best friend of Śrīdāmā, Subala, and Stokakṛṣṇa! O companion of Vṛṣāla and Vṛṣabha, Ojasvī and Devaprastha!**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdāmā is mentioned first because he is the chief of Kṛṣṇa’s *priya-vayasyas,* His close friends of the same age. Subala is next because he is the first of Kṛṣṇa’s *priya-narma-vayasyas,* His most confidential friends. Stokakṛṣṇa is another prominent *priya-vayasya.* He is named Stokakṛṣṇa because he looks so much like Kṛṣṇa and he helps in various pastimes by his great strength.

### Text 178

*varūthapārjuna-sakha*  
*bhadrasenāṁśu-vallabha*  
*tālī-vana-kṛta-krīḍa*  
*bala-pātita-dhenuka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*varūthapa-arjuna*—of Varūthapa and Arjuna; *sakha*—O friend; *bhadrasena-aṁśu*—of Bhadrasena and Aṁśu; *vallabha*—O beloved; *tālī-vana*—in the forest of *tālī* trees; *kṛta*—done; *krīḍa*—whose playing; *bala*—by Baladeva; *pātita*—brought down; *dhenuka*—the ass demon Dhenuka.

**Перевод:**

**O friend of Varūthapa and Arjuna! Beloved of Bhadrasena and Aṁśu! You played in the Tālavana forest, and through Baladeva You brought down the demon Dhenuka.**

**Комментарий:**

Vṛṣāla, Vṛṣabha, Ojasvī, Devaprastha, and Varūthapa are younger boys, connected to Kṛṣṇa by friendship with a slight taste of servitude. They especially enjoy serving Kṛṣṇa in small ways. Arjuna, more intimate, is a *priya-narma-vayasya,* expert in understanding various *bhāvas* and engaged by Kṛṣṇa in confidential missions. Bhadrasena and Aṁśu are *priya-vayasyas* who serve Kṛṣṇa in all kinds of games and fighting sports.

### Text 179

*uttāla-tāla-rājī-bhid*  
*rāsabhāsura-nāśana*  
*gopa-vṛnda-stavānandin*  
*puṇya-śravaṇa-kīrtana*  
*(namaḥ 43)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*uttāla*—tall; *tāla*—of palm trees; *rājī*—of rows; *bhit*—O breaker; *rāsabha-asura*—of the jackass demon; *nāśana*—O killer; *gopa-vṛnda*—of all the cowherd boys; *stava*—by the praise; *ānandin*—who felt bliss; *puṇya*—auspicious; *śravaṇa*—hearing; *kīrtana*—chanting about whom; *namaḥ 43*—obeisance 43.

**Перевод:**

**You knocked down rows of tall palm trees, O killer of the jackass demons. And You felt bliss at being praised by all the cowherd boys. The hearing and chanting of Your glories brings every virtue. (Obeisance 43)**

### Text 180

*gopī-saubhāgya-sambhāvyaṁ*  
*go-dhūli-cchuritālakam*  
*alakābaddha-sumanaḥ-*  
*śikhaṇḍaṁ rucirekṣaṇam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; saubhāgya*—of the good fortune; *sambhāvyam*—to the source; *go-dhūli*—with the dust from the cows; *churita*—strewn; *alakam*—whose locks of hair; *alaka*—to the locks of whose hair; *ābaddha*—bound; *sumanaḥ*—flowers; *śikhaṇḍam*—and peacock feathers; *rucira*—charming; *īkṣaṇam*—whose eyes.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs looked to You as the source of their good fortune. The locks of Your hair were strewn with dust from the cows. Bound to those locks were flowers and peacock feathers. And Your eyes appeared charming.**

### Text 181

*sa-vrīḍa-hāsa-vinaya-*  
*kaṭākṣākṣepa-sundaram*  
*gopī-lobhana-veśaṁ tvāṁ*  
*vande gopī-rati-pradam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sa-vrīḍa*—bashful; *hāsa*—with smiling; *vinaya*—shy; *kaṭa-akṣa*—of sidelong glances; *ākṣepa*—and with the casting; *sundaram*—who were beautiful; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; lobhana*—arousing the interest; *veśam*—whose dress; *tvām*—to You; *vande*—I offer respects; *gopī*—for the *gopīs; rati*—conjugal attraction; *pradam*—who provided.

**Перевод:**

**So beautiful with Your bashful smiles and shy, sidelong glances, Your style of dress enticing the gopīs, You granted them conjugal attraction. I offer my respects to You.**

### Text 182

*jayāmbā-kārita-snāna*  
*puṇḍarīkāvataṁsaka*  
*muktā-hāra-lasat-kaṇṭha*  
*kara-kaṅkaṇa-sundara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *ambā*—by Your mother; *kārita*—performed; *snāna*—whose bath; *puṇḍarīka*—a white lotus; *avataṁsaka*—whose headdress; *muktā*—made of pearls; *hāra*—a necklace; *lasat*—shining; *kaṇṭha*—on the neck; *kara*—on the wrists; *kaṅkaṇa*—bracelets; *sundara*—beautiful.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, who were bathed by Your mother. You wore handsome bracelets on Your wrists, a white lotus in Your headdress, and a brilliant pearl necklace on Your neck.**

### Text 183

*mañju-śiñjita-mañjīra*  
*svarṇālaṅkāra-bhūṣaṇa*  
*divya-srag-gandha-vāso-bhṛj*  
*janany-upahṛtānna-bhuk*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mañju*—lovely; *śiñjita*—tinkling; *mañjīra*—whose ankle bells; *svarṇa*—made of gold; *alaṅkāra*—with jewelry; *bhūṣaṇa*—whose decoration; *divya*—heavenly; *srak*—of garlands; *gandha*—and fragrant perfumes; *vāsaḥ*—and clothes; *bhṛt*—O wearer; *jananī*—by Your mother; *upahṛta*—brought; *anna*—of the food; *bhuk*—O enjoyer.

**Перевод:**

**You wore lovely tinkling ankle bells, golden jewelry, heavenly garlands, fragrant perfumes, and fine garments. You enjoyed the food Your mother brought You.**

### Text 184

*vilāsa-lalita-smera-*  
*garva-līlāvalokana*  
*sukha-palyaṅka-saṁviṣṭa*  
*rādhā-saṁlāpa-nirvṛta*  
*(namaḥ 44)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vilāsa*—on account of sports; *lalita*—dancing; *smera*—with smiles; *garva*—of pride; *līlā*—and with the play; *avalokana*—whose glances; *sukha*—comfortably; *palyaṅka*—on the bed; *saṁviṣṭa*—who sat; *rādhā*—about Rādhā; *saṁlāpa*—by talking; *nirvṛta*—who were satisfied; *namaḥ 44*—obeisance 44.

**Перевод:**

**Your glances danced with gentle smiles as You looked forward to Your future amorous sports and showed a mood of youthful pride. Comfortably sitting on Your bed, You took pleasure in talking about Śrī Rādhā. (Obeisance 44)**

**Комментарий:**

As Kṛṣṇa sat on His bed, which had been nicely arranged by His friends and the maidservants of the house, He indulged in the comforts of being fanned, having His feet massaged, being entertained by music, engaging in pleasant conversation, and accepting betel nut to chew. And He was most pleased when a close friend brought into the discussion the topic of Śrī Rādhā.

### Text 185

*yamunā-taṭa-sañcārin*  
*kāliya-hrada-tīra-ga*  
*namas te ’ti-sudhā-dṛṣṭe*  
*viṣārta-vraja-jīvana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yamunā*—of the Yamunā; *taṭa*—on the banks; *sañcārin*—who wandered; *kāliya-hrada*—of Kāliya’s lake; *tīra-ga*—who came to the shore; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *te*—to You; *ati-sudhā*—exactly like nectar; *dṛṣṭe*—whose glance; *viṣa-ārta*—poisoned; *vraja*—of the Vraja-vāsīs; *jīvana*—O giver of life.

**Перевод:**

**You wandered along the banks of the Yamunā and came to the shore of Kāliya’s lake. I bow down to You, whose nectarean glance brought the poisoned Vraja-vāsīs back to life.**

### Text 186

*ati-vismita-gopāla-*  
*kulānumita-ceṣṭita*  
*jaya sva-jana-rakṣārtha-*  
*nigūḍhaiśvarya-darśaka*  
*(namaḥ 45)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ati-vismita*—totally amazed; *gopāla-kula*—by the assembly of cowherd boys; *anumita*—guessed at; *ceṣṭita*—whose deed; *jaya*—victory to You; *sva-jana*—Your devotees; *rakṣā-artha*—for protecting; *nigūḍha*—hidden; *aiśvarya*—Your powers; *darśaka*—who exhibited; *namaḥ 45*—obeisance 45.

**Перевод:**

**How did You do this? Your band of cowherd friends, utterly amazed, could only guess. Victory to You, who display Your hidden powers to protect Your dear devotees. (Obeisance 45)**

**Комментарий:**

“Please be victorious by revealing these pastimes in my heart.”

*iti daśama-skandhe pañca-daśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the fifteenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 16

### Text 187

*tuṅga-nīpa-samārūḍhaṁ*  
*sarpa-hrada-vihāriṇam*  
*kāliya-krodha-janakaṁ*  
*kruddhāhi-kula-veṣṭitam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tuṅga*—tall; *nīpa*—a *kadamba* tree; *samārūḍham*—who climbed; *sarpa*—of the serpent; *hrada*—in the lake; *vihāriṇam*—who sported; *kāliya*—of Kāliya; *krodha*—the anger; *janakam*—who created; *kruddha*—angered; *ahi*—of the serpent; *kula*—in the coils; *veṣṭitam*—who were entangled.

**Перевод:**

**You climbed a tall kadamba tree and sported in the serpent’s lake. You made Kāliya furious at You and became entangled in the coils of that angered serpent.**

### Text 188

*moha-magna-suhṛd-vargaṁ*  
*sāśru-gokula-vīkṣitam*  
*mahotpāta-samudvigna-*  
*vrajānviṣṭa-gatiṁ bhaje*

**Пословный перевод:**

*moha*—in bewilderment; *magna*—drowned; *suhṛt-vargam—*whose dear friends; *sa-aśru*—tearfully; *go-kula*—by the cows; *vīkṣitam*—who were watched; *mahā-utpāta*—by very fearful omens; *samudvigna*—disturbed; *vraja*—by the people of Vraja; *anviṣṭa*—sought out; *gatim*—whose movement; *bhaje*—I worship.

**Перевод:**

**As Your dear friends sank into bewilderment, the cows watched You with tearful eyes. Alarmed at fearful omens, everyone in Vraja set out to find where You had gone. I offer You my worship.**

### Text 189

*pada-cihnāpta-mārgaṁ tvāṁ*  
*mṛta-prāya-sva-bāndhavam*  
*rāma-rakṣita-nandādi-*  
*mumūrṣu-vraja-śocitam*  
*(namaḥ 46)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pada-cihna*—by the marks of Your feet; *āpta*—found; *mārgam*—whose path; *tvām—*You; *mṛta-prāya*—as if dead; *sva*—Your; *bāndhavam*—relatives; *rāma*—by Balarāma; *rakṣita*—preserved; *nanda*—Nanda; *ādi*—and the others; *mumūrṣu*—who were wishing to die; *vraja*—by the Vraja-vāsīs; *śocitam*—who were lamented; *namaḥ 46*—obeisance 46.

**Перевод:**

**By the marks of Your feet, Your family found the path You had taken and became like corpses. Lamenting for You and wishing to die, Nanda and the other Vraja-vāsīs were kept from giving up their lives only by the efforts of Balarāma. (Obeisance 46)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa’s footprints were easy to distinguish because they were marked with unique signs—flag, thunderbolt, barleycorn, lotus flower, and elephant goad.

### Text 190

*namas te svīya-duḥkha-ghna*  
*sarpa-krīḍā-viśārada*  
*kāliyāhi-phaṇā-raṅga-*  
*naṭa kāliya-mardana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*namaḥ*—obeisances; *te*—to You; *svīya*—of Your dear ones; *duḥkha-ghna*—O destroyer of the sorrow; *sarpa*—with the snake; *krīḍā*—in playing; *viśārada*—O expert; *kāliya-ahi*—of the serpent Kāliya; *phaṇā*—of the hoods; *raṅga*—on the stage; *naṭa*—O dancer; *kāliya*—of Kāliya; *mardana*—O subduer.

**Перевод:**

**I bow down to You, destroyer of sorrow for Your dear ones, skillful player with the snake, dancer on the stage of Kāliya’s hoods, subduer of Kāliya!**

### Text 191

*kāliya-phaṇa-māṇikya-*

*nija-gandharva-siddhādi-*  
*gīta-vādyādi-nartita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kāliya*—of Kāliya; *phaṇa*—on the hoods; *māṇikya*—with the glow of rubies; *rañjita*—made to shine; *śrī*—divine; *pada-ambuja*—whose lotus feet; *nija*—Your own; *gandharva-siddha-ādi*—of Gandharvas, Siddhas, and others; *gīta*—with the songs; *vādya*—instrumental music; *ādi*—and so on; *nartita*—whose dancing.

**Перевод:**

**Your divine lotus feet shone with the glow from the rubies on Kāliya’s hoods as You danced to the songs and music played by Your servants like the Siddhas and Gandharvas.**

*pādāmbuja-vimardāti-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pāda-ambuja*—of Your lotus feet; *vimarda*—by the pounding; *ati-namita*—brought very low; *ahi-indra*—of the king of serpents; *mastaka*—the heads; *rakta*—blood; *udgāri*—vomiting; *vibhinna*—broken; *aṅga*—whose limbs; *dīna*—wretched; *kāliya*—by Kāliya; *saṁsmṛta*—who were recognized; *namaḥ 47*—obeisance 47.

**Перевод:**

**The pounding of Your lotus feet pushed down the heads of the king of serpents. Wretched Kāliya, vomiting blood, his limbs broken, finally recognized who You were. (Obeisance 47)**

### Text 193

*nāga-patnī-stuti-prīta*  
*hitārthocita-daṇḍa-kṛt*  
*krodha-prasāda-gāmbhīrya*  
*mahā-puṇyaika-toṣya he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nāga-patnī*—of the serpent’s wives; *stuti*—by the prayers; *prīta*—who were pleased; *hita-artha*—for benefit; *ucita*—suitable; *daṇḍa-kṛt*—O giver of punishment; *krodha*—anger; *prasāda*—of whose mercy; *gāmbhīrya*—the depth; *mahā-puṇya*—by those who are very religious; *eka*—only; *toṣya*—who can be satisfied; *he*—O.

**Перевод:**

**You were pleased by the prayers of the serpent’s wives: O You who punish suitably for one’s benefit, Your anger the depth of Your mercy, You are satisfied only by the most righteous.**

*nirupādhi-kṛpā-kārin*  
*sarpa-strī-prārthya-dāyaka*  
*sarvārtha-tyāgi-bhaktārthya-*  
*svāṅghri-rekhācitoraga*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nirupādhi*—causeless; *kṛpā*—of mercy; *kārin*—O bestower; *sarpa-strī*—by the serpent’s wives; *prārthya*—of what was prayed for; *dāyaka*—O bestower; *sarva-artha*—all goals of life; *tyāgi*—who have given up; *bhakta*—by Your devotees; *arthya*—aspired for; *sva*—Your; *aṅghri*—of the feet; *rekhā*—with the signs; *ācita*—marked; *uraga*—the snake.

**Перевод:**

**Granting the serpent’s wives what they prayed for, O bestower of causeless mercy, You marked that serpent with the signs of Your feet, the same signs aspired for by devotees who have given up all other goals of life.**

*acintyaiśvarya-mahiman*

**Пословный перевод:**

*acintya*—inconceivable; *aiśvarya*—whose power; *mahiman*—and glories; *nānā*—various; *jīva*—of the living beings; *svabhāva*—of the individual natures; *sṛk*—O creator; *nānā*—various; *krīḍanaka*—with toys; *krīḍin*—who play; *sva*—Your; *prajā*—of the creatures; *āgaḥ*—the sins; *kṣamā*—of forgiving; *ucita*—who are capable.

**Перевод:**

**Inconceivable in power and glory, You create the inherent natures of the diverse living beings. You treat every sort of thing like a toy in Your play. You are able to forgive the offenses of Your creatures.**

*’yogya-mohita-nigraha*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nāga-strī*—of the serpent women; *pati*—of the husband; *bhikṣā*—in charity; *da*—O giver; *jaya*—victory to You; *kāliya*—by Kāliya; *bhāṣita*—who were addressed; *agrāhya*—contaminated; *sṛṣṭa*—who created; *duṣṭa*—evil; *āgaḥ*—sins; *ayogya*—improper; *mohita*—of the bewildered; *nigraha*—whose punishment.

**Перевод:**

**To the serpent’s wives you gave back their husband in charity. Victory to You, to whom Kāliya said: Since it is You who made this polluted creation and its evil sins, You should not be too harsh on the bewildered.**

*svāṅka-mudrāṅkitāhīndra-*

*sva*—Your own; *aṅka-mudrā*—with the signs; *aṅkita*—marked; *ahi-indra*—of the king of serpents; *mūrdhan*—the heads; *kāliya*—of Kāliya; *śāsana*—O chastiser; *pūrva-sthāna*—to his original home; *āpita*—who sent; *ahi-indra*—the king of serpents; *suparṇa-ja*—due to Garuḍa; *bhaya*—of his fear; *apahṛt*—O remover.

**Перевод:**

**O chastiser of Kāliya, You stamped the heads of that serpent king with the signs of Your feet, sent him back to his former home, and swept away his fear of Garuḍa.**

**Пословный перевод:**

*nāga*—of the serpent; *upāyana*—by the offerings; *hṛṣṭa*—pleased; *ātman—*whose mind; *kāliya*—to Kāliya; *ati-prasādita*—who showed great favor; *yamunā*—in the Yamunā; *hrada*—of the lake; *saṁśodhin*—O purifier; *hrada*—from the lake; *utsārita*—who exiled; *kāliya*—Kāliya; *namaḥ 48*—obeisance 48.

**Перевод:**

**Your mind pleased by Kāliya’s offerings, You showed him great favor and then exiled him and purified the Yamunā’s lake. (Obeisance 48)**

*iti daśama-skandhe ṣoḍaśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

## Chapter 17

### Text 199

*sva-baly-aśana-kāliya-*  
*darpa-mardana-vāhana*  
*saubhary-ukti-svakāgamya-*  
*sarpāvāsa-hradoddhara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva*—his (Garuḍa’s) own; *bali*—offerings; *aśana*—by eating; *kāliya*—of Kāliya; *darpa*—the pride; *mardana*—subduing; *vāhana*—whose carrier, Garuḍa; *saubhari*—of Saubhari; *ukti*—by the declaration; *svaka*—for him; *agamya*—unapproachable; *sarpa*—of the serpent; *āvāsa*—the abode; *hrada*—the lake; *uddhara*—who saved.

**Перевод:**

**Kāliya had eaten the offerings meant for Your carrier Garuḍa, and Garuḍa had crushed Kāliya’s pride. Kāliya had then made his abode in the lake declared off limits to Garuḍa by Saubhari, and You saved that lake.**

*mahā-maṇi-gaṇākīrṇa*  
*vraja-jīvana-darśana*

*divya*—wonderful; *srak*—with garlands; *gandha*—scents; *vastra*—and clothes; *āḍhya*—who were adorned; *divya*—wonderful; *ābharaṇa*—with ornaments; *bhūṣita*—who were decorated; *mahā-maṇi*—of precious gems; *gaṇa*—with a multitude; *ākīrṇa*—who were covered; *vraja*—to the Vraja-vāsīs; *jīvana*—O giver of life; *darśana*—by showing Yourself.

**Перевод:**

**Adorned with wonderful garlands and scents, decorated with wonderful ornaments, and covered with many precious gems, You showed Yourself to the Vraja-vāsīs to give them life.**

*sva-janārti-vināśana*

*sa-hāsa*—with laughter; *śrī-bala*—by Śrī Baladeva; *āśliṣṭa*—who were hugged; *gopa*—of the cowherd men; *āliṅgana*—by the embraces; *nirvṛta*—who were delighted; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *pīta*—who drank; *dāva-agne*—the forest fire; *sva-jana*—of Your devotees; *ārti*—of the anguish; *vināśana*—O destroyer; *namaḥ 49*—obeisance 49.

**Перевод:**

**Laughing, Śrī Baladeva hugged You, and You enjoyed the embraces of the cowherd men. Please be satisfied with me, O swallower of the forest fire, O destroyer of anguish for Your devotees! (Obeisance 49)**

*iti daśama-skandhe sapta-daśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the seventeenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 18

### Text 202

*kāka-pakṣa-dhara śrīmad-*  
*vasantita-nidāgha he*  
*nayanācchādana-krīḍa*  
*rāja-līlānukāraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kāka-pakṣa*—of crow’s wings; *dhara*—O wearer; *śrīmat*—glorious; *vasantita*—who turned into springtime; *nidāgha*—the summer; *he*—O; *nayana*—of the eyes; *ācchādana*—with covering; *krīḍa*—whose games; *rāja*—of kings; *līlā*—the pastimes; *anukāraka*—who imitated.

**Перевод:**

**O wearer of “crow’s wings,” You turned the summer into glorious springtime. You played games like blindman’s buff, and You acted out the pastimes of kings.**

**Комментарий:**

“Crow’s wings” are side locks worn by boys whose heads have been shaved. Kṛṣṇa sported such locks at this age, and He played a game with His friends in which one of them, blindfolded, would have to guess the identity of another. He would also play like a king, sitting with a mountain ledge as His throne, being worshiped with a flower umbrella and royal fans, and receiving subjects to bestow honors upon them.

### Text 203

*mṛgādi-ceṣṭā-krīḍā-kṛd*  
*dolā-naukā-vinodaka*  
*nānā-laukika-līlā-bhṛn*  
*nānā-sthāna-vihāra-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mṛga*—of animals; *ādi*—and so on; *ceṣṭā*—the activity; *krīḍā*—as Your play; *kṛt*—who performed; *dolā*—with swings; *naukā*—and boats; *vinodaka*—O enjoyer; *nānā*—various; *laukika*—common; *līlā*—in games; *bhṛt*—who were engaged; *nānā*—various; *sthāna*—in places; *vihāra*—pastimes; *kṛt*—who performed.

**Перевод:**

**You amused Yourself by imitating so many animals and birds. You enjoyed with swings and boats, played all sorts of common games, and sported in all sorts of places.**

*gopa-rūpi-pralamba-jña*  
*dvandva-krīḍā-pravartaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*krīḍā*—in Your playing; *samprāpta*—who arrived; *bhāṇḍīra*—at the Bhāṇḍīra forest; *jaya*—victory to You; *bhāṇḍīra*—of the Bhāṇḍīra tree; *maṇḍana*—O ornament; *gopa-rūpi*—in the guise of a cowherd; *pralamba*—Pralamba; *jña*—who recognized; *dvandva*—in pairs; *krīḍā*—of the game; *pravartaka*—O starter.

**Перевод:**

**In the course of playing You arrived at the Bhāṇḍīra tree. Victory to You, ornament of Bhāṇḍīra! Recognizing Pralamba in his guise as a cowherd boy, You started a game of competition in pairs.**

### Text 205

*vāhya-vāhaka-kelīman*

*bala-pātita-durdharṣa-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vāhya*—of carriers; *vāhaka*—and of carried; *kelīman*—O enjoyer of the game; *jaya*—victory to You; *śrīdāma*—of Śrīdāmā; *vāhaka*—O carrier; *bala*—by Balarāma; *pātita*—brought down; *durdharṣa*—invincible; *pralamba*—Pralamba; *bala*—of Balarāma; *vatsala*—O darling; *namaḥ 50*—obeisance 50.

**Перевод:**

**O enjoyer of that game of carriers and carried, You carried Śrīdāmā. Victory to You! You had Balarāma strike down the invincible Pralamba. O darling of Balarāma! (Obeisance 50)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe ’ṣṭā-daśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the eighteenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 19

### Text 206

*jaya muñjāṭavī-bhraṣṭa-*

*dāvāgni-bhīta-gopāla-*  
*dṛṅ-nimīlana-deśaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *muñja-aṭavī*—in the Muñja forest; *bhraṣṭa*—who had lost; *mārga*—the way; *paśu*—of the animals; *ārti*—of the distress; *nāśaka*—O eradicator; *dāva-agni*—of the forest fire; *bhīta*—afraid; *gopāla*—the cowherd boys; *dṛk-nimīlana*—to close their eyes; *deśaka*—who advised.

**Перевод:**

**You put an end to the distress of the animals who had lost their way in the Muñja forest. And You advised the cowherd boys, who were afraid of the forest fire, to close their eyes.**

*muñjāṭavyāgni-śamana*  
*pītolbaṇa-davānala*  
*bhāṇḍīrāpita-go-gopa*  
*yogādhīśa namo ’stu te*  
*(namaḥ 51)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*muñja-āṭavya*—in the Muñja forest; *agni*—of the fire; *śamana*—O subduer; *pīta*—who drank; *ulbaṇa*—fearsome; *dava-anala*—the forest fire; *bhāṇḍīra*—the Bhāṇḍīra tree; *āpita*—who made obtain; *go*—the cows; *gopa*—and the cowherds; *yoga-adhīśa*—O master of *yoga; namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You; *namaḥ 51*—obeisance 51.

**Перевод:**

**O extinguisher of the Muñjāṭavī fire, swallower of the fearsome forest blaze, You returned the cows and cowherds to the Bhāṇḍīra tree. O master of mystic yoga, I bow down to You! (Obeisance 51)**

*iti daśama-skandhe ekona-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the nineteenth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 20

### Text 208

*prāvṛṭ-śrī-bhūṣitāraṇya*  
*vṛṣṭi-kāla-vinoda-kṛt*  
*guhā-vanaspati-kroḍa-*  
*sevin mūla-phalāśana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prāvṛṭ*—during the rains; *śrī*—with splendor; *bhūṣita*—who caused to be adorned; *araṇya*—the forest; *vṛṣṭi-kāla*—in the rainy season; *vinoda-kṛt*—O enjoyer; *guhā*—of caves; *vanaspati*—and of trees; *kroḍa*—to the hollows; *sevin*—who resorted; *mūla*—of roots; *phala*—and fruits; *aśana*—O eater.

**Перевод:**

**During the rains, You made the forest splendorous and enjoyed Your monsoon pastimes, resorting to the hollows of caves and trees and eating roots and fruits.**

*pāṣāṇa*—on rocks; *nyasta*—placed; *dadhi*—of yogurt; *anna*—and rice; *bhuk*—O eater; *varṣā*—during the rainy season; *harṣita*—who made happy; *vraja*—Vraja; *śādvala*—green grasses; *aśana*—those who were eating; *varṣā*—of the rains; *śrī*—and the beauty; *sammānaka*—who praised; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**Перевод:**

**You ate meals of rice and yogurt served on the surface of rocks. During the season of rains You made all of Vraja happy. As the cows pastured on green grass, You praised them and the beauty of the rains. I bow down to You.**

### Text 210

*he śaran-nirmala-vyoma-*  
*cāru-kānte prasīda me*  
*śarac-candra-lasad-vaktra*  
*kṛta-gopī-mahā-smara*  
*(namaḥ 52)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*he*—O; *śarat*—of the autumn; *nirmala*—clear; *vyoma*—like the sky; *cāru*—attractive; *kānte*—whose complexion; *prasīda*—be satisfied; *me*—with me; *śarat*—autumnal; *candra*—like the moon; *lasat*—shining; *vaktra*—whose face; *kṛta*—who created; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; mahā*—great; *smara*—lust; *namaḥ 52*—obeisance 52.

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, please be satisfied with me. Your luster attractive like the clear autumn sky, Your face shining like the autumn moon, You brought the gopīs completely under the sway of Cupid. (Obeisance 52)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twentieth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 21

### Text 211

*śarad-vihāra-madhura*  
*śarat-puṣpa-vibhūṣaṇa*  
*karṇikārāvataṁsaṁ tvāṁ*  
*naṭa-veśa-dharaṁ bhaje*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śarat*—in the autumn season; *vihāra*—by playing; *madhura*—who were charming; *śarat*—autumnal; *puṣpa*—with flowers; *vibhūṣaṇa*—who were decorated; *karṇikāra*—*karṇikāra* blossoms; *avataṁsam*—as the decoration on whose ears; *tvām*—You; *naṭa*—of a stage performer; *veśa*—the dress; *dharam*—who wore; *bhaje*—I worship.

**Перевод:**

**You were so charming while playing in the autumn and decorated with autumn flowers. I worship You, who wore karṇikāra blossoms on Your ears and who dressed like a dancing actor.**

### Text 212

*vinyasta-vadanāmbhoja-*  
*locana-prānta-nartaka*  
*bimbādharārpitodāra-*  
*veṇo jaya su-gāyana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vinyasta*—placed; *vadana-ambhoja*—on Your lotus face; *locana*—of the eyes; *prānta*—the corners; *nartaka*—who made dance; *bimba*—like a *bimba* fruit; *adhara*—whose lips; *arpita*—held; *udāra*—splendid; *veṇo*—whose flute; *jaya*—victory to You; *su-gāyana*—O excellent singer.

**Перевод:**

**You made Your eyes—beautifully spread across Your lotus face—dance in their corners, and You held Your splendid flute to Your bimba-red lips. Victory to You, O excellent singer!**

**Комментарий:**

“Victory to You” here means “May You succeed in attracting the *gopīs* with the song of Your flute.”

### Text 213

*namo vaktrāvalokāya*  
*tri-bhaṅga-lalitāya te*  
*veṇu-mohita-viśvāya*  
*gopikodgīta-kīrtaye*  
*(namaḥ 53)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*namaḥ*—obeisances; *vaktra*—crooked; *avalokāya*—whose glances; *tri-bhaṅga*—by being bent in three places; *lalitāya*—who are charming; *te*—to You; *veṇu*—with Your flute; *mohita*—who bewildered; *viśvāya*—the universe; *gopikā*—by the *gopīs; udgīta*—sung loudly; *kīrtaye*—whose glories; *namaḥ 53*—obeisance 53.

**Перевод:**

**I bow to You, O caster of crooked glances, enchanting in Your threefold-bending form. As Your flute bewildered the universe, the young gopīs boldly sang Your glories. (Obeisance 53)**

**Комментарий:**

At this age Kṛṣṇa would stand with His legs, waist, and neck bent in a delightful way.

### Text 214

*cakṣuḥ-sāphalya-sampādi-*  
*śrīmad-vaktrābja-vīkṣaṇa*  
*nānā-mālā-lasad-veśa*  
*gopāla-sabha-śobhana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*cakṣuḥ*—of one’s eyes; *sāphalya*—the perfection; *sampādi*—causing; *śrīmat*—divine; *vaktra-abja*—whose lotus face; *vīkṣaṇa*—seeing; *nānā*—various; *mālā*—with garlands; *lasat*—enhanced; *veśa*—whose attire; *gopāla*—of the cowherds; *sabha*—of the community; *śobhana*—O splendor.

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Your divine lotus face makes having eyes worthwhile. Flower garlands of every kind enhance Your dress. O splendor of the cowherd community!**

**Комментарий:**

This verse begins Śrīla Sanātana’s summary of what the *gopīs* sang upon hearing Kṛṣṇa’s flute.

### Text 215

*sadāti-puṇyavad-veṇu-*  
*pīyamānādharāmṛta*  
*vṛndāvanāti-kīrti-śrī-*  
*prada-padābja-lakṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sadā*—always; *ati*—very; *puṇya-vat*—virtuous; *veṇu*—by Your flute; *pīyamāna*—being drunk; *adhara*—of whose lips; *amṛta*—the nectar; *vṛndāvana*—on Vṛndāvana; *ati*—great; *kīrti*—fame; *śrī*—and beauty; *prada*—which bestowed; *pada-abja*—of whose lotus feet; *lakṣaṇa*—the marks.

**Перевод:**

**The virtuous flute always drinks the nectar of Your lips. The marks left by Your lotus feet bestow great fame and beauty upon Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 216

*apūrva-muralī-gīta-*  
*nāda-nartita-barhiṇa*  
*śākhotkīrṇa-śakuntaugha*  
*sarva-prāṇi-manohara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*apūrva*—unique; *muralī*—of Your flute; *gīta*—of the song; *nāda*—by the reverberation; *nartita*—made to dance; *barhiṇa*—the peacocks; *śākha*—on the tree branches; *utkīrṇa*—who made into painted images; *śakunta-ogha*—many birds; *sarva*—of all; *prāṇi*—living beings; *manohara*—O enchanter.

**Перевод:**

**Your unique resounding flute-song makes the peacocks dance, turns the flocks of birds on the tree branches into painted pictures, and steals the hearts of all living beings.**

### Text 217

*vismārita-tṛṇa-grāsa-*  
*mṛgī-kula-vilobhita*  
*su-śīla-rūpa-saṅgīta-*  
*devī-gaṇa-vimohana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vismārita*—made to forget; *tṛṇa*—of grass; *grāsa*—eating; *mṛgī-kula*—of the groups of does; *vilobhita*—who became the object of desire; *su-śīla*—with Your fine character; *rūpa*—beauty; *saṅgīta*—and music; *devī-gaṇa*—the assembled goddesses; *vimohana*—who entranced.

**Перевод:**

**The song makes You the object of yearning for the does, who forget to eat the grass in their mouths. And with Your fine character, beauty, and music You entrance the multitude of devīs.**

### Text 218

*gāḍha-rodita-go-vṛnda*  
*premotkarṇita-tarṇaka*  
*nirvyāpārī-kṛtāśeṣa-*  
*muni-tulya-vihaṅgama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gāḍha*—intensely; *rodita*—who made cry; *go-vṛnda*—the herds of cows; *prema*—in pure love; *utkarṇita*—made to hold their ears high; *tarṇaka*—the calves; *nirvyāpārī*—void of activity; *kṛta*—who made; *aśeṣa*—all; *muni-tulya*—like sages; *vihaṅgama*—the birds.

**Перевод:**

**Your flute-song makes the herds of cows start mooing intensely, the calves perk their ears in pure love, and all the birds cease their activity, like silent sages.**

### Text 219

*gīta-stabdha-sarit-pūra*  
*cchatrāyita-balāhaka*  
*pulindī-prema-kṛd-ghāsa-*  
*lagna-pādābja-kuṅkuma*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gīta*—by this song; *stabdha*—stunned; *sarit-pūra*—the river currents; *chatrāyita*—made to serve as an umbrella; *balāhaka*—a cloud; *pulindī*—in the Pulinda women; *prema-kṛt*—which created pure love; *ghāsa*—on the grass; *lagna*—smeared; *pāda-abja*—of whose lotus feet; *kuṅkuma*—the *kuṅkuma* powder.

**Перевод:**

**The song stuns the river currents and makes a cloud begin to serve as Your umbrella. The kuṅkuma powder smeared on the grass by Your lotus feet awakens pure love in the Pulindīs, the aborigine women.**

### Text 220

*hari-sevaka-varyatva-*  
*sampad-govardhanārcita*  
*sva-prema-paramānanda-*  
*citrāyita-carācara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hari*—of Lord Hari; *sevaka*—of the servants; *varyatva*—being the best; *sampat*—with his treasures; *govardhana*—by Govardhana; *arcita*—who are worshiped; *sva-prema*—of love for You; *parama*—supreme; *ānanda*—by the ecstasy; *citrāyita*—made like painted figures; *cara-acara*—the moving and nonmoving beings.

**Перевод:**

**Govardhana Hill worships You with his treasures, thus acting as the best of Your servants. All moving and nonmoving beings, by the supreme ecstasy of their love for You, become like figures in a painting.**

### Text 221

*rāga-pallavita-sthāṇo*  
*gītānamita-pādapa*  
*gopāla-vilasad-veśa*  
*gopī-māra-vivardhana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*rāga*—out of loving attraction; *pallavita*—made to bear sprouts; *sthāṇo*—tree trunks; *gīta*—with the song; *ānamita*—made to bend down; *pādapa*—trees; *gopāla*—of a cowherd; *vilasat*—resplendent; *veśa*—whose dress; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; māra*—of the lust; *vivardhana*—O inciter.

**Перевод:**

**Tree trunks bear new sprouts out of loving attraction, and trees bend down when they hear the song of Your flute. Resplendent in the dress of a cowherd, You incite the lust of the gopīs.**

### Text 222

*aśeṣa-jaṅgama-sthāṇu-*  
*svabhāva-parivartaka*  
*ārdrī-kṛta-śilā-kāṣṭha*  
*nirjīvojjīvanāva naḥ*  
*(namaḥ 54)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*aśeṣa*—all; *jaṅgama*—of the mobile creatures; *sthāṇu*—and immobile creatures; *svabhāva*—the natural tendencies; *parivartaka*—who invert; *ārdrī-kṛta*—melted; *śilā*—stones; *kāṣṭha*—and logs; *nirjīva*—of lifeless objects; *ujjīvana*—O enlivener; *ava*—please protect; *naḥ*—us; *namaḥ 54*—obeisance 54.

**Перевод:**

**You reverse the natural tendencies of all moving and nonmoving beings. O melter of stones and logs, enlivener of lifeless objects, please protect us. (Obeisance 54)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe eka-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-first chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 22

### Text 223

*gopa-kanyā-vrata-prīta*  
*prasīda varadeśvara*  
*jala-krīḍā-samāsakta-*  
*gopī-vastrāpahāraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopa-kanyā*—of the cowherd maidens; *vrata*—by the vow; *prīta*—who were pleased; *prasīda*—be satisfied with me; *vara-da*—of givers of benedictions; *īśvara*—O Lord; *jala-krīḍā*—in water sports; *samāsakta*—who were engrossed; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; vastra*—of the clothes; *apahāraka*—O stealer.

**Перевод:**

**O best of those who give blessings, You were pleased by the vow of the cowherd maidens. Please be satisfied with me. You thief, You stole the clothes of the gopīs engrossed in playing in the water!**

### Text 224

*kadambārūḍha vande tvāṁ*  
*citra-narmokti-kovida*  
*gopī-stava-vilubdhātman*  
*gopikā-yācitāṁśuka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kadamba*—a *kadamba* tree; *ārūḍha*—who climbed; *vande*—I offer homage; *tvām*—to You; *citra*—of different kinds; *narma-ukti*—in clever words; *kovida*—who were skillful; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; stava*—by the entreaty; *vilubdha*—incited; *ātman*—whose mind; *gopikā*—by the *gopīs; yācita*—begged for; *aṁśuka*—their garments.

**Перевод:**

**I offer my homage to You, who climbed the kadamba tree. O skillful speaker of all sorts of joking words, the pleas of the gopīs quickened Your greed, and You made the gopīs beg for their garments.**

### Text 225

*sroto-vāsaḥ-sphurad-gopa-*  
*kanyākarṣaṇa-lālasa*  
*śītārta-yamunottīrṇa-*  
*gopī-bhāva-prasādita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*srotaḥ*—by the river water; *vāsaḥ*—dressed; *sphurat*—beautiful; *gopa-kanyā*—the cowherd maidens; *ākarṣaṇa*—for attracting; *lālasa*—whose eagerness; *śīta*—by the cold; *ārta*—afflicted; *yamunā*—in the Yamunā; *uttīrṇa*—standing; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; bhāva*—by the loving mood; *prasādita*—who were gratified.

**Перевод:**

**Eager to attract the cowherd maids beautifully dressed only by the river water, You were gratified by their mood of love as, pained by cold, they stood up in the Yamunā.**

### Text 226

*skandhāropita-gopa-strī-*  
*vastra sa-smita-bhāṣaṇa*  
*gopī-namas-kriyādeṣṭar*  
*gopy-eka-kara-vandita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*skandha*—on Your shoulder; *āropita*—who placed; *gopa-strī*—of the cowherd girls; *vastra*—the clothes; *sa-smita*—with laughter; *bhāṣaṇa*—whose speaking; *gopī*—to the *gopīs; namaḥ-kriyā*—the offering of obeisances; *ādeṣṭaḥ*—who ordered; *gopī*—by the *gopīs; eka-kara*—with one hand; *vandita*—who were offered respects.

**Перевод:**

**You put the clothes of the cowherd girls on Your shoulder and laughed as you spoke to the girls with joking words. When You ordered them to offer obeisances, they each offered You respects with one hand.**

### Text 227

*gopy-añjali-viśeṣārthin*  
*gopa-kanyā-namaskṛta*  
*gopī-vastra-da he gopī-*  
*kāmitākāmita-prada*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; añjali*—the offering of joined palms; *viśeṣa*—particularly; *arthin*—who wanted; *gopa-kanyā*—by the cowherd maids; *namaskṛta*—who were offered respects; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; vastra-da*—who gave the clothes; *he*—O; *gopī*—by the *gopīs; kāmita*—what was desired; *akāmita*—and what was not even desired; *prada*—one who grant.

**Перевод:**

**But You expressly wanted the cowherd maids to pay respects with joined palms, and they did so. O Kṛṣṇa, who returned the gopīs’ clothes, You granted the gopīs what they desired, and even what they didn’t.**

**Комментарий:**

The young *gopīs* had taken the vow of worshiping Kātyāyanī in the hope that the goddess would bless them to have Kṛṣṇa as their husband. By seeing them naked, Kṛṣṇa fulfilled that desire.

### Text 228

*gopī-citta-mahā-cora*  
*gopa-kanyā-bhujaṅgama*  
*dehi sva-gopikā-dāsyaṁ*  
*gopī-bhāva-vimohita*  
*(namaḥ 55)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; citta*—of the hearts; *mahā-cora*—O great thief; *gopa-kanyā*—of the cowherd maidens; *bhujaṅgama*—O snake; *dehi*—please give; *sva*—Your; *gopikā*—of the *gopīs; dāsyam*—the service; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; bhāva*—by the loving mood; *vimohita*—who are enchanted; *namaḥ 55*—obeisance 55.

**Перевод:**

**O crooked snake who threaten the cowherd maidens, O great thief of their hearts, please make me a servant of Your gopikās, whose ecstatic mood utterly beguiles You*****.*** **(Obeisance 55)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa is like a snake because He emits a kind of venom that poisons the *gopīs* with lusty desires.

### Text 229

*śrī-vṛndāvana-dūra-stha-*  
*viprā-bhāvābhikarṣita*  
*ātapatrāyitāśeṣa-*  
*taru-darśana-harṣita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrī-vṛndāvana*—from Vṛndāvana; *dūra-stha*—living far away; *viprā*—of the *brāhmaṇas’* wives; *bhāva*—by the loving mood; *abhikarṣita*—who were attracted; *ātapatrāyita*—who gave shade; *aśeṣa*—all; *taru*—the trees; *darśana*—by seeing; *harṣita*—who were delighted.

**Перевод:**

**You were attracted by the love of the brāhmaṇas’ wives, who lived far from Śrī Vṛndāvana. And You were delighted to see all the trees, who give shade from the heat of the sun.**

### Text 230

*paropakāra-nirata-*  
*taru-janmābhinandaka*  
*yamunāmṛta-santṛpta-*  
*go-gopa-gaṇa-sevita*  
*(namaḥ 56)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*para-upakāra*—to helping others; *nirata*—dedicated; *taru*—of the trees; *janma*—the birth; *abhinandaka*—who congratulated; *yamunā*—of the Yamunā; *amṛta*—by the nectar; *santṛpta*—who were satisfied; *go*—by the cows; *gopa-gaṇa*—and the cowherds; *sevita*—who were served; *namaḥ 56*—obeisance 56.

**Перевод:**

**You praised the trees for their birth, in which they were dedicated to helping others. And You were served by the cows and cowherds, who were fully satisfied by the nectarean water of the Yamunā. (Obeisance 56)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe dvā-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-second chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 23

### Text 231

*yajña-patnī-prasādārtha-*  
*gopa-kṣud-ati-vardhana*  
*kṣudhārta-gopa-vāg-vyagra*  
*jaya yajvānna-yācaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yajña*—of the *brāhmaṇas* performing sacrifices; *patnī*—to the wives; *prasāda-artha*—for showing mercy; *gopa*—in the cowherd boys; *kṣut*—hunger; *ati-vardhana*—who very much incited; *kṣudhā*—by hunger; *ārta*—afflicted; *gopa*—of the cowherd boys; *vāk*—by the words; *vyagra*—who were made concerned; *jaya*—victory to You; *yajva*—from the performers of sacrifice; *anna*—food; *yācaka*—who caused begging.

**Перевод:**

**To favor the wives of the Vedic brāhmaṇas, You stirred hunger in the cowherd boys. The words of Your hungry cowherd friends gave You concern. Victory to You, who made the boys beg food from the performers of sacrifice.**

### Text 232

*duṣprajña-yajvāvajñāta*  
*bhakta-viprā-didṛkṣita*  
*brāhmaṇy-ākarṣakodanta*  
*yajña-patnī-manohara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*duṣprajña*—with corrupted intelligence; *yajva*—by the sacrificial *brāhmaṇas; avajñāta*—who were neglected; *bhakta*—devoted; *viprā*—by the *brāhmaṇas’* wives; *didṛkṣita*—who were wanted to be seen; *brāhmaṇī*—the *brāhmaṇas’* wives; *ākarṣaka*—attracting; *udanta*—the news of whom; *yajña-patnī*—of the *yajña-patnīs; manohara*—O enchanter.

**Перевод:**

**The foolish sacrificial brāhmaṇas refused Your request. But their devoted wives, the yajña-patnīs, were eager to see You. O enchanter of the yajña-patnīs, hearing news of You they were drawn to You.**

**Комментарий:**

The Vedic *brāhmaṇas* considered Kṛṣṇa a mere human being, but their wives were spontaneously attracted to His service. Thus the wives were much more intelligent than their supposedly accomplished husbands.

### Text 233

*brāhmaṇī-tāpa-bhic citra-*  
*veśāvasthāna-bhūṣaṇa*  
*jaya dvija-satī-ślāghin*  
*yajña-patnīṣṭa-dāsyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brāhmaṇī*—of the *brāhmaṇa* ladies; *tāpa*—of the distress; *bhit*—O breaker; *citra*—wonderful; *veśa*—whose dress; *avasthāna*—pose; *bhūṣaṇa*—and ornaments; *jaya*—victory to You; *dvija*—of the *brāhmaṇas; satī*—the chaste wives; *ślāghin*—who praised; *yajña-patnī*—by the *yajña-patnīs; iṣṭa*—desired; *dāsyaka*—whose servitude.

**Перевод:**

**Wonderful in Your dress, Your ornaments, and the way You stood, You tore apart the distress of the yajña-patnīs, who were intent on becoming Your servants. O Kṛṣṇa, who praised those chaste wives of the brāhmaṇas, victory to You!**

### Text 234

*brāhmaṇī-kāku-santuṣṭa*  
*brāhmaṇī-prema-bhakti-da*  
*pati-ruddha-satī-sadyo-*  
*vimukti-da namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brāhmaṇī*—of the *brāhmaṇas’* wives; *kāku*—by the plaintive words; *santuṣṭa*—who were pleased; *brāhmaṇī*—on the *brāhmaṇas’* wives; *prema-bhakti-da*—O bestower of loving devotion; *pati*—by her husband; *ruddha*—obstructed; *satī*—to the chaste lady; *sadyaḥ*—immediate; *vimukti-da*—who gave liberation; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**Перевод:**

**Pleased with them for their plaintive words, You granted those wives pure loving devotion. And to one chaste wife, whose husband had blocked her, You gave liberation at once. I bow down to You.**

### Text 235

*yajamānī-vitīrṇānna-*  
*tṛpta viprānutāpa-da*  
*svīya-saṅga-dvija-jñāna-*  
*prada brahmaṇya-deva he*  
*(namaḥ 57)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yajamānī*—by the mistresses of the sacrifice; *vitīrṇa*—served; *anna*—with the food; *tṛpta*—who were satisfied; *vipra*—the *brāhmaṇas; anutāpa-da*—who made feel remorse; *svīya*—of their (wives); *saṅga*—through the association; *dvija*—to the *brāhmaṇas; jñāna*—of knowledge; *prada*—O giver; *brahmaṇya-deva he*—O Lord who favor the *brāhmaṇas; namaḥ 57*—obeisance 57.

**Перевод:**

**Fully satisfied with the food served You by those mistresses of the sacrifice, You made the brāhmaṇas feel remorse. You then gave those brāhmaṇas real knowledge through the association of their wives. O Lord who favor the brāhmaṇas! (Obeisance 57)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe trayo-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-third chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 24

### Text 236

*jaya vāsava-yāja-jña*  
*pitṛ-pṛṣṭa-makhārthaka*  
*śruta-tātokta-yajñārtha*  
*karma-vādāvatāraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *vāsava-yāja*—of the sacrifice for Indra; *jña*—who were aware; *pitṛ*—Your father; *pṛṣṭa*—who asked; *makha*—of the sacrifice; *arthaka*—the purpose; *śruta*—who heard; *tāta*—by Your father; *ukta*—explained; *yajña-artha*—the purpose of the sacrifice; *karma-vāda*—the theory of fruitive work; *avatāraka*—who introduced.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! Aware of the sacrifice for Indra, You asked Your father its purpose and heard him explain its meaning. You then introduced the theory of fruitive work.**

**Комментарий:**

Although just a little boy, Kṛṣṇa knew all the details of sacrificial performance and could ask perceptive questions about the *indra-yajña.* Nanda Mahārāja had to respond to Kṛṣṇa’s intelligent inquiry, explaining to Him why the cowherds performed the sacrifice every year.

### Text 237

*nānāpanyāya-vādaugha-*  
*śakra-yāga-nivāraka*  
*govardhanādri-go-yajña-*  
*pravartaka namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—various; *apanyāya*—of spurious arguments; *vāda*—of presentations; *ogha*—by a flood; *śakra*—of Indra; *yāga*—the sacrifice; *nivāraka*—who stopped; *govardhana-adri*—for Govardhana Hill; *go*—and the cows; *yajña*—of a sacrifice; *pravartaka*—O initiator; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**Перевод:**

**By all kinds of spurious arguments, You stopped the sacrifice to Indra and started the sacrifice for Govardhana Hill and the cows. I bow down to You.**

*yajña-dattopahāra-bhuk*  
*gopa-viśvāsanārthādri-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prokta*—who explained; *adri*—of the hill; *go*—and the cows; *makha-vidhe*—the method for worshiping; *yajña*—for the sacrifice; *datta*—provided; *upahāra*—the offerings; *bhuk*—who ate; *gopa*—of the cowherds; *viśvāsana-artha*—for inspiring the confidence; *adri*—of the hill; *chala*—on the pretense; *sthūla*—huge; *anya*—another; *rūpa*—form; *dhṛk*—who assumed.

**Перевод:**

**You explained how to worship the cows and the hill. And to inspire the cowherds with confidence, You assumed a second, huge form and claimed to be the hill Yourself, and You devoured the offerings provided for the sacrifice.**

*govardhana*—of Govardhana; *śiraḥ-ratna*—O crown jewel; *govardhana*—of Govardhana; *mahattva-da*—O exalter; *kṛta*—who had arranged; *bhūṣā*—their ornaments; *aśana*—and the food offerings; *ābhīra*—by the cowherds; *kārita*—who caused to be performed; *adri*—of the hill; *parikrama*—circumambulation; *namaḥ 58*—obeisance 58.

**O crown jewel of Govardhana, who gave glory to Govardhana, You had the cowherds dress themselves up, prepare offerings of food, and circumambulate the hill. (Obeisance 58)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe catur-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-fourth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

### Text 240

**Thus ends the twenty-fifth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 27

### Text 247

*bhīta-lajjita-deveśa-*

*jita-māyāsta-dūṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhīta*—frightened; *lajjita*—and ashamed; *deva-īśa*—of the king of heaven; *kirīṭa*—by the crown; *spṛṣṭa*—touched; *pāda*—whose feet; *he*—O; *vāsava*—by Indra; *stuta*—who were praised; *sarva-jña*—O knower of everything; *jita*—who have conquered; *māya*—illusion; *asta*—who have eradicated; *dūṣaṇa*—faults.

**Перевод:**

**The king of heaven, frightened and ashamed, touched his crown to Your feet. He then offered You his prayers: O all-knowing one, conqueror of illusion, dispeller of all faults!**

*dharma-pāla khala-dhvaṁsin*

*śaraṇāgata-vatsala*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dharma*—of religion; *pāla*—O protector; *khala*—of the cruel; *dhvaṁsin*—O destroyer; *duṣṭa*—of wicked people; *māna*—the pride; *ghna*—killing; *ceṣṭita*—whose activities; *svīya*—of Your servants; *aparādha*—of the offenses; *kṣamaṇa*—O forgiver; *śaraṇa-āgata*—to those who come for shelter; *vatsala*—who are compassionate.

**Перевод:**

**O protector of religion, O destroyer of the cruel, by Your acts You kill the pride of the wicked. You forgive the offenses of Your servants and show compassion to those who take shelter of You.**

### Text 249

*śakra-śikṣaka śakratva-*  
*prada he surabhīḍita*  
*surabhī-prārthitendratva*  
*śrī-govinda namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śakra*—of Indra; *śikṣaka*—O instructor; *śakratva*—of the position of Indra; *prada*—O giver; *he*—O; *surabhī-īḍita*—who were prayed to by Surabhī; *surabhī*—by Mother Surabhī; *prārthita*—requested; *indratva*—whose position of Indra; *śrī-govinda*—O Śrī Govinda; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**After You instructed Indra and gave him back his heavenly post, Mother Surabhī worshiped You and asked You to become her Indra. O Śrī Govinda, I bow down to You.**

**Комментарий:**

Even after being forgiven, Indra felt ashamed, and therefore Surabhī, the mother of the cows, presided over the coronation ceremony in which Kṛṣṇa was heralded as Govinda—the Indra of the cows and the owner and attractor of all His devotees’ senses.

**Перевод:**

**That wish-fulfilling cow ceremonially bathed You in a flood of milk, the demigods worshiped You, and Airāvata bathed You in water brought in his trunk from the celestial Gaṅgā.**

*iti daśama-skandhe sapta-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-seventh chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 28

### Text 252

*prasīda me payo-magna-*

**Перевод:**

**Please be satisfied with me. O dear son of Your father, when Nanda disappeared in the water You searched for him, and when You reached the abode of Varuṇa You favored Varuṇa with a cherished opportunity to see You.**

*varuṇārcita-pādābja*  
*varuṇāti-prasādita*

*nanda-bandha-vimocana*

*varuṇa*—by Varuṇa; *arcita*—worshiped; *pāda-abja*—whose lotus feet; *varuṇa*—with Varuṇa; *ati-prasādita*—who were very satisfied; *varuṇa*—of Varuṇa; *āgaḥ*—of the offense; *kṣamā-kārin*—O forgiver; *nanda*—of Nanda Mahārāja; *bandha*—from imprisonment; *vimocana*—O releaser.

**Перевод:**

**Very satisfied with Varuṇa, who worshiped Your lotus feet, You forgave his offense and had Nanda freed from imprisonment.**
