*nanda*—by Nanda; *śrāvita*—who were made to hear; *māhātmya*—Your glories; *gopa*—of the cowherds; *jñāna-ati*—beyond the capacity to understand; *vaibhava*—whose powers; *gopa*—of the cowherds; *saṅkalpa*—of the desires; *vijñātaḥ*—who were aware; *karuṇā*—with mercy; *ākula*—excited; *mānasa*—whose heart.

**Перевод:**

**Though Nanda described Your glories, Your powers were beyond what the cowherds could understand. Knowing their desires, You were eager to show them mercy.**

### Text 255

*sva-lokāloka-saṁhṛṣṭa-*  
*gopa-vargārtha-varga-da*  
*brahma-hradoddhṛtābhīrā-*  
*bhīṣṭa-brahma-pada-prada*  
*(namaḥ 62)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-loka*—Your world; *āloka*—by seeing; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—overjoyed; *gopa-varga*—all the cowherds; *artha-varga*—of the various goals of life; *da*—O granter; *brahma-hrada*—from the lake of Brahman; *uddhṛta*—arisen; *ābhīra*—of the cowherds; *abhīṣṭa*—desired; *brahma-pada*—of the spiritual destination; *prada*—O giver; *namaḥ 62*—obeisance 62.

**Перевод:**

**To each cowherd, overjoyed when allowed to see Your world, You granted all the main goals of life. You brought the cowherds to the lake known as Brahma-hrada, and when they rose from their bath You gave them the spiritual destination they desired. (Obeisance 62)**

**Комментарий:**

There is no difference between the Goloka above Vaikuṇṭha and the Gokula on earth, but by the influence of Yogamāyā’s illusion the cowherds thought their own position inferior. Kṛṣṇa gratified them with a vision of Goloka and the assurance that they would enter His eternal abode at the end of their current pastimes.

*iti daśama-skandhe aṣṭā-viṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-eighth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 29

### Text 256

*jaya jaya nija-pādāmbhoja-sat-prema-dāyin*  
*rasika-jana-mano-hṛd-rāsa-līlā-vinodin*  
*vivṛta-madhura-kaiśorāti-līlā-prabhāva*  
*priya-jana-vaśa-vartin vyakta-satya-svabhāva*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *jaya*— victory to You; *nija*—Your; *pāda-ambhoja*—for the lotus feet; *sat*—transcendental; *prema*—of love; *dāyin*—O bestower; *rasika-jana*—of those who know how to taste *rasa; manaḥ*—the hearts; *hṛt*—which steals; *rāsa-līlā*—of the *rāsa-līlā; vinodin*—O enjoyer; *vivṛta*—who displayed; *madhura*—the sweetness of which; *kaiśora*—in adolescence; *ati*—powerful; *līlā*—of pastimes; *prabhāva*—the influence; *priya-jana*—of Your dear devotees; *vaśa-vartin*—who submitted to the control; *vyakta*—who revealed; *satya*—eternal; *svabhāva*—Your true nature.

**Перевод:**

**Victory, victory to You, bestower of transcendental love for Your lotus feet! The rāsa-līlā steals the hearts of those who relish rasa. O enjoyer of the rāsa-līlā, in Your adolescence You reached the height of sweetness and showed the powerful influence of Your pastimes. By submitting to the control of Your dear devotees, You revealed Your true eternal nature.**

**Комментарий:**

In other words, may Kṛṣṇa enjoy all victory in His *rāsa-līlā* and manifest His supreme glories while dancing with the *gopīs.* Out of great respect for this pastime, which of all of Kṛṣṇa’s Vṛndāvana *līlās* is the most important, Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī repeats the exclamation *jaya.* He then describes the four sweetest glories of God, visible in Kṛṣṇa alone: Kṛṣṇa gives His devotees pure love for His lotus feet in the most exalted mood of devotion, the sweetness of pure love (*prema-mādhurya*), He enjoys the *rāsa-līlā* (*līlā-mādhurya*), which is relishable by *rasika* Vaiṣṇavas, He displays His sweet new youth (*rūpa-mādhurya*), and He demonstrates the all-powerful influence of His pleasure pastimes, especially with the charm of His flute (*veṇu-mādhurya*). In the *rāsa-līlā* Kṛṣṇa acts as a *dhīra-lalita* hero by submitting to the control of the *gopīs.*

### Text 257

*tyaktātma-rāmatā-māya*  
*tucchī-kṛta-nijāgama*  
*bhakta-prārthya-nija-prema-*  
*dhārā-dānārtha-rāsa-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tyakta*—who made abandon; *ātma-rāmatā*—of being self-satisfied sages; *māya*—the false pride; *tucchī-kṛta*—who belittled; *nija*—Your own; *āgama*—revealed scriptures; *bhakta*—by Your devotees; *prārthya*—prayed for; *nija*—Your; *prema*—of *prema; dhārā*—a flood; *dāna-artha*—in order to give away; *rāsa-kṛt*—O performer of the *rāsa-līlā.*

**Перевод:**

**To make the self-contented sages abandon their false pride, You belittled the value of Your own revealed scriptures. To give away a flood of prema, always yearned for by Your devotees, You performed the rāsa-līlā.**

### Text 258

*śaran-niśā-vihārotka*  
*candrodaya-ratāśaya*  
*gopī-vimohanodgīta*  
*paramākarṣa-paṇḍita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śarat*—of the autumn; *niśā*—in the night; *vihāra*—for enjoyment; *utka*—who were eager; *candra*—of the moon; *udaya*—by the rise; *rata*—interested in loving affairs; *āśaya*—whose mind; *gopī*-v*imohana*—enchanting the *gopīs; udgīta*—whose flute-song; *parama*—supreme *ākarṣa*—of the art of attraction; *paṇḍita*—O scholar.

**Перевод:**

**You were eager to enjoy in the autumn night, the rising moon turning Your heart toward thoughts of love. And You enchanted the gopīs with Your flute-song. O scholar in the supreme art of attraction!**

### Text 259

*anādṛta-niṣedhaughī-*  
*kṛta-gopa-satī-gaṇa*  
*tyakta-sarva-kriyāpekṣa-*  
*gopa-strī-prāpta-saṅgama*  
*(namaḥ 63)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*anādṛta*—disregarding; *niṣedha*—prohibition; *oghī-kṛta*—who caused to assemble; *gopa*—of the cowherds; *satī-gaṇa*—the chaste wives; *tyakta*—abandoning; *sarva*—all; *kriyā*—for obligations; *apekṣa*—consideration; *gopa-strī*—by the cowherd girls; *prāpta*—obtained; *saṅgama*—whose association; *namaḥ 63*—obeisance 63.

**Перевод:**

**The chaste cowherd girls, caring nothing for any prohibitions, left all their duties and flocked to be with You. (Obeisance 63)**

### Text 260

*prasīda bhartṛ-saṁruddha-*  
*gopī-premāgni-vardhana*  
*sva-kāmonmatta-gopa-strī-*  
*deha-bandha-vimocana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—please be satisfied; *bhartṛ*—by their husbands; *saṁruddha*—restrained; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; prema-agni*—of the fire of love; *vardhana*—O You who increased; *sva-kāma*—by desire for You; *unmatta*—driven insane; *gopa-strī*—of the cowherd women; *deha-bandha*—from bodily entanglement; *vimocana*—O deliverer.

**Перевод:**

**Please be satisfied with me. In the gopīs kept home by their husbands, You stoked the fire of love. Those gopīs were driven mad by desire for You, and You set them free from all bodily ties.**

### Text 261

*śuka-krodhokti-nirṇīta-*  
*mahā-mahima-sāgara*  
*krodhādi-bhajamānārtha-*  
*prada-smaraṇa māṁ smara*  
*(namaḥ 64)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śuka*—by Śukadeva; *krodha*—in anger; *ukti*—words spoken; *nirṇīta*—delineated; *mahā*—vast; *mahima*—of whose glories; *sāgara*—the ocean; *krodha-ādi*—in the moods of anger and so on; *bhajamāna*—to those who in worship; *artha-prada*—giving success; *smaraṇa*—whose remembrance; *mām*—me; *smara*—please remember; *namaḥ 64*—obeisance 64.

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva’s angry words defined the vast ocean of Your glories. To those who worship You even in such moods as anger, remembering You gives all success. O Kṛṣṇa, please remember me. (Obeisance 64)**

**Комментарий:**

At this point Parīkṣit Mahārāja raised a doubt about the liberation of the *gopīs:* Since the *gopīs* didn’t understand that Kṛṣṇa is the Absolute Truth, why was their worship of Him more effective than their ignorant devotion to their husbands and children? After all, weren’t Kṛṣṇa and the husbands and children of the *gopīs* all nondifferent from the Supreme? Śukadeva Gosvāmī replied sharply to remind Parīkṣit of Kṛṣṇa’s unique position.

### Text 262

*gopikā-nayanāsvādya*  
*gopī-vañcana-vāk-paṭo*  
*gopī-miṣṭokti-śuśrūṣā-*  
*sva-dharma-bhaya-darśaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopikā*—of the young *gopīs;* *nayana*—with the eyes; *āsvādya*—who could be tasted; *gopī*—the *gopīs; vañcana*—deceiving; *vāk*—in speech; *paṭo*—who were expert; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; miṣṭa*—sweet; *ukti*—the words; *śuśrūṣā*—out of eagerness to hear; *sva-dharma*—regarding religious duty; *bhaya*—the cause of fear; *darśaka*—who pointed out.

**Перевод:**

**You allowed the young gopīs to taste Your beauty with their eyes. O eloquent deceiver of the gopīs, out of keenness to hear their sweet words, You instructed them about the danger of transgressing religious duties.**

### Text 263

*gopī-mahādhi-vistārin*  
*gopī-rodana-vardhana*  
*gopy-arthitāṅga-saṁsarga*  
*gopī-kākūkti-nirvṛta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; mahā*—great; *ādhi*—of the mental anguish; *vistārin*—O amplifier; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; rodana*—of the crying; *vardhana*—O nourisher; *gopī*—by the *gopīs; arthita*—hankered for; *aṅga—*of Your body; *saṁsarga*—the touch; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; kāku-ukti*—by the pitiful laments; *nirvṛta*—who were satisfied.

**Перевод:**

**You deepened the anguish of the gopīs and made them cry more and more. They yearned for the touch of Your body, and You took pleasure in their pitiful laments.**

### Text 264

*avahitthā-parityakta*  
*prodyan-mānasa-vikriya*  
*dhūrtāgra-gaṇya māṁ pāhi*  
*kāma-mugdha smitānana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*avahitthā*—with disregard; *parityakta*—who were rejected; *prodyat*—visible; *mānasa*—of whose mind; *vikriya*—the change; *dhūrta*—of rascals; *agra-gaṇya*—O foremost; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *kāma*—by desire to enjoy; *mugdha*—who were bewildered; *smita*—smiling; *ānana*—whose face.

**Перевод:**

**When the gopīs spurned Your advice, Your mind visibly changed. A smile appeared on Your face as You succumbed to the desire to enjoy. O most infamous of rascals, please protect me.**

### Text 265

*vyakta-svabhāva-madhura*  
*smara-lolita-locana*  
*gopī-manoharāpāṅga*  
*gopikā-śata-yūtha-pa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vyakta*—manifest; *svabhāva*—natural; *madhura*—whose charm; *smara*—from the influence of Cupid; *lolita*—restless; *locana*—whose eyes; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; manohara*—stealing the hearts; *apāṅga*—whose sidelong glances; *gopikā*—of young *gopīs; śata*—hundreds; *yūtha*—of groups; *pa*—O leader.

**Перевод:**

**Your eyes restless under the sway of Cupid, You showed Your natural charm. O leader of young gopīs in hundreds of groups, Your sidelong glances stole their hearts.**

### Text 266

*vaijayantī-srag-ākalpa*  
*śarac-candra-nibhānana*  
*yamunā-pulināsīna*  
*gopī-ramaṇa pāhi mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vaijayantī-srak*—with a *vaijayantī* garland; *ākalpa*—whose decoration; *śarat*—of autumn; *candra-nibha*—like the moon; *ānana*—whose face; *yamunā*—of the Yamunā; *pulina*—on the shore; *āsīna*—who were seated; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; ramaṇa*—O enjoyer; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām—*me.

**Перевод:**

**You were seated on the shore of the Yamunā, adorned with a vaijayantī garland, Your face shining like the autumn moon. O enjoyer of the gopīs, please protect me.**

### Text 267

*jita-manmatha tantra-jña*  
*gopī-māna-vivardhana*  
*gopikāti-prasādārtha-*  
*kṛtāntardhāna-vibhrama*  
*(namaḥ 65)*

**Пословный перевод:**

***jita*****—who conquered;** ***manmatha*****—Cupid;** ***tantra-jña*****—O knower of confidential sciences;** ***gopī*****—of the** ***gopīs; māna*****—of the pride;** ***vivardhana*****—O increaser;** ***gopikā*****—to the** ***gopīs; ati-prasāda*****—of most special favor;** ***artha*****—for the sake;**

**Перевод:**

**You built up the pride of the gopīs and then astonishingly disappeared, just to bestow upon them Your most special favor. (Obeisance 65)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe ūna-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the twenty-ninth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 30

### Text 268

*jaya gopī-gaṇānviṣṭa*  
*vṛkṣa-sampṛṣṭa-darśana*  
*tulasī-mālatī-mallī-*  
*yūthikā-pṛṣṭa-vīkṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *gopī-gaṇa*—by the *gopīs; anviṣṭa*—who were searched for; *vṛkṣa*—the trees; *sampṛṣṭa*—asked; *darśana*—about seeing whom; *tulasī-mālatī-mallī-yūthikā*—the *tulasī, mālatī, mallī,* and *yūthikā; pṛṣṭa*—asked; *vīkṣaṇa*—about Your being seen.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs began to search for You all around. They asked the trees whether they had seen You and also asked the tulasī and the jasmine plants like the mālatī, mallī, and yuthikā. Victory to You!**

### Text 269

*kṣity-utsava-samāloka-*  
*sambhāvita-samāgama*  
*eṇī-pṛṣṭāṅghripāpṛṣṭa*  
*latotpulaka-sūcita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kṣiti*—of the earth; *utsava*—the festive joy; *samāloka*—from seeing; *sambhāvita*—surmised; *samāgama*—whose coming; *eṇī*—from the deer; *pṛṣṭa*—asked; *aṅghripa*—from the trees; *apṛṣṭa*—not even asked; *latā*—on the creepers; *utpulaka*—from the joyful eruptions on the skin; *sūcita*—inferred.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs surmised that the festive joy of the earth must have been due to Your coming. They asked Your whereabouts from the deer, but not from the trees; from the joyful eruptions on the creepers clinging to the trees, the gopīs could see that You had passed by.**

**Комментарий:**

While the *gopīs* continued their search, they saw the ground below them erupting with new sprouts of *dūrvā* grass, as if in a festive mood. This, the *gopīs* reasoned, was evidence that Kṛṣṇa had recently passed that way. Later, when they saw new sprouts growing on the creepers entwined around some trees, they took this apparent symptom of ecstasy as evidence that Kṛṣṇa had been present there also.

### Text 270

*unmattī-kṛta-gopy-ogha*  
*gopikānukṛtehita*  
*jaya gopī-gaṇāviṣṭa*  
*sva-bhāvāpita-gopika*

**Пословный перевод:**

*unmattī-kṛta*—who drove to insanity; *gopī-ogha*—the throngs of *gopīs; gopikā*—by the *gopīs; anukṛta*—imitated; *īhita*—whose activities; *jaya*—victory to You; *gopī-gaṇa*—of the *gopīs; āviṣṭa*—who took possession; *sva-bhāva*—Your own moods; *āpita*—who made assume; *gopika*—the young *gopīs.*

**Перевод:**

**The throngs of gopīs went mad and imitated Your exploits. Victory to You, who took hold of the gopīs’ minds and made the gopīs assume Your moods.**

**Комментарий:**

As the anxiety of the *gopīs* reached its peak, some of them, thinking themselves Kṛṣṇa, acted out His pastimes, starting from the killing of Pūtanā.

### Text 271

*gopī-lakṣita-pādābja-*  
*lakṣma-mārgita-paddhate*  
*anya-strī-yukta-pādābja-*  
*cihnekṣā-gopikārti-da*  
*(namaḥ 66)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—by the *gopīs; lakṣita*—traced; *pāda-abja*—of Your lotus feet; *lakṣma*—by the prints; *mārgita*—found; *paddhate*—whose path; *anya*—another; *strī*—of a woman; *yukta*—mixed; *pāda-abja*—of the lotus feet; *cihna*—the marks; *īkṣā*—by their seeing; *gopikā*—for the *gopīs; ārti-da*—O giver of distress; *namaḥ 66*—obeisance 66.

**Перевод:**

**By finding the prints of Your lotus feet, the gopīs found Your path. And what distress You caused the young gopīs when they saw Your lotus footprints mixed with those of some other woman! (Obeisance 66)**

### Text 272

*rādhārādhita rādheśa*  
*rādhikā-prāṇa-vallabha*  
*rādhā-ramaṇa vande tvāṁ*  
*rādhikā-prema-nirjita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*rādhā-ārādhita*—who were fully worshiped by Rādhā; *rādhā-īśa*—O Lord of Rādhā; *rādhikā-prāṇa-vallabha*—O treasure of Rādhikā’s life; *rādhā-ramaṇa*—O enjoyer of Rādhikā; *vande*—I offer my respects; *tvām*—to You; *rādhikā*—of Rādhikā; *prema*—by the love; *nirjita*—who were defeated.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord of Rādhā, fully worshiped by Rādhā, treasure of Rādhikā’s life! O enjoyer of Rādhā, I offer my respects to You, who were defeated by Rādhikā’s love.**

**Комментарий:**

In the name Rādheśa, *īśa* means both “master” and “satisfier of the desires.” By worshiping Kṛṣṇa perfectly, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī earned the privilege of having Him respond to Her in both of these ways.

### Text 273

*rādhā-sannyasta-sarvasva*  
*strī-straiṇa-gati-darśaka*  
*rādhānutāpa-sammoha-*  
*karāntardhāna-kautuka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*rādhā*—to Rādhā; *sannyasta*—surrendered; *sarva-sva*—whose everything; *strī*—of women; *straiṇa*—and of men attached to women; *gati*—the destination; *darśaka*—who showed; *rādhā*—of Rādhā; *anutāpa*—remorse; *sammoha*—and bewilderment; *kara*—causing; *antardhāna*—of disappearance; *kautuka*—whose whim.

**Перевод:**

**You offered Rādhā everything You had, and showed the fate of women and of men attached to them. You made Her feel remorseful and bewildered when by Your whim You suddenly disappeared.**

**Комментарий:**

By implied contrast with the sublime enjoyment He and Rādhā shared, Kṛṣṇa demonstrated the wretched shortsightedness of men and women under the sway of mundane lust.

### Text 274

*sakhī-gaṇāpta-rādhokta*  
*tad-vismāpana-ceṣṭita*  
*rādhā-sahita-gopa-strī-*  
*muhur-mārgita pāhi mām*  
*(namaḥ 67)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sakhī-gaṇa*—with Her girlfriends; *āpta*—rejoined; *rādhā*—by Rādhā; *ukta*—who were spoken about; *tat*—them; *vismāpana*—which amazed; *ceṣṭita*—whose activities; *rādhā-sahita*—together with Rādhā; *gopa-strī*—by the cowherd girls; *muhuḥ*—again; *mārgita*—who were searched for; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me; *namaḥ 67*—obeisance 67.

**Перевод:**

**When Rādhā rejoined Her girlfriends and told them what had happened, Your dealings amazed the gopīs, and together with Her they searched for You. Please protect me. (Obeisance 67)**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīla Sanātana prays for Kṛṣṇa to save him by lifting him out of the ocean of misery he feels in separation from the Lord.

*iti daśama-skandhe triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirtieth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 31

### Text 275

*punaḥ-pulina-samprāpta-*  
*gopī-gītārthitodaya*  
*janma-mātra-vraja-śrī-da*  
*sva-janānveṣaṇārti-da*

**Пословный перевод:**

*punaḥ*—again; *pulina*—the riverbank; *samprāpta*—who reached; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; gīta*—by the song; *arthita*—prayed for; *udaya*—whose appearance; *janma-mātra*—merely by Your birth; *vraja*—on Vraja; *śrī-da*—who bestowed opulence; *sva-jana*—to Your devotees; *anveṣaṇa*—by their having to search; *ārti-da*—O giver of pain.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs returned to the riverbank and begged You to reappear, with a song: Merely by Your birth here You showered all opulence upon Vraja. And by making Your devotees search for You, You give them pain.**

### Text 276

*dṛg-abja-hanyamāna-strī-*  
*vadha-niḥśaṅka-hṛdaya*  
*viṣādi-nānā-duḥkha-ghna*  
*svīyārti-jñāntarātma-dṛk*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dṛk*—by Your glance *abja*—lotuslike; *hanyamāna*—being killed; *strī*—of women; *vadha*—for the murder; *niḥśaṅka*—feeling no remorse; *hṛdaya*—whose heart; *viṣa*—caused by poison; *ādi*—and so on; *nānā*—various; *duḥkha*—of miseries; *ghna*—O destroyer; *svīya*—of Your dear devotees; *ārti-jña*—O knower of the distress; *antara-ātma*—of the heart; *dṛk*—O witness.

**Перевод:**

**Though You destroy every sort of torment from poison or other threats, Your heart feels no remorse for murdering the women You have killed with the glance of Your lotus eyes. You know the distress of Your dear devotees, O witness in everyone’s heart.**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa protected His Vraja-vāsīs from all kinds of dangers—not only from poison but also from snakes, rain, wind, lightning, fire, and powerful demons like Vṛṣa and Vyoma.

### Text 277

*viśva-rakṣārtha-sañjāta*  
*bhaktābhaya-da-hasta he*  
*sva-jana-prārthya-saṁsparśa*  
*nānā-guṇa-padāmbuja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*viśva*—of the world; *rakṣā-artha*—for the protection; *sañjāta*—who were born; *bhakta*—to Your devotees; *abhaya-da*—granting fearlessness; *hasta*—whose hand; *he*—O; *sva-jana*—by Your devotees; *prārthya*—prayed for; *saṁsparśa*—whose contact; *nānā*—various; *guṇa*—having excellent qualities; *pada-ambuja*—whose lotus feet.

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, born to protect the whole world, Your hand grants fearlessness to Your devotees, who always pray for intimate touch with You. Your lotus feet have many excellences.**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet destroy the sins of anyone who surrenders to them, and they are the abode of the goddess of fortune. They are found on the head of the king of serpents, Kāliya, and they have the power to subdue lusty passions.

### Text 278

*manojña-madhurālāpa*  
*dāsī-gaṇa-vimohana*  
*śruti-maṅgala-santapta-*  
*prāṇārtha-da-kathāmṛta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*manojña*—attractive; *madhura*—and sweet; *ālāpa*—whose talks; *dāsī-gaṇa*—of all Your maidservants; *vimohana*—O enchanter; *śruti*—by hearing; *maṅgala*—creating good fortune; *santapta*—to the suffering souls; *prāṇa*—life; *artha*—and desired success; *da*—giving; *kathā*—of whose topics; *amṛta*—the nectar.

**Перевод:**

**Your talks are attractive and sweet, O enchanter of all Your maidservants. And nectarean topics about You, simply when heard, create good fortune and give life to the suffering souls and grant their every desire.**

**Комментарий:**

The many conditioned souls, who suffer the reactions of past sins in this world, can be freed from their torment by turning their minds to *kṛṣṇa-kathā.* And for those already liberated but suffering in the fire of separation from Kṛṣṇa, *kṛṣṇa-kathā* is again the only effective relief.

### Text 279

*manaḥ-kṣobhaka-mādhurya*  
*mṛdulāṅghri-vanāṭaka*  
*yugāyita-viyogāṇo*  
*mano-hṛd-adharāmṛta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*manaḥ*—the mind; *kṣobhaka*—disturbing; *mādhurya*—whose charms; *mṛdula*—tender; *aṅghri*—with Your feet; *vana*—in the forest; *aṭaka*—O roamer; *yugāyita*—seeming like a long age; *viyoga*—of whose separation; *aṇo*—the shortest moment; *manaḥ*—the heart; *hṛt*—stealing; *adhara*—from whose lips; *amṛta*—the nectar.

**Перевод:**

**While Your tender feet roam the forest, Your charms disturb the mind. The shortest moment apart from You seems as long as a cosmic age. The nectar from Your lips is the thief who steals our hearts.**

### Text 280

*sarva-tyāgārthita-gate*  
*mahā-mohana-rūpa he*  
*vraja-maṅgala-kṛd-vyakte*  
*sva-jana-prārthya-pūraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sarva*—everything; *tyāga*—by giving up; *arthita*—hoped for; *gate*—O destination; *mahā-mohana*—supremely enchanting; *rūpa*—whose form; *he*—O; *vraja*—of Vraja; *maṅgala-kṛt*—for creating the good fortune; *vyakte*—who appeared; *sva-jana*—of Your devotees; *prārthya*—of the prayers; *pūraka*—O fulfiller.

**Перевод:**

**Hoping to attain You, people give up everything. Your beauty is supremely enchanting. You have appeared for the good fortune of Vraja, and You fulfill the sincere requests of Your devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Surrendered devotees who follow in the footsteps of the *gopīs* may renounce even their families and friends for the sake of achieving Kṛṣṇa. Such devotees are happy to make these sacrifices because Kṛṣṇa is all-attractive. Since Kṛṣṇa always responds when His devotees beg medicinal relief from the ills in their hearts, one risks no loss by unconditionally surrendering to Him.

### Text 281

*ati-komala-pādābja-*  
*kaṇṭakāraṇya-sañcara*  
*gopa-strī-jīvitākarṣi-*  
*durga-bhū-bhramaṇāva mām*  
*(namaḥ 68)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ati-komala*—very soft; *pāda-abja*—with Your lotus feet; *kaṇṭaka*—thorny; *araṇya*—in the forest; *sañcara*—O wanderer; *gopa-strī*—of the cowherd women; *jīvita*—the life airs; *ākarṣi*—which pinched; *durga-bhū*—in the impenetrable wilderness; *bhramaṇa*—whose rambling; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me; *namaḥ 68*—obeisance 68.

**Перевод:**

**Your wandering in the thorny forest with Your very soft lotus feet, rambling in that impenetrable wilderness, pinched the life airs of the cowherd women. O Kṛṣṇa, please protect me. (Obeisance 68)**

**Комментарий:**

Here Śrīla Sanātana summarizes the last of the prayers sung by the *gopīs* in separation from the Lord and prays to the Lord for protection.

*iti daśama-skandhe eka-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-first chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 32

### Text 282

*aty-ucca-gopikā-duḥkha-*  
*rodanonmathitendriya*  
*jaya gopī-punar-dṛṣṭa-*  
*smayamāna-mukhāmbuja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ati-ucca*—very loud; *gopikā*—of the *gopīs; duḥkha*—in distress; *rodana*—by the crying; *unmathita*—shaken; *indriya*—whose senses; *jaya*—victory to You; *gopī*—by the *gopīs; punaḥ*—again; *dṛṣṭa*—seen; *smayamāna*—smiling; *mukha-ambuja*—whose lotus face.

**Перевод:**

**The very loud cries of the sorrowful gopīs shook Your senses. Victory to You, who again showed the gopīs Your smiling lotus face.**

### Text 283

*śrīman-madana-gopāla*  
*pīta-kauśeya-vastra-dhṛk*  
*prīty-utphullākṣa-gopa-strī-*  
*veṣṭita prāṇa-dāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrīmat*—splendid; *madana-gopāla*—O Madana-gopāla; *pīta*—yellow; *kauśeya*—silk; *vastra*—clothes; *dhṛk*—who wore; *prīti*—with love; *utphulla*—blossomed; *akṣa*—their eyes; *gopa-strī*—by the *gopīs; veṣṭita*—who were surrounded; *prāṇa*—of their very lives; *dāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**O splendid Madana-gopāla, dressed in yellow silk, surrounded by gopīs, their eyes blossoming with love, You gave back to those women their very lives.**

### Text 284

*ballavī-stana-saktāṅghri*  
*gopī-netrābja-ṣaṭpada*  
*gopa-strī-virahārti-ghna*  
*ballavī-kāma-pūraka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ballavī*—of the *gopīs; stana*—on the breasts; *sakta*—placed; *aṅghri*—whose feet; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; netra-abja*—of the lotus eyes; *ṣaṭpada*—O bee; *gopa-strī*—of the *gopīs; viraha*—from separation; *ārti*—of the distress; *ghna*—O destroyer; *ballavī*—of the cowherd girls; *kāma*—of the desires; *pūraka*—O fulfiller.

**Перевод:**

**You placed Your feet on the gopīs’ breasts. O bee buzzing round the gopīs’ lotus eyes! You killed the sorrow the gopīs felt in separation from Your and fulfilled all their desires.**

**Комментарий:**

Among the principal *gopīs,* Padmā is particularly submissive to her lover Kṛṣṇa; it was she who allowed Him to hold His feet to her breasts. Śrī Rādhā, on the other hand, is much more self-willed in Her dealings with Kṛṣṇa, so He buzzed like a bee around Her eyes, not taking the liberty to touch Her. Both by touching and by glancing, Kṛṣṇa relieved the *gopīs’* hearts of all distress.

### Text 285

*gopī-celāñcalāsīna*  
*gopī-gaṇa-sabhājita*  
*jaya gopī-sado-jātā-*  
*dhika-śrī-rājamāna he*  
*(namaḥ 69)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; cela*—of the garments; *añcala*—on the hems; *āsīna*—who were seated; *gopī-gaṇa*—by the *gopīs; sabhājita*—who were honored; *jaya*—victory to You; *gopī-sadaḥ*—in the assembly of the *gopīs; jāta*—developed; *adhika*—even more; *śrī*—with splendor; *rājamāna*—who were shining; *he*—O; *namaḥ 69*—obeisance 69.

**Перевод:**

**As You sat on the edge of the gopīs’ veils, the gopīs honored You. Victory to You, who shone with unprecedented splendor in the assembly of gopīs! (Obeisance 69)**

### Text 286

*vidagdha-gopikāgāḍha-*  
*tri-praśnottara-dāyaka*  
*vijñāta-gopy-abhiprāya*  
*mahā-catura-siṁha he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vidagdha*—clever; *gopikā*—of the *gopīs; agāḍha*—enigmatic; *tri*—three; *praśna*—of the questions; *uttara-dāyaka*—O answerer; *vijñāta*—who understood; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; abhiprāya*—the inner intention; *mahā-catura*—among most resourceful heroes; *siṁha he*—O lion.

**Перевод:**

**O lion among resourceful heroes, You understood the inner intent of the gopīs as You answered their clever, enigmatic threefold question.**

**Комментарий:**

The *gopīs* tried to make Kṛṣṇa admit to being ungrateful by asking Him which of three alternatives was true: Did He love them, hate them, or not care one way or the other? In His answer Kṛṣṇa adroitly avoided choosing any of the three.

### Text 287

*sva-vāk-svāptākṛtajñatvā-*  
*di-doṣa-parihāraka*  
*nijāsādhāraṇa-prema-*  
*kāruṇya-sthāpakāva mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-vāk*—by Your answers; *sva-āpta*—ascribed to You; *akṛta-jñatva*—ingratitude; *ādi*—and so on; *doṣa*—of the faults; *parihāraka*—O refuter; *nija*—Your; *asādhāraṇa*—extraordinary; *prema*—of love; *kāruṇya*—and mercy; *sthāpaka*—O establisher; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**By Your answer You refuted ungratefulness and the other faults ascribed to You and established the truth of Your extraordinary love and mercy. Please protect me.**

**Комментарий:**

“By Your unique mercy,” Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī prays, “please protect me with the gift of *prema.*”

### Text 288

*svīya-saṅgāparityāgin*  
*sva-dānātṛpta-mānasa*  
*priyopakāra-saṁvyagra*  
*viraha-prema-vardhana*  
*(namaḥ 70)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*svīya*—of those dear to You; *saṅga*—the association; *aparityāgin*—who never abandon; *sva-dāna*—with giving Yourself away; *atṛpta*—not satisfied; *mānasa*—whose heart; *priya*—Your beloveds; *upakāra*—with benefiting; *saṁvyagra*—who are very much concerned; *viraha*—in separation; *prema*—of love; *vardhana*—O inflamer; *namaḥ 70*—obeisance 70.

**Перевод:**

**You never abandon those dear to You, and Your heart is never content with giving them only Yourself. You are obsessed with how to repay them, O inflamer of love in separation. (Obeisance 70)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe dvā-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-second chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

*gopī-viraha-santāpa-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; viraha*—of separation; *santāpa*—the burning pain; *hara*—which removed; *āliṅgana*—with the embraces; *kovida*—O expert; *rāsa-krīḍā*—of the *rāsa-līlā; rasa*—by the taste; *ākṛṣṭa*—who were attracted; *jaya*—victory to You; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; priyam-kara*—O delighter.

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa,Your expert embraces relieve the burning pain the gopīs feel in separation. O Lord attracted to the taste of the rāsa-līlā, O delight of the gopīs, victory to You!**

### Text 290

*rāsotsava-samārambhin*

*gopī-hema-maṇi-śreṇī-*

*rāsa-utsava*—of the *rāsa* festival; *samārambhin*—O initiator; *gopī-maṇḍala*—by circles of *gopīs; maṇḍita*—who were decorated; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; hema*—golden; *maṇi*—jeweled; *śreṇī*—in the chain; *madhya-madhya*—interspersed; *harit-maṇi*—O emerald.

**Surrounded by circles of gopīs, You commenced the rāsa festival. In the jeweled chain of golden gopīs, You were an emerald interspersed between each gopī and the next.**

*devatā-gaṇa-gītādi-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sva-sva*—each their own; *pārśva*—by the side; *sthiti*—of standing; *jñāna*—by their knowledge; *ānandita*—enraptured; *strī-gaṇa*—by the women; *āvṛta*—who were surrounded; *devatā-gaṇa*—of bands of demigods; *gīta-ādi*—with the songs and so on; *su-sevita*—who were well served; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—to You.

**Перевод:**

**The women who surrounded You were enraptured, each thinking You stood beside her alone, and bands of demigods served You well with song, music, and rains of flowers. I bow down to You.**

### Text 292

*gopikodgīta-su-prīta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopikā*—of the *gopīs; udgīta*—by the joyful music; *su-prīta*—who were very much pleased; *nṛtya*—in dancing; *gīta*—and singing; *vicakṣaṇa*—O expert; *sva-ātma*—Your own; *āsya*—from the mouth; *datta*—who gave; *tāmbūla*—betel nuts; *śrānta*—fatigued; *gopī*—by a *gopī; dhṛta*—held; *aṁsaka*—whose shoulder.

**O expert dancer and singer, You were very much pleased with the jubilant music of the gopīs. To one fatigued gopī You gave betel nuts from Your own mouth, and She held Your shoulder.**

**Комментарий:**

One *gopī* in particular, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, was growing tired from Her exuberant and most expert dancing and singing. So Kṛṣṇa gave Her extra support by letting Her put Her hand on His shoulder.

*sva-anurūpa*—exactly suiting You; *vraja-vadhū*—of the young girls of Vraja; *nṛtya*—by the dancing; *gīta*—singing; *ādi*—and so on; *harṣita*—who rejoiced; *vimohita*—bewildered; *śaśāṅka-ādi*—the moon and so on; *sthairya*—by making stand still; *rātri*—of the night; *ati-dairghya*—extreme length; *kṛt*—who made.

*vidagdha*—romantically expert; *ballavī-vṛnda*—by the many cowherd women; *rati*—of loving exchanges; *cihna*—with the signs; *aṅkita*—marked; *aṅga*—whose body; *he*—O; *rati*—from the exchanges of love; *śrānta*—fatigued; *vraja-vadhū*—of the Vraja maidens; *mukha*—the faces; *mārjana*—in wiping; *tat-para*—who were absorbed; *namaḥ 71*—obeisance 71.

**The cowherd maidens of Vraja, expert in romantic arts, marked Your body with signs of love. And when those maidens grew fatigued from exchanges of love, You took care to gently wipe their faces. (Obeisance 71)**

**Перевод:**

**O Kṛṣṇa, most skillful in water sports, I bow down to You. Encircled by bees hovering around Your flower garlands, You were splashed by a flock of laughing cowherd girls.**

**As You played in the Yamunā, bobbing up and down, Your body seemed lost in her waters. You then strolled along the Yamunā shore, eager to enjoy amorous sports in the Yamunā groves.**

**Victory to You, O Kṛṣṇa, attracted to Śrī Rādhikā, enjoying Candrāvalī, drinking the lotus face of Padmā like a bee, and caressed by the glance of Lalitā!**

**Пословный перевод:**

*viśākhā*—Viśākhā; *artha*—a benediction; *viśeṣa*—special; *arthin*—who wanted; *śyāmalā*—for Śyāmalā; *rati*—whose love; *nirmala*—pure; *bhadrā*—of Bhadrā; *bhadra*—gentle; *rasa*—by the loving mood; *adhīna*—who were controlled; *dhanyā*—of Dhanyā; *prāṇa*—of the life; *dhana-īśvara*—O lord of the wealth.

**Перевод:**

**You wanted a special blessing from Viśākhā, You were drawn to Śyāmalā in pure love, You came under the control of Bhadrā’s gentle mood, and You were the lord of the wealth of Dhanyā’s life.**

*gopa-janmāgata-sva-strī-*  
*nirantara-vilāsa-kṛt*  
*gopī-lampaṭa he gopī-*  
*stana-kuṅkuma-maṇḍita*  
*(namaḥ 72)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopa-janma*—birth as cowherds; *āgata*—who had obtained; *sva-strī*—with Your own consorts; *nirantara*—constant; *vilāsa-kṛt*—O enjoyer; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; lampaṭa he*—O uncontrolled pursuer; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; stana*—from the breasts; *kuṅkuma*—with the *kuṅkuma; maṇḍita*—who were decorated; *namaḥ 72*—obeisance 72.

**Перевод:**

**You endlessly enjoyed with Your own consorts, who had been born as the wives of cowherds. O brazen pursuer of the gopīs, You were decorated with kuṅkuma from the gopīs’ breasts. (Obeisance 72)**

### Text 300

*parīkṣit-pṛṣṭa-rāsārtha*  
*śukoktaiśvarya-sañcaya*  
*mumukṣu-mukta-bhaktārtha-*  
*sac-cid-ānanda-ceṣṭita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*parīkṣit*—by Parīkṣit; *pṛṣṭa*—inquired; *rāsa*—of whose *rāsa* dance; *artha*—about the meaning; *śuka*—by Śukadeva; *ukta*—spoken; *aiśvarya-sañcaya*—about whose many divine opulences; *mumukṣu*—of those aspiring for liberation; *mukta*—of the liberated; *bhakta*—and of the devotees; *artha*—for the sake; *sat*—of eternity; *cit*—knowledge; *ānanda*—and bliss; *ceṣṭita*—whose activities.

**Перевод:**

**When Parīkṣit asked about the meaning of Your rāsa dance, Śukadeva explained Your divine opulences. Your eternal, blissful pastimes, full of knowledge, are for the benefit of all—aspirants for liberation, the liberated, and Your pure devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

After hearing Śrīla Śukadeva’s description of the *rāsa-līlā,* Parīkṣit Mahārāja noticed that some of the assembled sages—the logicians and ritualists—were looking at one another with doubtful smiles. To correct the misunderstanding of these non-Vaiṣṇavas, Parīkṣit asked Śukadeva to clarify the significance of this most confidential of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes.

### Text 301

*gopī-mahā-mahima-da*  
*gopāsūyādy-anāspada*  
*gopārpita-gṛhāpatya-*  
*patnī-prāṇa prasīda me*  
*(namaḥ 73)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—upon the *gopīs; mahā-mahima*—of the greatest glory; *da*—O bestower; *gopa*—by the cowherd men; *asūyā-ādi*—of envy and so on; *anāspada*—who were not a recipient; *gopa*—by the cowherds; *arpita*—dedicated; *gṛha*—their homes; *apatya*—children; *patnī*—wives; *prāṇa*—and lives; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *me*—with me; *namaḥ 73*—obeisance 73.

**Перевод:**

**You favored the gopīs with the highest glory. And the cowherd men harbored no envy toward You. Rather, they dedicated to You their homes, their children, their wives, and their very lives. Please be satisfied with me. (Obeisance 73)**

**Комментарий:**

All the *vraja-vāsīs* loved Kṛṣṇa unconditionally. Even the cowherd men whose wives danced with Kṛṣṇa at night had no bad feelings toward Him; Yogamāyā arranged for those men to remain unaware of the secret connection between Kṛṣṇa and their wives.

*iti daśama-skandhe trayas-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-third chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 34

### Text 304

*kāma-pāla*—with the fulfiller of desires; *saha*—together; *krīḍā*—by sporting; *sammānita*—who graced; *niśā-mukha*—the early night; *manohara*—attractive; *mahā-gīta*—by excellent songs; *mohita*—charmed; *strī-gaṇa*—by many women; *āvṛta*—who were surrounded.

**Перевод:**

**Sporting together with Balarāma, the fulfiller of everyone’s desires, You graced the early night, surrounded by women charmed by Your excellent attractive songs.**

### Text 305

*śaṅkhacūḍa-paritrasta-*  
*gopikākrośa-dhāvita*

*śaṅkhacūḍa-śiro-hara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śaṅkhacūḍa*—by Śaṅkhacūḍa; *paritrasta*—terrified; *gopikā*—of the *gopīs; ākrośa*—by the cries; *dhāvita*—who were moved to run; *strī*—the women; *rakṣā*—for guarding; *sthāpita*—who appointed; *bala*—Baladeva; *śaṅkhacūḍa*—of Śaṅkhacūḍa; *śiraḥ*—of the head; *hara*—O remover.

**Перевод:**

**When You heard the cries of the gopīs terrified by Śaṅkhacūḍa, You ran toward those women, had Baladeva guard them, and knocked off Śaṅkhacūḍa’s head.**

### Text 306

*śaṅkhacūḍa-śiro-ratna-*  
*prīṇitāgraja pāhi mām*  
*anyonya-gopī-sāpatnyā-*  
*nutpādaka namo ’stu te*  
*(namaḥ 75)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śaṅkhacūḍa*—of Śaṅkhacūḍa; *śiraḥ-ratna*—with the jewel from the head; *prīṇita*—who pleased; *agraja*—Your elder brother; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me; *anyonya*—among one other; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; sāpatnya*—jealous rivalry; *anutpādaka*—who did not create; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You; *namaḥ 75*—obeisance 75.

**Перевод:**

**And as for the jewel from that demon’s head, You gave it to Your elder brother. Thus You pleased Your brother and avoided creating jealous rivalry among the gopīs. Please protect me. I bow down to You. (Obeisance 75)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe catus-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-fourth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 35

### Text 307

*ahar-viraha-santapta-*  
*gopī-gīta-guṇodaya*  
*jaya śokābdhi-nistāra-*  
*prakārāty-ucca-kīrtana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ahaḥ*—during the day; *viraha*—by separation; *santapta*—burning; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; gīta*—by the songs; *guṇa*—of Your qualities; *udaya*—the revealing; *jaya*—victory to You; *śoka*—of sorrow; *abdhi*—from the ocean; *nistāra*—of deliverance; *prakāra*—the means; *ati-ucca*—very loud; *kīrtana*—whose glorification.

**Перевод:**

**The songs of the gopīs, who in the daytime burned in the fire of separation from You, revealed Your qualities. For those gopīs, reverberant praise of You was the means of deliverance from the ocean of sorrow. Victory to You!**

**Комментарий:**

The texts from 308 through the first half of 314 summarize the songs of the *gopīs.*

### Text 308

*sācī-kṛtānanāmbhoja*  
*vyatyasta-pada-pallava*  
*nartita-bhrū-yugāpāṅga*  
*veṇu-vādya-viśārada*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sācī-kṛta*—tilted; *ānana-ambhoja*—whose lotus face; *vyatyasta*—crossed; *pada-*p*allava*—whose budlike feet; *nartita*—made to dance; *bhrū-yuga*—whose pair of eyebrows; *apāṅga*—and the corners of whose eyes; *veṇu-vādya*—in playing the flute; *viśārada*—O expert.

**Перевод:**

**With Your lotus face tilted, and one budlike foot crossed over the other, and Your eyebrows and eye-corners dancing, You expertly play Your flute.**

### Text 309

*viśva-mohana-rūpaṁ tvāṁ*  
*siddha-strī-kāma-vardhanam*  
*vande citrāyitāśeṣa-*  
*vrajāraṇya-paśu-vrajam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*viśva*—the universe; *mohana*—enchanting; *rūpam*—whose form; *tvām*—to You; *siddha-strī*—in the wives of the Siddhas; *kāma*—of desire; *vardhanam*—O inciter; *vande*—I offer respects; *citrāyita*—standing still as in a painting; *aśeṣa*—all; *vraja-araṇya*—of the Vṛndāvana forest; *paśu-vrajam*—the many animals.

**Перевод:**

**I offer my respects to You, whose beautiful form enchants the whole universe. You arouse desire in the wives of the Siddhas. And You stun all the animals of the Vṛndāvana forest, leaving them standing like figures in a painting.**

### Text 310

*avāhita-pravāhaugha*  
*latādi-madhu-varṣaka*  
*sva-pārśvāpita-haṁsāde*  
*parjanya-cchatra-sevita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*avāhita*—not flowing; *pravāha*—whose current; *ogha*—the streams; *latā*—the creepers; *ādi*—and other plants; *madhu*—sweet sap; *varṣaka*—who make rain; *sva-pārśva*—to Your side; *āpita*—coming; *haṁsa-āde*—the swans and so on; *parjanya*—by the cloud; *chatra*—as an umbrella; *sevita*—who are served.

**Перевод:**

**You make the currents of the streams stop flowing and sweet sap rain from the trees and creepers. You make the swans and other waterfowl come to Your side, and the cloud serve You as an umbrella.**

### Text 311

*brahmādy-atarkya-saṅgīta*  
*kāmārpaka-samīkṣaṇa*  
*sva-padoddhṛta-bhū-tāpa*  
*vanitā-taru-bhāva-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brahma-ādi*—for Brahmā and other demigods; *atarkya*—inexplicable; *saṅgīta*—whose music; *kāma*—to Cupid; *arpaka*—which delivers persons; *samīkṣaṇa*—whose glances; *sva-pada*—by Your feet; *uddhṛta*—relieved; *bhū*—of the earth; *tāpa*—the distress; *vanitā*—for women; *taru*—of trees; *bhāva*—the condition; *kṛt*—who produce.

**Перевод:**

**Your flute-song is beyond the power of Brahmā and the other demigods to fathom. Your glances deliver to Cupid whomever You see. Your feet relieve the earth of her suffering. And You make young women stand just like trees.**

**Комментарий:**

While discussing the wonderful effects of Kṛṣṇa’s flute-song on all the inhabitants of Vraja, the *gopīs* themselves became immobile and almost devoid of external awareness, just like trees.

### Text 312

*hṛta-citta-mṛgī prāpta-*  
*dinānta-śrānti-kāntita*  
*yamunā-snāna-ramyāṅga*  
*sukha-vāyu-prapūjita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hṛta*—stolen; *citta*—having hearts; *mṛgī*—the she-deer; *prāpta*—obtained; *dina*—of the day; *anta*—at the end; *śrānti*—by the fatigue; *kāntita*—who were beautified; *yamunā*—in the Yamunā; *snāna*—by bathing; *ramya*—attractive; *aṅga*—whose body; *sukha*—pleasant; *vāyu*—by the wind; *prapūjita*—who are offered worship.

**Перевод:**

**You steal the hearts of the does. And at the end of the day You seem beautified by fatigue. Bathing in the Yamunā makes Your body very attractive, and You are worshiped by the pleasant wind.**

### Text 313

*brahmādi-vandyamānāṅghre*  
*suhṛd-ānanda-vardhana*  
*mada-cchurita-lolākṣa*  
*muditānana-paṅkaja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brahma-ādi*—by Brahmā and others; *vandyamāna*—honored; *aṅghre*—whose feet; *suhṛt*—of Your friends; *ānanda*—of the bliss; *vardhana*—O increaser; *mada*—with joy; *churita*—glowing; *lola*—restless; *akṣa*—whose eyes; *mudita*—happy; *ānana-paṅkaja*—whose lotus face.

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā and other demigods honor Your feet. O Lord who increase the bliss of Your friends, Your restless eyes glow with joy, and Your lotus face appears very happy.**

### Text 314

*vana-mālā-parītāṅga*  
*gajendra-gati-sundara*  
*gopikā-śrāvitotkarṣa*  
*hṛṣṭa-mātṛka pāhi mām*  
*(namaḥ 76)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vana-mālā*—in a garland of forest flowers; *parīta*—enveloped; *aṅga*—whose body; *gaja-indra*—like that of the king of elephants; *gati*—with Your gait; *sundara*—who are beautiful; *gopikā*—by the young *gopīs; śrāvita*—related; *utkarṣa*—whose excellence; *hṛṣṭa*—delighted; *mātṛka*—whose mother; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me; *namaḥ 76*—obeisance 76.

**Перевод:**

**Your body encircled by a garland of forest flowers, Your beautiful gait like that of the king of elephants, You delight Your mother, to whom the young gopīs told Your glories. Please protect me. (Obeisance 76)**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī wants the protection of always being able to envision Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes in his heart.

*iti daśama-skandhe pañca-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-fifth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 36

### Text 315

*ariṣṭa-trāsitāśeṣa-*  
*vrajāśvāsaka rakṣa mām*  
*sva-bhujāsphoṭanāhvāna*  
*vṛṣabhāsura-kopana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ariṣṭa*—by Ariṣṭa; *trāsita*—terrified; *aśeṣa-vraja*—of all of Vraja; *āśvāsaka*—O pacifier; *rakṣa*—please protect; *mām*—me; *sva*—Your; *bhuja*—hands; *āsphoṭana*—by slapping; *āhvāna*—whose calling; *vṛṣabha-asura*—the bull demon; *kopana*—who made angry.

**Перевод:**

**Save me, O Lord, who calmed the Vraja-vāsīs, all terrified by Ariṣṭa, the demonic bull. You slapped Your arms and called out to the bull, making him very angry.**

### Text 316

*utpāṭita-viṣāṇāgra-*  
*ghātitogra-vṛṣāsura*  
*gokulāriṣṭa-vidhvaṁsin*  
*ariṣṭāsura-bhañjana*  
*(namaḥ 77)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*utpāṭita*—pulled out; *viṣāṇa*—of his horn; *agra*—with the end; *ghātita*—who killed; *ugra*—terrible; *vṛṣa-asura*—the bull demon; *gokula*—of Gokula; *ariṣṭa*—of the calamity; *vidhvaṁsin*—O destroyer; *ariṣṭāsura*—of Ariṣṭāsura; *bhañjana*—O crusher; *namaḥ 77*—obeisance 77.

**Перевод:**

**You pulled out one of that bull demon’s horns and killed him with its sharp end. Thus You destroyed the calamity facing Gokula, O crusher of Ariṣṭāsura! (Obeisance 77)**

### Text 317

*nārada-jñāpitodanta-*  
*kaṁsa-durmantra-vardhana*  
*kaṁsa-samprārthitākrūra-*  
*purānayana pāhi mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nārada*—by Nārada; *jñāpita*—informed; *udanta*—whose news; *kaṁsa*—to Kaṁsa; *durmantra*—of the bad advice; *vardhana*—O increaser; *kaṁsa*—by Kaṁsa; *samprārthita*—requested; *akrūra*—Akrūra; *pura*—to the city; *ānayana*—who arranged to bring; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**By having Nārada tell Kaṁsa of Your exploits, You enhanced the influence of Kaṁsa’s bad advisers. Kaṁsa then called upon Akrūra to bring You to the city. Please protect me.**

**Комментарий:**

Recognizing that Kṛṣṇa’s Vṛndāvana pastimes were almost finished, Nārada felt responsible to help start Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes in Mathurā. He therefore agitated Kaṁsa’s mind with clever arguments, so that Kaṁsa became more intent than ever on killing Kṛṣṇa, Balarāma, and Their parents.

### Text 318

*duṣṭopāya-durudyoga-*  
*śatākulita-kaṁsa-rāṭ*  
*rājājñānanditākrūra*  
*jaya dāna-pati-priya*  
*(namaḥ 78)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*duṣṭa*—evil; *upāya*—of plans; *durudyoga*—of evil attempts; *śata*—by hundreds; *ākulita*—agitated; *kaṁsa-rāṭ*—King Kaṁsa; *rāja*—of the king; *ājñā*—by the order; *ānandita*—delighted; *akrūra*—Akrūra; *jaya*—victory to You; *dāna-pati*—to the master of charity; *priya*—who were dear; *namaḥ 78*—obeisance 78.

**Перевод:**

**Though King Kaṁsa was astir with hundreds of evil plans and endeavors, Akrūra was glad to receive the king’s order. Victory to You, O friend of Akrūra, the famous giver of charity. (Obeisance 78)**

**Комментарий:**

“All victory to You! May You use Your invincible strength to kill all the demons.”

*iti daśama-skandhe ṣaṭ-triṁśo ’ḍhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-sixth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 37

### Text 319

*jaya gokula-santrāsi-*  
*keśi-vikṣepaṇa prabho*  
*hayāsura-mahāsyāntaḥ-*  
*praveśita-mahā-bhuja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *gokula*—Gokula; *santrāsi*—terrorizing; *keśi*—of Keśī; *vikṣepaṇa*—O thrower; *prabho*—O Lord; *haya-asura*—of the horse demon; *mahā*—huge; *āsya*—the mouth; *antaḥ*—within; *praveśita*—who put; *mahā-bhuja*—Your powerful arm.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, O Lord! When Keśī, the horse demon, terrorized Gokula, You threw him far away. Then You thrust Your powerful arm inside his huge mouth.**

### Text 320

*helā-hata-mahā-daitya*  
*jaya keśi-nisūdana*  
*keśavaṁ keśi-mathanaṁ*  
*vande tvāṁ devatārcitam*  
*(namaḥ 79)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*helā*—effortlessly; *hata*—who killed; *mahā-daitya*—the great demon; *jaya*—victory to You; *keśi-nisūdana*—O slaughterer of Keśī; *keśavam*—to Keśava; *keśi-mathanam*—the crusher of Keśī; *vande*—I offer respect; *tvām*—to You; *devatā*—by the demigods; *arcitam*—who were worshiped; *namaḥ 79*—obeisance 79.

**Перевод:**

**You easily killed that great demon. O destroyer of Keśī, victory to You! O Keśava, O crusher of Keśī, I offer respect to You, whom the demigods worshiped. (Obeisance 79)**

**Комментарий:**

One explanation of the name Keśava is that Kṛṣṇa’s glossy hair (*keśa*) is very beautiful. Yet another is that He killed the demon Keśī. According to *Śrī Hari-vaṁśa* (2.24.65), after Kṛṣṇa defeated Keśī, Nārada said:

*yasmāt tvayā hataḥ keśī*  
*tasmān mac-chāsanaṁ śṛṇu*  
*keśavo nāma nāmnā tvaṁ*  
*khyāto loke bhaviṣyati*

**“Please hear my judgment: Because You killed Keśī, in the future You will be known throughout the world by the name Keśava.”**

### Text 321

*jaya bhāgavata-śreṣṭha-*  
*śrī-nārada-samīḍita*  
*aparicchinna-san-mūrte*  
*sarva-jīveśvareśvara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *bhāgavata*—of devotees; *śreṣṭha*—by the best; *śrī-nārada*—Śrī Nārada; *samīḍita*—who were worshiped properly; *aparicchinna*—having no limit; *sat*—transcendental; *mūrte*—whose form; *sarva-jīva*—of all the living entities; *īśvara*—of the lords; *īśvara*—O Lord.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, whom Śrī Nārada, the best of devotees, justly praised: Lord of the lords of all living beings, Your transcendental form has no limit.**

**Комментарий:**

The second half of this verse and the first half of the next summarize Nārada’s prayers. Kṛṣṇa’s purely spiritual body is incomparable and unlimited by any measure of time or space. He has absolute control over the demigods, who rule the conditioned souls in the material world, and He is the origin of Nārāyaṇa, the Lord of the liberated souls in the spiritual world.

### Text 322

*sṛṣṭi-sthity-anta-kṛn-māyā-*  
*guṇa-sṛk satya-vāñchita*  
*ṛṣi-vāk-smṛta-devārtha-*  
*kaṁsa-saṁharaṇādika*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sṛṣṭi*—of creation; *sthiti*—maintenance; *anta*—and destruction; *kṛt*—executing; *māyā*—of Your material energy; *guṇa*—the modes; *sṛk*—who send forth; *satya*—true; *vāñchita*—whose desires; *ṛṣi*—of the sage; *vāk*—by the words; *smṛta*—who remembered; *deva-artha*—for the demigods; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *saṁharaṇa*—the destruction; *ādika*—and so on.

**Перевод:**

**You send forth the modes of māyā, which carry out the creation, maintenance, and destruction of the world. Your desires always come true. The words of the sage Nārada reminded You that You had to destroy Kaṁsa and help the demigods in other ways.**

### Text 323

*nārada-jñāpitāśeṣa-*  
*kārya-svīkāra-kovida*  
*darśanotsava-saṁhṛṣṭa-*  
*śrī-nārada-namaskṛta*  
*(namaḥ 80)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nārada*—by Nārada; *jñāpita*—informed; *aśeṣa*—all; *kārya*—the obligations; *svī-kāra*—in accepting; *kovida*—O expert; *darśana*—of seeing; *utsava*—by the festival; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—filled with joy; *śrī-nārada*—by Śrī Nārada; *namaskṛta*—who were offered obeisances; *namaḥ 80*—obeisance 80.

**Перевод:**

**You expertly consented to all the obligations of which Nārada told You. And Nārada, filled with joy from the festival of seeing You, offered You his obeisances. (Obeisance 80)**

### Text 324

*he meṣāyita-gopāla-*  
*pālana-steya-vibhrama*  
*gopa-veśa-dhara-vyoma-*  
*caurya-nīta-suhṛd-gaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*he*—O; *meṣāyita*—assuming the role of sheep; *gopāla*—with cowherd boys; *pālana*—of guards; *steya*—and thieves; *vibhrama*—whose sport; *gopa*—of a cowherd boy; *veśa-dhara*—wearing the disguise; *vyoma*—by the demon Vyoma; *caurya*—in theft; *nīta*—taken away; *suhṛt-gaṇa*—whose friends.

**Перевод:**

**When You and Your friends played guards and thieves, with some cowherd boys acting as sheep, the demon Vyoma disguised himself as one of the boys, stole Your friends, and took them away.**

### Text 325

*duṣṭa-vyomāsura-grāhin*  
*jaya vyoma-nipātana*  
*maya-putra-guhāruddha-*  
*gopa-varga-vimokṣaka*  
*(namaḥ 81)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*duṣṭa*—wicked; *vyoma-asura*—of the demon Vyoma; *grāhin*—O seizer; *jaya*—victory to You; *vyoma*—of Vyoma; *nipātana*—O feller; *maya-putra*—by the son of Maya; *guha*—in a cave; *āruddha*—trapped; *gopa-varga*—of the many cowherd boys; *vimokṣaka*—O releaser; *namaḥ 81*—obeisance 81.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! You caught hold of wicked Vyomāsura, threw him to the ground and killed him, and released all the cowherd boys whom that son of Māyā had trapped in a cave. (Obeisance 81)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe sapta-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-seventh chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 38

### Text 326

*jaya dāna-pati-dhyāta-*  
*mahā-mahima-sañcaya*  
*sal-lakṣaṇārtha-sad-bhāgyā-*  
*krūra-sambhāvitekṣaṇa*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *dāna-pati*—by the master of charity; *dhyāta*—meditated on; *mahā-mahima*—whose supreme greatness; *sañcaya*—abundant; *sat*—auspicious; *lakṣaṇa*—the signs; *artha*—whose real wealth; *sat*—true; *bhāgya*—whose good fortune; *akrūra*—by Akrūra; *sambhāvita*—looked forward to; *īkṣaṇa*—whose sight.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! Akrūra, the great giver of charity, thought deeply about the many sides of Your supreme greatness. Truly fortunate, he knew his real wealth to be the good omens he saw, and eagerly looked forward to seeing You.**

**Комментарий:**

As Akrūra began his early-morning journey to Vṛndāvana, he noticed several omens that indicated a most auspicious day.

### Text 327

*pādābja-dhyāyakākrūra-*  
*lālasānanda-vardhana*  
*akrūra-ratha-samprāpta*  
*goṣṭha-go-dohanāgata*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pāda-abja*—on Your lotus feet; *dhyāyaka*—who meditated; *akrūra*—of Akrūra; *lālasa*—of the eagerness; *ānanda*—and ecstasy; *vardhana*—O expander; *akrūra*—of Akrūra; *ratha*—the chariot; *samprāpta*—reached; *goṣṭha*—to the village; *go*—the cows; *dohana*—for milking; *āgata*—returned.

**Перевод:**

**As Akrūra meditated on Your lotus feet, You expanded his eagerness and his ecstasy. His chariot reached Vṛndāvana just as the cows were returning to the village to be milked.**

### Text 328

*jaya dāna-patīkṣāpta-*  
*kṣiti-kautuka-kṛt-pada*  
*śvāphalki-luṭhanādhāna-*  
*pādāmbuja-rajo-vraja*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *dāna-pati*—of the master of charity; *īkṣā*-*āpta*—were obtained by the vision (seen); *kṣiti*—the ground; *kautuka*—delightful; *kṛt*—making; *pada*—whose footprints; *śvāphalki*—of the son of Śvaphalka; *luṭhana*—of the rolling about; *ādhāna*—the place; *pāda-ambuja*—of whose lotus feet; *rajaḥ*—the dust; *vraja*—abundant.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You whose footprints made the earth delightful. When Akrūra, the master of charity, the son of Śvaphalka, achieved the sight of those lotus footprints, he rolled about in their abundant dust.**

### Text 329

*jaya śvaphalka-tanaya-*  
*nayanānanda-vardhana*  
*rathāvaplāvitākrūra*  
*jayākrūrābhivandita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *śvaphalka-tanaya*—of the son of Śvaphalka; *nayana*—for the eyes; *ānanda*—of the ecstasy; *vardhana*—O augmenter; *ratha*—from his chariot; *avaplāvita*—who caused to jump off; *akrūra*—Akrūra; *jaya*—victory to You; *akrūra*—by Akrūra; *abhivandita*—who were offered full obeisances.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! You heightened the ecstasy of Akrūra’s eyes, and he jumped off his chariot and fully prostrated himself before You. Victory to You!**

### Text 330

*su-prīty-āliṅgitākrūra*  
*jaya praṇata-vatsala*  
*gāndinī-nandanāśeṣa-*  
*mano-vāñchita-pūraka*  
*(namaḥ 82)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*su-prīti*—with great affection; *āliṅgita*—who embraced; *akrūra*—Akrūra; *jaya*—victory to You; *praṇata*—of the surrendered; *vatsala*—O considerate friend; *gāndinī-nandana*—of the son of Gāndinī; *aśeṣa*—all; *manaḥ*—in his heart; *vāñchita*—of the desires; *pūraka*—O fulfiller; *namaḥ 82*—obeisance 82.

**Перевод:**

**With great affection You embraced Akrūra, the son of Gāndinī. Victory to You, O considerate friend of the surrendered! You satisfied all his heartfelt desires. (Obeisance 82)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa didn’t much welcome the cause of Akrūra’s visit, but still the Lord was glad to see this beloved devotee.

*iti daśama-skandhe aṣṭā-triṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the thirty-eighth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 39

### Text 331

*akrūra-varṇitāśeṣa-*  
*kaṁsa-durvṛtta-kopita*  
*devakī-vasudevādi-*  
*duḥkha-śravaṇa-duḥkhita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*akrūra*—by Akrūra; *varṇita*—described; *aśeṣa*—all; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *durvṛtta*—because of the misconduct; *kopita*—who became angry; *devakī-vasudeva-ādi*—of Devakī, Vasudeva, and others; *duḥkha*—about the miseries; *śravaṇa*—by hearing; *duḥkhita*—who were saddened.

**Перевод:**

**When Akrūra told of Kaṁsa’s evil deeds, You grew enraged. And when You heard the sorrows of Your family—Devakī, Vasudeva, and others—Your heart grew heavy.**

### Text 332

*yātrā-mantrita-gopeśa*

*prātar-madhupurī-yāna-*  
*śravaṇākula-gokula*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yātrā*—a trip; *mantrita*—proposed; *gopa-īśa*—to the king of the cowherds; *mathurā*—to Mathurā; *gamana*—for going; *unmukha*—who had made up Your mind; *prātaḥ*—in the morning; *madhupurī*—to Madhupurī; *yāna*—about whose trip; *śravaṇa*—by hearing; *ākula*—agitated; *gokula*—Gokula.

**Перевод:**

**Having made up Your mind to go to Mathurā, You proposed to the king of the cowherds the journey. The news that You were traveling to Madhupurī in the morning threw all of Gokula into turmoil.**

### Text 333

*yaśodā-hṛdayāśaṅkā-*  
*cintā-jvara-śata-prada*  
*śokābdhi-pātitāśeṣa-*  
*vraja-yoṣid-gaṇāva mām*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *hṛdaya*—the heart; *āśaṅkā*—with doubts; *cintā*—and anxieties; *jvara*—of fevers; *śata*—by the hundreds; *prada*—O giver; *śoka-abdhi*—in an ocean of grief; *pātita*—who made fall; *aśeṣa*—all; *vraja*—of Vraja; *yoṣit-gaṇa*—the womenfolk; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me.

**Перевод:**

**You filled Yaśodā’s heart with hundreds of feverish doubts and anxieties and plunged all the women of Vraja into an ocean of grief. Please protect me.**

*śūnyāyamāna-jagatī-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śūnyāyamāna*—seeing as empty; *jagatī*—the world; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; jīvana*—of the lives; *tāpana*—the blazing fire; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; rodana*—of the crying; *vāḥ*—of water; *dhārā*—from the flood; *saṁvardhita*—who made overflow; *nadī-gaṇa*—the rivers; *namaḥ 83*—obeisance 83.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs saw the whole world empty, their lives a blazing fire of misery. You made the rivers overflow with the flood of their tears. (Obeisance 83)**

### Text 335

*jayākrūra-rathārūḍha*  
*gopī-rodana-kātara*  
*śakaṭārūḍha-nandādi-*  
*gopāla-gaṇa-veṣṭita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *akrūra*—of Akrūra; *ratha*—the chariot; *ārūḍha*—who mounted; *gopī*—of the *gopīs; rodana*—by the crying; *kātara*—who were distressed; *śakaṭa*—on their carts; *ārūḍha*—climbed; *nanda-ādi*—by Nanda and the others; *gopāla-gaṇa*—the cowherds; *veṣṭita*—who were surrounded.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You! Full of sorrow for the crying gopīs, You mounted Akrūra’s chariot, surrounded by Nanda and other cowherds, who sat upon their carts.**

**Комментарий:**

Some of the older cowherds, led by Nanda Mahārāja, and some of Kṛṣṇa’s friends, led by Śrīdāmā, joined Kṛṣṇa on the chariot.

### Text 336

*gopī-viyoga-santapta*  
*rādhikā-virahāsaha*  
*sva-dūta-prema-miṣṭokti-*  
*gopikāśvāsanākula*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—from the *gopīs; viyoga*—by being taken away; *santapta*—who were pained; *rādhikā*—from Śrī Rādhikā; *viraha*—the separation; *asaha*—who were unable to tolerate; *sva-dūta*—by Your messenger; *prema*—loving; *miṣṭa*—sweet; *ukti*—with words; *gopikā*—the *gopīs; āśvāsana*—to console; *ākula*—who were anxious.

**Перевод:**

**Pained at being taken from the gopīs and unable to bear being parted from Śrī Rādhikā, You were anxious to console them with sweet loving words from Your messenger.**

### Text 337

*gopī-hā-hā-mahā-rāva-*

*mṛta-prāya-vraja-vadhū-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; hā-hā*—alas, alas; *mahā-rāva*—due to the loud cries; *rodana*—sobbing; *ārti*—and torment; *nivartita*—stopped; *mṛta-prāya*—almost dead; *vraja-vadhū*—to the girls of Vraja; *cumbana*—by kisses; *āliṅgana*—and embraces; *asu*—of life; *da*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs’ tormented sobs and loud cries of “Alas! Alas!” prevented You from leaving. You restored life to the nearly dead girls of Vraja by kissing and embracing them.**

### Text 338

*prasīda sāntvanābhijña*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—please be satisfied; *sāntvana*—in consoling; *abhijña*—O expert; *nānā*—various; *śapatha*—of promises; *kāraka*—O maker; *kṛta*—who made; *avadhi-dinaḥ*—a date of return; *jīyāḥ*—may You be victorious; *āśā*—of hope; *prāṇa*—and life; *pradāyaka*—O bestower; *namaḥ 84*—obeisance 84.

**Перевод:**

**Please be satisfied with me, O expert in the art of giving solace, maker of so many promises, giver of the date for Your return. O bestower of hope and life, be victorious! (Obeisance 84)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa promised the *gopīs,* “Rest assured that as soon as I kill Kaṁsa and a few other demons I will quickly come back to you. Please believe Me and regain your peace of mind.”

### Text 339

*śvāphalki-sañcālita-yāna-vāhaṁ*

**Перевод:**

**As the son of Śvaphalka started the horses pulling Your chariot, Your path was blocked by the cowherd women, pained by Your mother’s terrible crying. Yet Nanda and other men silently held You firm. I bow down to You.**

**Перевод:**

**You made some women die, others faint, one group go mad, and thousands shed tears.**

*mahā-ārta*—of terrible distress; *svara*—with sounds; *sambhagna*—shattered; *kaṇṭhī*—their throats; *kṛta*—who made; *vadhū-śata*—hundreds of women; *prasīda*—please be merciful; *ratha*—of the chariot; *mārga*—on the road; *aṅka*—in the spot; *pātita*—who made fall down; *eka*—one; *abalā-gaṇa*—group of women.

*jayāśā-tantu-baddhāsu-*

**Перевод:**

**You tied the life airs of some women to a thread of hope—the chanting of Your glories. Because of You, ten thousand women stared anxiously down the road to Mathurā. Victory to You! (Obeisance 85)**

*śvāphalki-jala-sandṛṣṭa*

*yamunā*—in the Yamunā; *majjita*—immersed; *akrūra*—Akrūra; *jaya*—victory to You; *akrūra*—of Akrūra; *ratha*—on the chariot; *sthita*—who remained; *śvāphalki*—by the son of Śvaphalka (Akrūra); *jala*—in the water; *sandṛṣṭa*—who were seen; *parama*—supreme; *āścarya*—of wonders; *darśaka*—O shower.

**Перевод:**

**As Akrūra, son of Śvaphalka, took his bath in the Yamunā, You stayed on his chariot. Yet he saw You within the water, where You showed him astounding wonders. Victory to You!**

**Thus ends the thirty-ninth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 40

### Text 344

*akrūra-saṁstutānāde*  
*padmanābhādi-kāraṇa*  
*jagad-durvijñeya-gate*  
*bhajamānaika-gamya he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*akrūra*—by Akrūra; *saṁstuta*—who were praised; *anāde*—O beginningless one; *padmanābha*—of Lord Padmanābha (Brahmā); *ādi-kāraṇa*—O first cause; *jagat*—for the world; *durvijñeya*—incomprehensible; *gate*—whose identity; *bhajamāna*—by those who worship; *eka*—only; *gamya*—who are understandable; *he*—O.

**Перевод:**

**Akrūra prayed to You: O beginningless one, first cause of Padmanābha Brahmā, Your identity mystifies the created world, for only those who worship You can understand You.**

### Text 345

*nānā-yajñārcanīyāṅghre*  
*nānākhyā-rūpa-mārga-bhāk*  
*sarva-gatyāpagāmbhodhe*  
*sarva-deva-mayeśvara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—various; *yajña*—by sacrifices; *arcanīya*—to be worshiped; *aṅghre*—whose feet; *nānā*—various; *ākhyā*—names; *rūpa*—forms; *mārga*—and paths; *bhāk*—who assume; *sarva*—all; *gati*—from directions; *āpagā*—of the rivers; *ambhodhe*—O ocean; *sarva*—all; *deva*—the demigods; *maya*—who comprise; *īśvara*—O Supreme Lord.

**Перевод:**

**Many kinds of sacrifice worship Your feet. You have many names and forms, and many paths approach You. O great ocean, with rivers flowing into You from all directions, You are the Supreme Lord, in whom all demigods reside.**

**Комментарий:**

After all the controlling demigods of the material world are born from the universal form of Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa as the Paramātmā enters each of them to supervise their activities.

### Text 346

*jagad-āśraya-sarvāṅga*  
*brahmāṇḍāli-guhodara*  
*śoka-ghnānanda-da śrīmad-*  
*avatārāvalī-yaśaḥ*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jagat*—of the universe; *āśraya*—the foundation; *sarva*—all; *aṅga*—whose limbs; *brahmāṇḍa*—for universes; *āli*—many; *guhā*—a hiding place; *udara*—whose abdomen; *śoka*—of sorrow; *ghna*—O destroyer; *ānanda*—of bliss; *da*—O bestower; *śrīmat*—opulent; *avatāra-āvalī*—by many incarnations; *yaśaḥ*—whose fame.

**Перевод:**

**All the limbs of Your body form the foundation of the universe. Myriads of universes hide within Your abdomen. By Your many opulent incarnations You destroy grief, bestow bliss, and expand Your fame.**

### Text 347

*nānā-kārpaṇya-vijñāpi-*  
*mumukṣv-akrūra-yācita*  
*sva-prema-bhakti-sat-saṅga-*  
*dāyi-svaika-kṛpā-bhara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—various; *kārpaṇya*—his wretchedness; *vijñāpi*—describing; *mumukṣu*—desiring liberation; *akrūra*—by Akrūra; *yācita*—who were entreated; *sva*—Your; *prema-bhakti*—loving devotion; *sat-saṅga*—and the association of saintly devotees; *dāyi*—which bestows; *sva*—Your; *eka*—only; *kṛpā*—of mercy; *bhara*—the fullness.

**Перевод:**

**In various ways Akrūra told of his own wretchedness and prayed to You with the hope of liberation. But when You bestow Your greatest mercy, You simply give Your prema-bhakti and the company of saintly devotees.**

### Text 348

*gopy-avajñāhatākrūra-*  
*śuṣka-stotrābhivandita*  
*pitṛvya-vismayodanta-*  
*pracchakādbhuta-sāgara*  
*(namaḥ 86)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī*—of the *gopīs; avajñā*—by his neglect; *āhata*—spoiled; *akrūra*—of Akrūra; *śuṣka*—dry; *stotra*—by the prayers; *abhivandita*—who were worshiped; *pitṛvya*—from Your paternal uncle (Akrūra); *vismaya*—of the amazement; *udanta*—an explanation; *pracchaka*—who asked for; *adbhuta*—of wonders; *sāgara*—O ocean; *namaḥ 86*—obeisance 86.

**Перевод:**

**Your uncle Akrūra, spoiled by his neglect of the gopīs, worshiped You with dry prayers. And You asked him to explain why he felt so surprised, O sea of wonders. (Obeisance 86)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the fortieth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 41

### Text 349

*mathuropavana-prāpta-*  
*nandādi-sva-janāvṛta*  
*vrajārti-kāraṇākrūra-*  
*gṛha-yānārthanā-kara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mathurā*—of Mathurā; *upavana*—at a garden; *prāpta*—who arrived; *nanda-ādi*—by Nanda and the others; *sva-jana*—Your relatives; *āvṛta*—who were surrounded; *vraja*—of the Vraja-vāsīs; *ārti*—of the distress; *kāraṇa*—the cause; *akrūra*—by Akrūra; *gṛha*—to his house; *yāna*—for coming; *arthanā*—the request; *kara*—who caused.

**Перевод:**

**When You arrived at a garden on the outskirts of Mathurā, Nanda and Your other relatives surrounded You. And Akrūra, the cause of the Vraja-vāsīs’ anguish, asked You to come to his home.**

### Text 350

*svalaṅkṛta-mahāścarya-*

*pura-strī-vṛnda-nayana-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*su-alaṅkṛta*—well decorated; *mahā-āścarya*—very wonderful; *purī*—of the city; *darśana*—by the sight; *harṣita*—who were delighted; *pura*—of the city; *strī-vṛnda*—of all the women; *nayana*—the eyes; *manaḥ*—and minds; *hara*—who attracted; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You.

**Перевод:**

**I bow down to You, who were enlivened by the sight of the well-decorated, very wonderful city, the eyes and minds of all its women attracted to You.**

*dadhy-ādi-maṅgala-dravya-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dadhi*—yogurt; *ādi*—and so on; *maṅgala*—auspicious; *dravya*—with substances; *dvija*—by the *brāhmaṇas; ati-kṛta*—done too much; *pūjana*—whose worship; *pura-strī*—by the city women; *kṛta*—made; *gopa-strī*—of the cowherd women; *puṇya*—of the good fortune; *ślāgha*—by the praise; *ati-nirvṛta*—who were delighted; *namaḥ 87*—obeisance 87.

**While the brāhmaṇas were carried away with worshiping You with yogurt and other auspicious things, You delighted in hearing the city women praise the good fortune of the cowherd women. (Obeisance 87)**

*mathurā-jana-saṁvīkṣya*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mathurā-jana*—of the people of Mathurā; *saṁvīkṣya*—O center of attention; *rajaka*—from the washerman; *aṁśuka*—clothes; *yācaka*—who begged; *durmukha*—of the foulmouthed; *ākṣepa*—by the abusive response; *saṅkruddha*—who were angered; *raṅga-kāra*—of the dyer; *śiraḥ*—of the head; *hara*—O remover.

**You became the center of attention for the people of Mathurā. When You asked a cloth-dyer for some clothes and grew angry at his abusive response, You severed his head.**

*nija-priya*—Your favorite; *ambara*—of clothing; *dvandva*—a pair; *paridhāna*—by putting on; *vibhūṣita*—who became adorned; *abhīṣṭa*—desirable; *vastra*—by the clothes; *saṁhṛṣṭa*—who were delighted; *rāma*—Balarāma; *gopa-āli*—by the group of cowherd boys; *saṁyuta*—joined.

*prasīda vāyakonnīta-*

*prasīda*—please be satisfied; *vāyaka*—by a tailor; *unnīta*—presented; *caileya—*made of cloth; *ākalpa*—with ornaments; *bhūṣita*—who were decorated; *nānā-lakṣaṇa*—in a varied style; *veśa*—with the attire; *āḍhya*—splendid; *he*—O; *vāyaka*—to the tailor; *vara*—a benediction; *prada*—who gave; *namaḥ 88*—obeisance 88.
