**Please be satisfied with me. You decorated Yourself with cloth ornaments presented by a tailor. Then, splendidly dressed in a distinctive style, You offered him Your benediction. (Obeisance 88)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa rewarded the foulmouthed dyer by cutting off his head and blessed the tailor, who had put aside all thought of being Kaṁsa’s servant, by promising him a body just like His own in the spiritual world.

### Text 355

*prasīda he sudāmākhya-*  
*mālā-kāra-gṛhāgata*  
*mālika-prīti-pūjāpta*  
*mālya-vad bhakti-saṁstuta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—please be satisfied; *he*—O; *sudāma-ākhya*—named Sudāmā; *mālā-kāra*—of the garland-maker; *gṛha*—to the house; *āgata*—who came; *mālika*—of the florist; *prīti*—with love; *pūjā*—the worship; *āpta*—who received; *mālya-vat*—who wore garlands; *bhakti*—with devotion; *saṁstuta*—who were praised.

**Перевод:**

**Please be satisfied with me. You went to the house of the garland-maker named Sudāmā and accepted his loving worship by putting on his garlands. With great devotion he offered You suitable prayers.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīla Sanātana is saying, “If you are pleased with me, then show Your satisfaction by also gracing my home with Your presence.”

### Text 356

*sugandhi-nānā-mālāli-*  
*svalaṅkṛta namo ’stu te*  
*sudāmābhīpsita-vara-*  
*vāñchātīta-vara-prada*  
*(namaḥ 89)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*su-gandhi*—fragrant; *nānā*—various; *mālā-āli*—with many garlands; *su-alaṅkṛta*—who were beautifully decorated; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You; *sudāma*—by Sudāmā; *abhīpsita*—requested; *vara*—with the boons; *vāñchā-atīta*—beyond his desires; *vara-prada*—O benedictor; *namaḥ 89*—obeisance 89.

**Перевод:**

**I bow down to You, who were decorated with many fragrant garlands. You blessed Sudāmā with the boons he asked, and more, beyond his desires. (Obeisance 89)**

**Комментарий:**

Sudāmā asked for unshakable devotion to Kṛṣṇa, for friendship with His devotees, and for compassion for all living beings, but Kṛṣṇa gave him these boons and more—family prosperity, strength, long life, and effulgent good health.

*iti daśama-skandhe eka-catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the forty-first chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 42

### Text 357

*sa-hāsa-narma-sampraśnā-*  
*rthita-kubjānulepana*  
*kubjā-dattāṅga-rāgāḍhya*  
*sairindhrī-citta-mohana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sa-hāsa*—with a smile; *narma*—and joking words; *sampraśna-arthita*—who requested; *kubjā*—from Kubjā; *anulepana*—ointment; *kubjā*—by Kubjā; *datta*—given; *aṅga-rāga*—by the cosmetics; *āḍhya*—who were adorned; *sairindhrī*—of the maidservant (Kubjā); *citta*—of the heart; *mohana*—O enchanter.

**Перевод:**

**With a smile and joking words, You asked the maidservant Kubjā to give You some ointment. And adorned with the cosmetics she offered, You enchanted her heart.**

### Text 358

*kubjānulipta-sarvāṅga*  
*he ’ṅga-rāgānurañjita*  
*tri-vakrā-vakratā-hartaḥ*  
*kubjā-saundarya-dāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kubjā*—by Kubjā; *anulipta*—anointed; *sarva-aṅga*—whose whole body; *he*—O; *aṅga-rāga*—with cosmetics; *anurañjita*—who were shining; *tri-vakrā*—triple-bent Kubjā; *vakratā*—from her crookedness; *hartaḥ*—who freed; *kubjā*—to Kubjā; *saundarya*—of beauty; *dāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**Anointed by Kubjā, Your whole body shone with colorful ointments. She was bent in three places, but You freed her from her crooked state and made her beautiful.**

**Комментарий:**

Asked for some ointments, Kubjā took the liberty to apply her colorful creams all over Kṛṣṇa’s body and make artistic designs on His cheeks, chest, and arms.

### Text 359

*kubjākṛṣṭāmbara-dhara*  
*kubjā-ceṣṭāti-hāsita*  
*kṛta-kubjā-samāśvāsa*  
*jaya kubjā-vara-prada*  
*(namaḥ 90)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kubjā*—by Kubjā; *ākṛṣṭa*—pulled; *ambara*—Your garment; *dhara*—who wore; *kubjā*—of Kubjā; *ceṣṭā*—by the behavior; *ati-hāsita*—who were made to laugh profusely; *kṛta*—who made; *kubjā*—to Kubjā; *samāśvāsa*—assurance; *jaya*—victory to You; *kubjā*—of Kubjā; *vara-prada*—O giver of benedictions; *namaḥ 90*—obeisance 90.

**Перевод:**

**Kubjā tugged on the garment You wore and made You laugh profusely at the way she behaved. You then gave her all assurances. Victory to You, who gave blessings to Kubjā! (Obeisance 90)**

**Комментарий:**

Kṛṣṇa promised Kubjā He would visit her house very soon.

### Text 360

*nānopāyana-tāmbūla-*  
*gandhādi-vaṇig-arcita*  
*jaya citrāyitāśeṣa-*  
*pura-strī-gaṇa-vīkṣaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—various; *upāyana*—with articles of worship; *tāmbūla*—betel nuts; *gandha*—fragrances; *ādi*—and so on; *vaṇik*—by the merchants; *arcita*—who were worshiped; *jaya*—victory to You; *citrāyita*—made to stand motionless as in a painting; *aśeṣa*—all; *pura*—of the city; *strī-gaṇa*—upon the women; *vīkṣaka*—O glancer.

**Перевод:**

**The merchants worshiped You with various offerings—betel nut, fragrances, and more. Victory to You, whose glance rendered the city women still, like figures in a painting!**

### Text 361

*jaya praphulla-nayana*  
*līlā-hasita-locana*  
*matta-nāgendra-gamana*  
*nāgarī-gaṇa-mohana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *praphulla*—fully blossomed; *nayana*—whose eyes; *līlā*—playfully; *hasita*—smiling; *locana*—whose eyes; *matta*—intoxicated; *nāga-indra*—of a royal elephant; *gamana*—whose gait; *nāgarī-gaṇa*—of the romantic city women; *mohana*—O enchanter.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, whose playfully smiling eyes appeared like fully blossomed lotuses. Moving like an intoxicated royal elephant, You charmed all the romantic city women.**

### Text 362

*dhanuḥ-sthāna-praśna-kara*  
*jayādbhuta-dhanur-dhara*  
*līlā-sajjī-kṛteṣvāsa*  
*kaṁsa-kodaṇḍa-khaṇḍana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dhanuḥ*—of the bow; *sthāna*—about the location; *praśna-kara*—who inquired; *jaya*—victory to You; *adbhuta*—amazing; *dhanuḥ*—of the bow; *dhara*—O lifter; *līlā*—playfully; *sajjī-*k*ṛta*—who strung; *iṣu-āsa*—the bow; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *kodaṇḍa*—of the bow; *khaṇḍana*—O breaker.

**Перевод:**

**You asked where the bow was. Victory to You, O lifter of Kaṁsa’s amazing bow! You playfully strung it and broke it to pieces.**

### Text 363

*dhanū-rakṣaka-vṛnda-ghna*  
*kaṁsa-preṣita-sainya-han*  
*kaṁsāti-trāsa-janaka*  
*śakaṭāvāsa-saṅgata*  
*(namaḥ 91)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dhanuḥ*—of the bow; *rakṣaka-vṛnda*—of all the guards; *ghna*—O killer; *kaṁsa*—by Kaṁsa; *preṣita*—sent; *sainya*—of the soldiers; *han*—O killer; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *ati-trāsa*—of the extreme terror; *janaka*—O generator; *śakaṭa*—of carts; *āvāsa*—at the camp; *saṅgata*—who were rejoined; *namaḥ 91*—obeisance 91.

**Перевод:**

**You killed the whole contingent guarding the bow, and You killed the soldiers sent by Kaṁsa. And after thus terrifying that demon, You rejoined Your companions at the wagon camp. (Obeisance 91)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe dvi-catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the forty-second chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 43

### Text 364

*kaṁsa-kārita-mañcaugha*  
*raṅga-bhū-gamanotsuka*  
*jīyāt kuvalayāpīḍa-*  
*gaja-ruddha-patho bhavān*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kaṁsa*—Kaṁsa; *kārita*—who caused to make; *mañca-ogha*—many stages; *raṅga-bhū*—to the arena; *gamana*—in coming; *utsuka*—who were enthusiastic; *jīyāt*—may You be victorious; *kuvalayāpīḍa-gaja*—by the elephant Kuvalayāpīḍa; *ruddha*—obstructed; *pathaḥ*—whose path; *bhavān*—You.

**Перевод:**

**As You briskly approached the arena and its many raised platforms Kaṁsa had erected for Your sake, the elephant Kuvalayāpīḍa obstructed Your path. May You be victorious.**

### Text 365

*saṅkruddhāmbaṣṭha-nirdiṣṭa*  
*karīndra-krīḍitāva mām*  
*sadyaḥ kuvalayāpīḍa-*  
*ghātin siṁha-parākrama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*saṅkruddha*—angry; *ambaṣṭha*—by the elephant-keeper; *nirdiṣṭa*—who were rebuked; *kari-indra*—with the lordly elephant; *krīḍita*—who played; *ava*—please protect; *mām*—me; *sadyaḥ*—quickly; *kuvalayāpīḍa*—Kuvalayāpīḍa; *ghātin*—who killed; *siṁha*—like a lion; *parākrama*—whose valor.

**Перевод:**

**Rebuked by the angry elephant-keeper, You then played with the lordly elephant. O lionlike hero, You quickly killed Kuvalayāpīḍa. Please protect me.**

**Комментарий:**

“Please protect me from the maddened elephant of my mind.”

### Text 366

*samutpāṭita-nāgendra-*  
*mahā-danta-varāyudham*  
*vande kuvalayāpīḍa-*  
*mardanaṁ hata-hasti-pam*  
*(namaḥ 92)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*samutpāṭita*—extracted; *nāga-indra*—of the king of elephants; *mahā-danta*—the great tusk; *vara*—fine; *āyudham*—whose weapon; *vande*—I offer respects; *kuvalayāpīḍa*—of Kuvalayāpīḍa; *mardanam*—to the crusher; *hata*—who killed; *hasti-pam*—the elephant-keepers; *namaḥ 92*—obeisance 92.

**Перевод:**

**O crusher of Kuvalayāpīḍa, I offer my respects to You. With the fine weapon of the great torn-out tusk of that king of elephants, You slew all his keepers as well. (Obeisance 92)**

### Text 367

*raṅga-praveśa-subhaga-*  
*vīra-śrī-paribhūṣita*  
*skandha-nyasta-mahā-danta*  
*mada-rakta-kaṇāṅkita*

**Пословный перевод:**

*raṅga*—the arena; *praveśa*—for entering; *subhaga*—glorious; *vīra*—of a hero; *śrī*—with the splendor; *paribhūṣita*—who were decorated; *skandha*—on whose shoulder; *nyasta*—placed; *mahā-danta*—the mighty tusk; *mada*—of the liquid exuded from the mad elephant’s temples; *rakta*—and of his blood; *kaṇa*—with drops; *aṅkita*—who were marked.

**Перевод:**

**As You entered the arena, You were decorated with the glorious splendor of a hero. On Your shoulder rested the mighty tusk, and You were sprinkled with drops of the mad elephant’s blood and the liquid exuded from its forehead.**

### Text 368

*prasīda sveda-kaṇikā-*  
*laṅkṛtānana-paṅkaja*  
*raṅga-stha-lokābhiprāya-*  
*bhātāśeṣa-rasātmaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—please be satisfied; *sveda*—of perspiration; *kaṇikā*—by drops; *alaṅkṛta*—ornamented; *ānana-paṅkaja*—whose lotus face; *raṅga*—in the arena; *stha*—situated; *loka*—of the people; *abhiprāya*—according to the appreciations; *bhāta*—manifested; *aśeṣa*—all; *rasa*—spiritual tastes; *ātmaka*—who embodied.

**Перевод:**

**Please be satisfied with me. Your lotus face was ornamented with drops of perspiration. Because You embody all different rasas, the people in the arena saw You according to their different states of mind.**

### Text 369

*mahā-vīra mahā-ramya*  
*mahā-smara mahā-suhṛt*  
*maheśvara mahā-snigdha*  
*mahā-kāla mahā-guro*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mahā-vīra*—O supreme hero; *mahā-ramya*—O supreme attractor; *mahā-smara*—O supreme Cupid; *mahā-suhṛt*—O supreme friend; *mahā-īśvara*—O supreme controller; *mahā-snigdha*—O supreme object of parental affection; *mahā-kāla*—O supreme force of time; *mahā-guro*—O supreme spiritual master.

**Перевод:**

**O supreme hero! Supreme attractor! Supreme Cupid! Supreme friend! Supreme controller! Supreme darling of parental love! Supreme force of time! Supreme spiritual master!**

**Комментарий:**

As Kṛṣṇa entered the arena of competition, for the wrestlers He was the best of heroes. For the citizens of Mathurā who were not envious He was the most attractive reservoir of wonderful qualities and pastimes. For the women He was the supreme inciter of lust. For the cowherd boys, led by Śrīdāmā, Kṛṣṇa was the best of friends. For the corrupt kings, who knew that He intended to deal with each of them, He was the ultimate punisher. For His parents Devakī and Vasudeva, He was still their little child, the object of their love and concern. For Kaṁsa He was death personified, mighty time. And for the common people ignorant of philosophy, He was the supreme instructor.

### Text 370

*mahā-tattva mahā-sevya*  
*sarva-loka-manohara*  
*sa-premekṣaka-mañca-stha-*  
*loka-gīta-mahā-yaśaḥ*  
*(namaḥ 93)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mahā-tattva*—O supreme truth; *mahā-sevya*—O supreme object of service; *sarva-loka*—of all the worlds; *manohara*—O enchanter; *sa-prema*—with love; *īkṣaka*—who saw; *mañca-stha*—situated in the arena; *loka*—by the people; *gīta*—sung; *mahā-yaśaḥ*—whose supreme glories; *namaḥ 93*—obeisance 93.

**Перевод:**

**O supreme truth, supreme object of service, enchanter of all the worlds! All those in the arena who saw You with love chanted Your glories. (Obeisance 93)**

**Комментарий:**

For *yogīs, jñānīs,* and *bhaktas* like the four Kumāras, Kṛṣṇa was the Supreme Absolute Truth. For Vṛṣṇi nobles like Akrūra and Uddhava, He was the supreme worshipable Lord. In other words, each person in the arena saw Kṛṣṇa according to his own realization. In one way or another, He attracted everyone.

### Text 371

*cāṇūra-bhāṣitaṁ vande*  
*cāṇūrottara-dāyakam*  
*cāṇūrāti-parākrāntaṁ*  
*malla-yuddha-viśāradam*  
*(namaḥ 94)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*cāṇūra*—by Cāṇūra; *bhāṣitam*—who were addressed; *vande*—I offer respect; *cāṇūra*—to Cāṇūra; *uttara*—of the reply; *dāyakam*—to the giver; *cāṇūra*—by Cāṇūra; *ati-parākrāntam*—confronted with great strength; *malla-yuddha*—in wrestling; *viśāradam*—to the expert; *namaḥ 94*—obeisance 94.

**Перевод:**

**Cāṇūra spoke to You, and You gave him Your reply. Confronted by his great strength, You proved Yourself an excellent wrestler. I offer my respect to You. (Obeisance 94)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe tri-catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the forty-third chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

*sahaja-prema-mṛdula-*

*pura-strī-ninditāśeṣa-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sahaja*—natural; *prema*—with love; *mṛdula*—soft-hearted; *pura-strī-gaṇa*—by the many city women; *śocita*—who were lamented; *pura-strī*—by the city women; *nindita*—put to shame; *aśeṣa*—all; *sabhya*—the members of the assembly; *lajjā*—with shame; *ati-lajjita*—very much embarrassed.

**Перевод:**

**The women of the city, soft-hearted and filled with natural love for You, lamented Your plight. And You were very much embarrassed to hear those women put the whole assembly to shame with their reproaches.**

*strī-gaṇa*—by the ladies; *udgīta*—sung loudly; *mahima*—whose glories; *vraja-strī*—about the women of Vraja; *śruti*—by hearing; *harṣita*—who were pleased; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—of Your father and mother; *mahā-ārti*—of the great pain; *jña*—O knower; *jaya*—victory to You; *cāṇūra-mardana*—O crusher of Cāṇūra.

**Перевод:**

**As the ladies loudly sang Your glories, You were pleased to hear them praise the women of Vraja. You knew the great pain of Your father and mother. Victory to You, O crusher of Cāṇūra!**

*śala-toṣala-saṁhartar*

*śala-toṣala*—of Śala and Toṣala; *saṁhartaḥ*—O annihilator; *bala*—by Balarāma; *ghātita*—who had killed; *muṣṭika*—Muṣṭika; *vidrāvita*—who drove away; *anya*—other; *malla-ogha*—all the wrestlers; *rāma*—by Balarāma; *pātita*—made to fall down; *kūṭaka*—the wrestler Kūṭa; *namaḥ 95*—obeisance 95.

**Перевод:**

**You finished off Śala and Toṣala and had Balarāma kill Muṣṭika. And after Kūṭa fell to Balarāma, You drove all the other wrestlers away.**

### Text 375

*ucca-mañca-stha-durvṛtta-*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ucca*—high; *mañca*—on his platform; *stha*—standing; *durvṛtta*—wicked; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *durvākya*—by the abusive words; *kopita*—who were angered; *ātta*—lifting; *asi*—his sword; *carma*—and shield; *sañcāri*—pacing back and forth; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *keśa*—the hair; *graha*—in grabbing; *uddhata*—who were bold.

**Перевод:**

**Wicked Kaṁsa, standing high on his platform, angered You with his abusive words. And as he strode back and forth, sword and shield in hand, You boldly grabbed him by the hair.**

*bhūmi-pātita-bhojendra*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhūmi*—on the ground; *pātita*—who made fall; *bhoja-indra*—the king of the Bhojas; *kaṁsa-upari*—on top of Kaṁsa; *vikūrdita*—who jumped; *kaṁsa-dhvaṁsana*—O vanquisher of Kaṁsa; *kaṁsa-are*—O enemy of Kaṁsa; *jaya*—victory to You; *kaṁsa-nisūdana*—O killer of Kaṁsa.

**You threw that king of the Bhojas to the ground and jumped on top of him. O enemy of Kaṁsa, vanquisher of Kaṁsa, killer of Kaṁsa, victory to You!**

### Text 377

*hṛta*—destroyed; *urvī*—of the earth; *bhaya*—the fear; *bhāra*—burden; *ārte*—and distress; *jagat*—of the universe; *śalya*—of the thing that torments; *vināśaka*—O destroyer; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—Your father and mother; *praharṣa-artha*—for pleasing; *mṛta*—dead; *kaṁsa*—Kaṁsa; *vikarṣaka*—who dragged.

**Перевод:**

**You did away with the fear, the burden, the distress of the earth! Destroyer of the tormentor of the universe! You dragged Kaṁsa’s dead body for the pleasure of Your father and mother.**

### Text 378

*brahmeśādi-surānandin*  
*kālanemi-vimukti-da*  
*bala-ghātita-duṣṭāṣṭa-*  
*kaṁsa-sodara pāhi mām*  
*(namaḥ 96)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*brahma-īśa-ādi*—to Brahmā, Śiva, and others; *sura*—the demigods; *ānandin*—O giver of delight; *kālanemi*—to Kālanemi; *vimukti-da*—O giver of liberation; *bala*—by Balarāma; *ghātita*—who caused to be killed; *duṣṭa*—wicked; *aṣṭa*—eight; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *sodara*—the brothers; *pāhi*—please protect; *mām*—me; *namaḥ 96*—obeisance 96.

**Перевод:**

**You delighted the demigods—Brahmā, Śiva, and the rest. You liberated Kālanemi. And You had Kaṁsa’s eight wicked brothers killed by Balarāma. Please protect me. (Obeisance 96)**

**Комментарий:**

In his previous life, Kaṁsa had been the demon Kālanemi. Now killed again by Lord Viṣṇu, Kaṁsa was freed from material existence.

### Text 379

*kaṁsa-yoṣit-samāśvāsinn*  
*ādiṣṭa-mṛta-sat-kriya*  
*pitṛ-mātṛ-padānamra*

*(namaḥ 97)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *yoṣit*—of the wives; *samāśvāsin*—O consoler; *ādiṣṭa*—who gave instructions; *mṛta*—for the dead; *sat-kriya*—about the auspicious rituals; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—of Your father and mother; *pada*—at the feet; *ānamra*—who bowed; *pitṛ*—of Your parents; *bandha*—from their bondage; *vimokṣaka*—O releaser; *namaḥ 97*—obeisance 97.

**Перевод:**

**You consoled Kaṁsa’s wives and gave instructions about the holy rites for the dead king. You bowed down at the feet of Your father and mother and released them from bondage. (Obeisance 97)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe catuś-catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the forty-fourth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

## Chapter 45

### Text 380

*īśa-jñānākṛtāśleṣa-*  
*jananī-tāta-bhāva-vit*  
*sneha-vardhana-miṣṭokti-*  
*pitṛ-mātṛ-pramoda-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*īśa-jñāna*—because of knowing You to be the Supreme Lord; *akṛta*—not doing; *āśleṣa*—embrace; *jananī*—of Your mother; *tāta*—and father; *bhāva*—of the love; *vit*—O knower; *sneha*—their parental affection; *vardhana*—which nourished; *miṣṭa*—sweet; *ukti*—by words; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—of Your father and mother; *pramoda-kṛt*—O giver of pleasure.

**Перевод:**

**Your father and mother didn’t embrace You, because they thought of You as the Supreme Lord. Still, conscious of their love for You, You gave them pleasure with Your sweet words, which nourished their parental affection.**

### Text 381

*prāptāliṅgana-mun-mātṛ-*  
*tāta-kroḍādhiropita*  
*snehāvāk-pitṛ-mātr-aśru-*  
*dhārā-snāpita-mastaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prāpta*—felt; *āliṅgana*—from embracing; *mut*—with bliss; *mātṛ*—of Your mother; *tāta*—and father; *kroḍa*—on the laps; *adhiropita*—who were placed; *sneha*—due to affection; *avāk*—speechless; *pitṛ-mātṛ*—of Your father and mother; *aśru*—of tears; *dhārā*—with a torrent; *snāpita*—bathed; *mastaka*—whose head.

**Перевод:**

**Your mother and father then embraced You and felt bliss and placed You on their laps. Speechless with love, they bathed Your head with a torrent of tears.**

### Text 382

*paramānandita-śrīmad-*  
*devaky-ānakadundubhe*  
*jaya prema-sukhācchādi-*  
*jñāna duḥkha-nivāraka*  
*(namaḥ 98)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*parama*—supreme; *ānandita*—who made to experience bliss; *śrīmat*—blessed; *devakī*—Devakī; *ānakadundubhe—*and Ānakadundubhi (Vasudeva); *jaya*—victory to You; *prema*—of pure love; *sukha*—with the happiness; *ācchādi*—who covered; *jñāna*—their knowledge; *duḥkha*—of their distress; *nivāraka*—O dispeller; *namaḥ 98*—obeisance 98.

**Перевод:**

**You made the blessed Devakī and Vasudeva taste the highest bliss. Victory to You, who covered their knowledge with the joy of pure love and drove away their distress. (Obeisance 98)**

### Text 383

*sad-vākyānandita-śrīmad-*  
*ugrasenādhipatya-da*  
*dattograsena-rājya-śrīr*  
*ugrasena-nideśa-kṛt*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sat*—appropriate; *vākya*—by Your words; *ānandita*—filled with ecstasy; *śrīmat-ugrasena*—to Śrīmān Ugrasena; *ādhipatya*—of the post of emperor; *da*—O giver; *datta*—who bestowed; *ugrasena*—on Ugrasena; *rājya*—of sovereignty; *śrīḥ*—the opulences; *ugrasena*—of Ugrasena; *nideśa-kṛt*—O obeyer of the orders.

**Перевод:**

**With fitting words You filled Śrīmān Ugrasena with ecstasy as You gave him the royal throne. You bestowed on him the opulences of sovereignty and obeyed his commands.**

### Text 384

*prasīdatān me bhagavān*  
*bhakta-vatsala-nāma-dhṛk*  
*ugrasena-vaśānīta-*  
*tri-lokī-ratna-sañcaya*  
*(namaḥ 99)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīdatāt*—may He please be satisfied; *me*—with me; *bhagavān*—the Supreme Personality of Godhead; *bhakta-vatsala*—“concerned friend of the devotees”; *nāma-dhṛk*—O carrier of the name; *ugrasena*—of Ugrasena; *vaśa*—under the control; *ānīta*—which were brought; *tri-lokī*—of the three worlds; *ratna*—the treasures; *sañcaya*—who collected; *namaḥ 99*—obeisance 99.

**Перевод:**

**O Supreme Personality of Godhead, You bear the name “concerned friend of Your devotees.” Please be satisfied with me. You brought the three worlds under the control of Ugrasena and collected all their treasures for him. (Obeisance 99)**

### Text 385

*ānīta-kaṁsa-santrāsa-*  
*proṣita-jñāti-bāndhava*  
*jaya sammānitāśeṣa-*  
*yādavāvāsa-dāyaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ānīta*—who brought back; *kaṁsa*—of Kaṁsa; *santrāsa*—out of fear; *proṣita*—gone into exile; *jñāti*—Your blood relatives; *bāndhava*—and relatives by marriage; *jaya*—victory to You; *sammānita*—honored; *aśeṣa*—all; *yādava*—to the Yādavas; *āvāsa*—of places to live; *dāyaka*—O giver.

**Перевод:**

**Out of fear of Kaṁsa, both Your blood relatives and Your relatives by marriage had gone into exile, and You brought them back. Victory to You, who honored all the Yādavas and gave them places to live.**

### Text 386

*sadā-dayā-smitālokā-*  
*nanditākhila-yādava*  
*jaya roga-jarā-glāni-*  
*hāri-sandarśanāmṛta*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sadā*—perpetually; *dayā*—merciful; *smita*—smiling; *āloka*—by whose glance; *ānandita*—made blissful; *akhila*—all; *yādava*—the Yādavas; *jaya*—victory to You; *roga*—disease; *jarā*—old age; *glāni*—and disappointment; *hāri*—which dispelled; *sandarśana*—of whose countenance; *amṛta*—the nectar.

**Перевод:**

**By Your ever-merciful smiling glance You kept all the Yādavas in bliss. And by the nectar of Your countenance You dispelled disease, old age, and disappointment. Victory to You!**

### Text 387

*prasīda sātvata-śreṣṭha*  
*yādavendra prasīda me*  
*vṛṣṇi-puṅgava māṁ pāhi*  
*dāśārhādhipa mādhava*

**Пословный перевод:**

*prasīda*—be satisfied; *sātvata-śreṣṭha*—O best of the Sātvatas; *yādava-indra*—O chief of the Yādavas; *prasīda*—be satisfied; *me*—with me; *vṛṣṇi-puṅgava*—O hero of the Vṛṣṇis; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *dāśārha-adhipa*—O leader of the Dāśārhas; *mādhava*—O Mādhava.

**Перевод:**

**Be satisfied with me, O best of the Sātvatas. O chief of the Yādavas, be satisfied with me. O Mādhava, hero of the Vṛṣṇis, leader of the Dāśārhas, please protect me.**

### Text 388

*kukurāndhaka-vaṁśendra*  
*bhaimānvaya-vivardhana*  
*yayāti-kula-padmārka*  
*candra-vaṁśābdhi-candramaḥ*  
*(namaḥ 100)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kukura-andhaka*—of the Kukuras and Andhakas; *vaṁśa*—of the dynasties; *indra*—O chief; *bhaima*—coming from Bhīma; *anvaya*—of the descendants; *vivardhana*—O promoter; *yayāti-kula*—of the Yayāti clan; *padma*—for the lotuses; *arka*—O sun; *candra-vaṁśa*—of the moon dynasty; *abdhi*—from the ocean; *candramaḥ*—O moon; *namaḥ 100*—obeisance 100.

**Перевод:**

**You are the chief of the dynasties of Kukura and Andhaka, the promoter of the descendants of Bhīma, the sun for the lotuses of Yayāti’s clans, and the moon born from the dynasty of the moon. (Obeisance 100)**

**Комментарий:**

The Bhīma mentioned here is not the son of Pāṇḍu but a king of the Sātvata dynasty.

### Text 389

*jaya śrī-mathurā-nātha*  
*mathurā-maṅgala prabho*  
*madhurā-mūrta-mādhurya*  
*mathurā-maṇḍaleśvara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*jaya*—victory to You; *śrī-mathurā-nātha*—O master of Śrī Mathurā; *mathurā*—of Mathurā; *maṅgala*—O source of good fortune; *prabho*—O Lord; *madhurā*—for the city of sweetness; *mūrta*—personified; *mādhurya*—O sweetness; *mathurā-maṇḍala*—of the Mathurā district; *īśvara*—O sovereign.

**Перевод:**

**Victory to You, O Lord, master of Śrī Mathurā, source of Mathurā’s good fortune, sweetness personified for the city of sweetness, sovereign of the district of Mathurā!**

### Text 390

*nitya-śrī-mathurā-vāsin*  
*madhurā-mādhurī-prada*  
*he māthura-mahā-bhāgya*  
*namas te mathurā-pate*  
*(namaḥ 101)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nitya*—always; *śrī-mathurā*—in Śrī Mathurā; *vāsin*—who reside; *madhurā*—of the city of sweetness; *mādhurī*—of the charm; *prada*—O giver; *he*—O; *māthura*—for the residents of Mathurā; *mahā-bhāgya*—who are everything good; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *te*—to You; *mathurā-pate*—O ruler of Mathurā; *namaḥ 101*—obeisance 101.

**Перевод:**

**By always residing in Śrī Mathurā, You give that sweet city its special charm. For the Mathurā-vāsīs You are everything good. O ruler of Mathurā, I bow down to You. (Obeisance 101)**

### Text 391

*adya-śvo-gamana-vyāja-*  
*rakṣita-vraja-nāyaka*  
*prasīda muhur-āśleṣa-*  
*nanda-sambhāṣaṇākula*

**Пословный перевод:**

*adya*—today; *śvaḥ*—or tomorrow; *gamana*—of coming; *vyāja*—on whose pretense; *rakṣita*—saved; *vraja-nāyaka*—the king of Vraja; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *muhuḥ*—repeated; *āśleṣa*—with embraces; *nanda*—to Nanda; *sambhāṣaṇa*—by speaking; *ākula*—who were agitated.

**Перевод:**

**You saved the king of Vraja by the pretense that You would come home that very day or the next. You were shaken as you spoke with Nanda, embracing him again and again.**

### Text 392

*nānā-vāk-cāturī-dīna-*  
*nanda-rodana-vardhana*  
*aty-āliṅgana-gopāla-*  
*kula-duḥkhāśru-vāhaka*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nānā*—in various ways; *vāk*—in speaking; *cāturī*—by Your cleverness; *dīna*—poor; *nanda*—of Nanda; *rodana*—of the crying; *vardhana*—O increaser; *ati-āliṅgana*—by embracing profusely; *gopāla-kula*—of all the cowherds; *duḥkha*—of sorrow; *aśru*—tears; *vāhaka*—who brought.

**Перевод:**

**Your various clever attempts to speak with poor Nanda only made him cry all the more. And when You profusely embraced all the cowherds, You brought them to tears of grief.**

### Text 393

*muhur-muhyat-patad-vṛddha-*  
*nanda-sāntvana-kātara*  
*vāso-’laṅkāra-kupyādi-*  
*dāna-mārita-nanda he*

**Пословный перевод:**

*muhuḥ*—repeatedly; *muhyat*—fainting; *patat*—and falling; *vṛddha*—elderly; *nanda*—of Nanda; *sāntvana*—for consoling; *kātara*—who were anxious; *vāsaḥ*—of clothing; *alaṅkāra*—ornaments; *kupya*—metalware; *ādi*—and so on; *dāna*—by the giving; *mārita*—who drove to the verge of death; *nanda*—Nanda; *he*—O.

**Перевод:**

**Anxious to console elderly Nanda, who fainted and fell to the ground again and again, You offered gifts such as clothing, ornaments, and metalware—but that drove him to the verge of death.**

### Text 394

*hā-hā-mahā-ravākrandi-*  
*gopa-vṛndātma-śoka-da*  
*jala-sekādy-upānīta-*  
*nanda-prāṇa prasīda me*

**Пословный перевод:**

*hā-hā*—of *“*alas, alas”; *mahā-rava*—with the loud cries; *ākrandi*—sobbing; *gopa-vṛnda*—to all the cowherds; *ātma*—on Your account; *śoka-da*—O giver of sorrow; *jala*—of water; *seka*—by sprinkling; *ādi*—and so on; *upānīta*—who brought back; *nanda*—of Nanda; *prāṇa*—the life; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *me*—with me.

**Перевод:**

**You made all the cowherd men sorrow for You, sobbing with loud cries of “Alas, alas!” And by sprinkling Nanda with water, and doing whatever else You had to do, You brought him back to life. Please be satisfied with me.**

### Text 395

*tvarāgamana-satyokti-*  
*viśvastī-kṛta-nanda mām*  
*pārśve rakṣa su-sandeśa-*  
*yaśodā-dainya-vardhana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tvarā*—with haste; *āgamana*—of Your return; *satya-ukti*—by promises; *viśvastī-kṛta*—who won the confidence; *nanda*—of Nanda; *mām*—me; *pārśve*—by Your side; *rakṣa*—please keep; *su-sandeśa*—by Your well-intentioned message; *yaśodā*—of Yaśodā; *dainya*—of the wretchedness; *vardhana*—O increaser.

**Перевод:**

**You won Nanda’s confidence with Your promise to come back quickly. Please keep me always by Your side. You made Yaśodā’s misery still greater with Your well-intentioned message.**

### Text 396

*muhur-muhuḥ-parāvarta-*  
*māna-nandāśru-sampluta*  
*nandānuvrajana-vyāja*  
*vraja-dīna-janāsu-da*

**Пословный перевод:**

*muhuḥ-muhuḥ*—again and again; *parāvartamāna*—turning back; *nanda*—of Nanda; *aśru*—with the tears; *sampluta*—who were drenched; *nanda*—Nanda; *anuvrajana*—of following; *vyāja*—whose pretense; *vraja*—of Vraja; *dīna*—miserable; *jana*—of the people; *asu*—of the life airs; *da*—O restorer.

**Перевод:**

**Again and again Nanda turned back and drenched You with his tears. And as he left, You restored to the poor Vraja-vāsīs their life airs by pretending to follow.**

### Text 397

*gopy-artha-preṣita-svīya-*  
*bhūṣā-śapatha-vācika*  
*nirudhyamāna-netrābja-*  
*vāri-dhāra prasīda me*  
*(namaḥ 102)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopī-artha*—for the *gopīs; preṣita*—sent; *svīya*—Your own; *bhūṣā*—ornaments; *śapatha*—of Your promise; *vācika*—O speaker; *nirudhyamāna*—held back; *netra-abja*—from Your lotus eyes; *vāri-dhāra*—a flood of tears; *prasīda*—please be satisfied; *me*—with me; *namaḥ 102*—obeisance 102.

**Перевод:**

**You sent the gopīs Your own ornaments and Your promise, and held back a flood of tears from Your lotus eyes. Please be satisfied with me. (Obeisance 102)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti daśama-skandhe pañca-catvāriṁśo ’dhyāyaḥ*

**Thus ends the forty-fifth chapter of the Tenth Canto.**

*iti śrī-bhāgavatam*

**Thus ends the summary of** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.***

*Śrī Śrī-jagannātha-deva-stavaḥ*  
*(Śrī-nīlācala-nātha-stutiḥ)*

***Prayer to Śrī Jagannātha-deva***  
***(Praise of the Lord of Nīlācala)***

### Text 398

*śrī-jagannātha nīlādri-*  
*śiro-mukuṭa-ratna he*  
*dāru-brahman ghana-śyāma*  
*prasīda puruṣottama*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrī-jagannātha*—O Śrī Jagannātha; *nīla*—blue; *adri*—of the mountain; *śiraḥ*—on the head; *mukuṭa-ratna he*—O crown jewel; *dāru-brahman*—O Supreme Truth in wood; *ghana*—like a rain cloud; *śyāma*—who are dark blue; *prasīda*—please be merciful; *puruṣa-uttama*—O Supreme Personality of Godhead.

**Перевод:**

**O Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Jagannātha, crown jewel on the head of the blue mountain! O Supreme Truth in wood, dark blue Lord, please be merciful to me.**

### Text 399

*praphulla-puṇḍarīkākṣa*  
*lavaṇābdhi-taṭāmṛta*  
*guṭikodara māṁ pāhi*  
*nānā-bhoga-purandara*

**Пословный перевод:**

*praphulla*—fully blossomed; *puṇḍarīka*—like lotuses; *akṣa*—whose eyes; *lavaṇa-abdhi*—of the salt ocean; *taṭa*—on the shore; *amṛta*—O nectar; *guṭikā*—a little ball; *udara*—in whose interior; *mām*—me; *pāhi*—please protect; *nānā*—various; *bhoga*—of enjoyments; *purandara*—O master.

**Перевод:**

**Your eyes are wide open like lotus petals. You are the nectar on the shore of the salt ocean. Within Your body there is a little ball. O master of every sort of enjoyment, please protect me.**

**Комментарий:**

The little ball mentioned here is a Śālagrāma-śilā placed in the abdomen of Lord Jagannātha during the ceremony performed every twelve years to renew His body.

### Text 400

*nijādhara-sudhā-dāyinn*  
*indradyumna-prasādita*  
*subhadrā-lālana-vyagra*  
*rāmānuja namo ’stu te*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nija*—Your; *adhara*—from the lips; *sudhā*—of the nectar; *dāyin*—O bestower; *indradyumna*—by King Indradyumna; *prasādita*—who were satisfied; *subhadrā*—of Subhadrā; *lālana*—for taking care; *vyagra*—who are anxious; *rāma-anuja*—O younger brother of Balarāma; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *astu*—let there be; *te*—unto You.

**Перевод:**

**O bestower of the nectar of Your lips, I bow down to You, who were pleased by King Indradyumna. O younger brother of Baladeva, You are always anxious to care for Subhadrā.**

### Text 401

*guṇḍicā-ratha-yātrādi-*  
*mahotsava-vivardhana*  
*bhakta-vatsala vande tvāṁ*  
*guṇḍicā-ratha-maṇḍanam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*guṇḍicā*—to Guṇḍicā; *ratha*—of the chariot; *yātra*—the journey; *ādi*—and so on; *mahā-utsava*—of great festivals; *vivardhana*—O expander; *bhakta-vatsala*—O affectionate friend of Your devotees; *vande*—I offer respects; *tvām*—to You; *guṇḍicā*—to Guṇḍicā; *ratha*—on the chariot; *maṇḍanam*—who are resplendent.

**Перевод:**

**You expand wonderful festivals like Your chariot journey to Guṇḍicā. I offer respects to You, who are affectionate to Your devotees and resplendent on the Guṇḍicā chariot.**

### Text 402

*dīna-hīna-mahā-nīca-*  
*dayārdrī-kṛta-mānasa*  
*nitya-nūtana-māhātmya-*  
*darśiñ caitanya-vallabha*  
*(namaḥ 103)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dīna*—wretched; *hīna*—and hopeless; *mahā-nīca*—for the most fallen; *dayā*—with compassion; *ārdrī-kṛta*—melted; *mānasa*—whose heart; *nitya-nūtana*—ever-fresh; *māhātmya*—of glories; *darśin*—O displayer; *caitanya-vallabha*—O beloved of Śrī Caitanya; *namaḥ 103*—obeisance 103.

**Перевод:**

**Your heart melts with compassion for the wretched, hopeless, and most fallen. O displayer of ever-fresh glories, O beloved of Śrī Caitanya! (Obeisance 103)**

**Комментарий:**

*Śrī Śrīmac-caitanya-deva-stavaḥ*

***Prayer to Śrīmān Caitanyadeva***

### Text 403

*śrīmac-caitanya-deva tvāṁ*  
*vande gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*śacī-nandana māṁ trāhi*  
*yati-cūḍāmaṇe prabho*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrīmat*—divine; *caitanya-deva*—O Lord Caitanya; *tvām*—to You; *vande*—I offer respects; *gaura-aṅga*—with Your golden limbs; *sundara*—who are beautiful; *śacī-nandana*—O darling son of Śacī; *mām*—me; *trāhi*—please save; *yati*—of *sannyāsīs; cūḍāmaṇe*—O crest-jewel; *prabho*—O my Lord.

**Перевод:**

**Divine Lord Caitanya, beautiful Lord of golden limbs, I offer my respects to You. O darling son of Śacī, O master, crest jewel of sannyāsīs, please save me.**

### Text 404

*ā-jānu-bāho smerāsya*  
*nīlācala-vibhūṣaṇa*  
*jagat-pravartita-svādu-*  
*bhagavan-nāma-kīrtana*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ā-jānu*—down to Your knees; *bāho*—whose arms; *smera*—smiling; *āsya*—whose face; *nīlācala*—of Purī; *vibhūṣaṇa*—O ornament; *jagat*—in the world; *pravartita*—who initiated; *svādu*—sweet; *bhagavat-nāma*—of the names of the Supreme Lord; *nāma-kīrtana*—the chanting.

**Перевод:**

**Your arms reach down to Your knees, and a smile lights Your face. O ornament of Nīlācala, You brought to this world the sweet chanting of the Supreme Lord’s names.**

### Text 405

*advaitācārya-saṁślāghin*  
*sārvabhaumābhinandaka*  
*rāmānanda-kṛta-prīta*  
*sarva-vaiṣṇava-bāndhava*

**Пословный перевод:**

*advaita-ācārya*—of Advaitācārya; *saṁślāghin*—O glorifier; *sārvabhauma*—of Sārvabhauma; *abhinandaka*—O pleaser; *rāmānanda*—to Rāmānanda Rāya; *kṛta*—who showed; *prīta*—affection; *sarva-vaiṣṇava*—of all the Vaiṣṇavas; *bāndhava*—O friend.

**Перевод:**

### Text 406

**Пословный перевод:**

***Confession of the Pain of Helplessness***

### Text 417-418

*śrī-kṛṣṇa tava kāruṇya-*  
*mahimne me namo namaḥ*  
*yo māṁ nīcaṁ durācāraṁ*  
*nitya-pāpa-rataṁ śaṭham*  
*aho tasyā avasthāyāḥ*  
*satām iva daśām imām*  
*tasmāt sthānād idaṁ sthānaṁ*  
*mathurā-maṇḍalaṁ śubham*

**Пословный перевод:**

*śrī-kṛṣṇa*—O Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *tava*—Your; *kāruṇya*—of the mercy; *mahimne*—to the greatness; *me*—my; *namaḥ namaḥ*—repeated obeisances; *yaḥ*—who; *mām*—me; *nīcam*—lowly; *durācāram*—addicted to bad behavior; *nitya*—always; *pāpa-ratam*—fond of sinful activity; *śaṭham*—malicious; *aho*—alas; *tasyāḥ*—that; *avasthāyāḥ*—from the situation; *satām*—of saintly devotees; *iva*—like; *daśām*—the condition; *imām*—this; *tasmāt*—that; *sthānāt*—from the place; *idam*—this; *sthānam*—to the place; *mathurā-maṇḍalam*—to Mathurā-maṇḍala; *śubham*—auspicious.

**Перевод:**

**O Śrī Kṛṣṇa, I offer my repeated obeisances to the wonder of Your mercy. You took me—a fallen, wicked person, a cheater, always addicted to sinful ways—and lifted me from such depravity to an elevated state like that of Your saintly devotees. You took me from the terrible place where I lived and brought me to this most auspicious place, Mathurā-maṇḍala.**

### Text 419

*yasmiñ jñāna-kṛtaṁ vāpi*  
*sarva-pāpaṁ na tiṣṭhati*  
*caturdhā yatra muktiḥ syāt*  
*tvaṁ ca sannihitaḥ sadā*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yasmin*—where; *jñāna*—in knowledge; *kṛtam*—done; *vā api*—even; *sarva*—all; *pāpam*—sins; *na tiṣṭhati*—do not persist; *caturdhā*—in four ways; *yatra*—where; *muktiḥ*—liberation; *syāt*—comes about; *tvam*—You; *ca*—and; *sannihitaḥ*—present; *sadā*—always.

**Перевод:**

**Sins cannot persist in Mathurā, even those intentionally performed. In Mathurā liberation can be achieved in four ways, and You are always present.**

**Комментарий:**

A person born, initiated, deceased, or cremated in Mathurā is guaranteed liberation from the cycle of birth and death.

### Text 420

*yasmin sva-san-mahimnevā-*  
*rpito vasasi nityadā*  
*nija-mādhurya-sampattyā*  
*madhureti yad ucyate*

**Пословный перевод:**

*yasmin*—where; *sva*—Your; *sat*—transcendental; *mahimnā*—by the glories; *iva*—as if; *arpitaḥ*—deposited; *vasasi*—You live; *nityadā*—always; *nija*—Your; *mādhurya*—of the sweetness; *sampattyā*—with the wealth; *madhurā*—Madhurā; *iti*—thus; *yat*—which; *ucyate*—is called.

**Перевод:**

**You live here always, as if placed here by the force of Your divine glories. Because this abode has all the wealth of Your sweetness, it is called Madhurā \[Mathurā\].**

### Text 421

*tathā tasmāc ca duḥsaṅgād*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tathā*—so; *tasmāt*—therefore; *ca*—and; *duḥsaṅgāt*—due to bad association; *yaḥ*—who; *tvat*—Your; *priya-tamasya*—of the most beloved; *hi*—indeed; *śrīmat-caitanya-devasya*—Śrī Caitanyadeva; *saṅgam*—the association; *nīlācale*—in Nīlācala; *tathā*—then.

**Перевод:**

**I was stained by my past bad company, so You gave me the company of Your most beloved Śrī Caitanyadeva in Nīlācala.**

### Text 422

*rathopari tava śrīman-*  
*mukha-darśana-kautukam*  
*punar vṛndāvanaṁ hy etat*  
*tat-tat-krīḍāspadaṁ tava*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ratha-upari*—on the chariot; *tava*—Your; *śrīmat*—divine; *mukha*—of the face; *darśana*—of seeing; *kautukam*—the delight; *punaḥ*—again; *vṛndāvanam*—to Vṛndāvana; *hi*—indeed; *etat*—this; *tat-tat—*various; *krīḍā*—of pastimes; *āspadam*—the abode; *tava*—Your.

**Перевод:**

**You gave me the delight of seeing Your blessed face as You sat on Your chariot. Then You brought me back to this Vṛndāvana, the place of so many of Your pastimes.**

### Text 423

*gopikā yasya sat-kīrtiṁ*  
*bhavāṁś cāvarṇayan guṇān*  
*dūra-sthāḥ śravaṇād yasya*  
*labhante prema te śubhāḥ*

**Пословный перевод:**

*gopikāḥ*—the *gopīs; yasya*—whose; *sat-kīrtim*—transcendental fame; *bhavān*—You; *ca*—also; *avarṇayan*—described; *guṇān*—the qualities; *dūra-sthāḥ*—persons situated far away; *śravaṇāt*—by hearing; *yasya*—for whom; *labhante*—attain; *prema*—love; *te*—for You; *śubhāḥ*—becoming all-auspicious.

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs have described Vṛndāvana’s transcendental fame and qualities, and so have You. Hearing of these, even persons living far away attain love for You and become all-auspicious.**

**Комментарий:**

The *gopīs* have glorified Vṛndāvana-dhāma in such words as these:

*akṣaṇvatāṁ phalam idaṁ na paraṁ vidāmaḥ*  
*sakhyaḥ paśūn anuviveśayator vayasyaiḥ*  
*vaktraṁ vrajeśa-sutayor anu-veṇu-juṣṭaṁ*  
*yair vā nipītam anurakta-kaṭākṣa-mokṣam*

**“O friends, those eyes that see the beautiful faces of the sons of Mahārāja Nanda are certainly fortunate. As these two sons, surrounded by Their friends, enter the forest, driving the cows before Them, They hold Their flutes to Their mouths and glance lovingly upon the residents of Vṛndāvana. For those who have eyes, we think there is no greater object of vision.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.21.7)**

Śrī Kṛṣṇa has corroborated this opinion in many of His own statements, such as this:

*aho amī deva-varāmarārcitaṁ*  
*pādāmbujaṁ te sumanaḥ-phalārhaṇam*  
*namanty upādāya śikhābhir ātmanas*  
*tamo-’pahatyai taru-janma yat-kṛtam*

**“O greatest of Lords \[Balarāma\], just see how these trees bow their heads at Your lotus feet, which are worshiped by the immortal demigods. The trees offer You their fruits and flowers to dispel the dark ignorance that has caused their birth as trees.” (*****Bhāgavatam*** **10.15.5)**

### Text 424

*carācaraṁ prāṇi-jātaṁ*  
*yasya tvat-prema-samplutam*  
*nityam adyāpi yasmiṁs tv a-*  
*pūrva-vat krīḍasi sphuṭam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*cara-acaram*—moving and nonmoving; *prāṇi-jātam*—all living entities; *yasya*—where; *tvat*—Your; *prema*—in pure love; *samplutam*—immersed; *nityam*—eternally; *adya*—now; *api*—even; *yasmin*—where; *tu*—and; *apūrva-vat*—as if never before; *krīḍasi*—You play; *sphuṭam*—manifestly.

**Перевод:**

**All creatures that live here, moving and nonmoving, are immersed in pure love for You. Here You can be seen playing always, even today, as if You had never played Your pastimes before.**

### Text 425

*atraiva tvat-priyaṁ yaś ca*  
*mad-eka-dhana-jīvanam*  
*prāpayan me punaḥ saṅgaṁ*  
*tasmai nityaṁ namo namaḥ*

**Пословный перевод:**

*atra*—here; *eva*—indeed; *tvat*—Your; *priyam*—the beloved devotee; *yaḥ*—who; *ca*—and; *mat*—my; *eka-dhana*—only wealth; *jīvanam*—and very life; *prāpayan*—arranging to obtain; *me*—for me; *punaḥ*—again; *saṅgam*—the association; *tasmai*—to You; *nityam*—always; *namaḥ namaḥ*—repeated obeisances.

**Перевод:**

**Here You returned to me the company of Your beloved devotee, the only wealth of my life. I will always offer You my obeisances, over and over again.**

**Комментарий:**

In Vṛndāvana Śrīla Sanātana Gosvāmī rejoined his dear brother, Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, after long separation.

### Text 426

*kadācic caraṇāmbhojaṁ*  
*hṛdi me smārayaty api*

**Пословный перевод:**

*adhunā*—now; *yaḥ*—who; *mama*—my; *mukhāt*—from the mouth; *niḥsārayati*—causes to emerge; *nāma*—the name; *te*—Your; *kadācit*—sometimes; *caraṇa-ambhojam*—the lotus feet; *hṛdi*—in the heart; *me*—my; *smārayati*—caused to be remembered; *api*—also.

**Перевод:**

**Now You make Your name appear from my mouth, and sometimes You allow me to remember Your lotus feet in my heart.**

### Text 427

*mat-kāyenādhamenāpi*  
*namas te kārayed ayam*  
*sarvāpadbhyo ’pi māṁ rakṣed*  
*dadyāt te bhakti-sampadam*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mat*—my; *kāyena*—by the body; *adhamena*—most inferior; *api*—even; *namaḥ*—obeisances; *te*—to You; *kārayet*—should be offered; *ayam*—these; *sarva*—from all; *āpadbhyaḥ*—dangers; *api*—even; *mām*—me; *rakṣet*—please protect; *dadyāt*—please give; *te*—Your; *bhakti*—of devotional service; *sampadam*—the treasure.

**Перевод:**

**Even though my body is most lowly, please engage it in offering You these obeisances. Please protect me from all dangers and give me the treasure of devotional service to You.**

### Text 428-430

*dātuṁ śaknoti me ’jasraṁ*  
*prema-smaraṇa-kīrtanam*  
*tava prema-kaṭākṣaṁ ca*  
*mayi prāpayituṁ kṣamaḥ*  
*go-gopa-gopikāsaktaṁ*  
*tvāṁ ca darśayituṁ prabhuḥ*  
*evaṁ yo mama hīnasya*  
*sarvāśālambanaṁ param*  
*mahā-kāruṇya-mahimā*  
*purāṇo nitya-nūtanaḥ*  
*tvadīyaḥ sac-cid-ānandas*  
*tasmai nityaṁ namo namaḥ*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dātum*—to give; *śaknoti*—can; *me*—to me; *ajasram*—constant; *prema*—in love; *smaraṇa*—remembrance; *kīrtanam*—and glorification; *tava*—of You; *prema*—loving; *kaṭa-akṣam*—the sidelong glance; *ca*—and; *mayi*—to me; *prāpayitum*—of causing the obtaining; *kṣamaḥ*—capable; *go*—to the cows; *gopa*—cowherds; *gopikā*—and cowherdesses; *āsaktam*—who are attached; *tvām*—You; *ca*—and; *darśayitum*—for showing; *prabhuḥ*—competent; *evam*—thus; *yaḥ*—who; *mama*—of me; *hīnasya*—who am helpless; *sarva*—all; *āśā*—of the hopes; *ālambanam*—the resort; *param*—supreme; *mahā*—supreme; *kāruṇya*—of the mercy; *mahimā*—the greatness; *purāṇaḥ*—ancient; *nitya-nūtanaḥ*—ever-fresh; *tvadīyaḥ*—Your; *sat*—of eternity; *cit*—of knowledge; *ānandaḥ*—and bliss; *tasmai*—to You; *nityam*—constantly; *namaḥ namaḥ*—repeated obeisances.

**Перевод:**

**Your mercy is a great wonder, nondifferent from You. It is pure eternity, knowledge, and bliss. It can empower me to always remember and glorify You in pure love. It can obtain for me Your loving sidelong glance. It can enable me to see You immersed in the company of the cows and the cowherd boys and girls. Thus for a helpless person like me it is the ultimate resort of all hopes. Your wonderful mercy is ancient and eternal but also ever new and fresh. To Your mercy, again and again, I offer my obeisances forever.**

### Text 431-432

*etal-līlā-stavaṁ nāma*  
*stotraṁ śrī-kṛṣṇa tārakam*  
*praṇāmāṣṭottara-śate*  
*yo ’rthāvagama-pūrvakam*  
*kīrtayet so ’cirād bhakto*  
*labhatāṁ kṛpayā tava*  
*rūpe nāmani līlāyām*  
*ākrīḍe ’pi parāṁ ratim*  
*(namaḥ 108)*

**Пословный перевод:**

*etat*—this; *līlā*—of the pastimes; *stavam*—the praise; *nāma*—so named; *stotram*—the prayer; *śrī-kṛṣṇa*—O Śrī Kṛṣṇa; *tārakam*—which bestows liberation; *praṇāma*—of obeisances; *aṣṭa-uttara*—plus eight; *śate*—there being one hundred; *yaḥ*—one who; *artha*—of the meaning; *avagama-pūrvakam*—with understanding; *kīrtayet*—chants; *saḥ*—he; *acirāt*—quickly; *bhaktaḥ*—the devotee; *labhatām*—can obtain; *kṛpayā*—by the mercy; *tava*—Your; *rūpe*—for Your form; *nāmani*—names; *līlāyām*—pastimes; *ākrīḍe*—and place of enjoyment; *api*—also; *parām*—transcendental; *ratim*—attraction; *namaḥ* 108—obeisance 108.

**Перевод:**

**O Śrī Kṛṣṇa, any devotee who chants these prayers for deliverance, known as Your Līlā-stava, understanding their meaning, and offers 108 obeisances, will by Your mercy quickly attain divine attraction to Your beautiful form, names, and pastimes and the place where You enjoy. (Obeisance 108)**

**Комментарий:**

*iti śrī-śrī-kṛṣṇa-līlā-stava-nāma stotraṁ samāptam*

**Thus ends the prayer known as** ***Śrī Kṛṣṇa-līlā-stava.***

## Application 1

**Variant readings of the text of** ***Śrī Kṛṣṇa-līlā-stava***

*In preparing this edition of* Śrī Kṛṣṇa-līlā-stava, *I consulted three printed editions, published by Haridāsa Dāsa, the Gaudiya Mission, and by Kusuma-sarovaravālā Kṛṣṇadāsa (see the Bibliography for details). The first two of these editions were published in Bengali script, the third in Devanāgarī. Listed below are the variants found among those editions. Haridāsa dāsa additionally cites occasional variant readings.*

*The editions and manuscripts are identified in this appendix by the following letters:*

*h Haridāsa Dāsa edition*

*g Gaudiya Mission edition*

k Kusuma-sarovaravālā Kṛṣṇadāsa edition

a alternative readings given by Haridāsa Dāsa

\+ corroborated by the commentary

*The first column of the listing gives the number for the verse of the variant, followed by a letter (a, b, c…) to indicate the* pāda *(usually, the quarter verse). The next column gives the reading chosen for this edition, preceded by a + sign when the choice is shown correct by the commentary. The last column gives the variant, preceded by one or more letters indicating its source or sources. When the variant is obviously wrong grammatically or syntactically, it is given in brackets.*

*Before considering variants, I have first transliterated the Sanskrit into roman characters according to the following standards used by the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust: Pāṇini’s options for doubling consonants are never applied, the optional conversion of* anusvāra *to nasal stops is applied always inside words and never at the end of words,* avagrahas *are indicated only at the beginning of words and only when they stand for a deleted short* a, *hyphens are added to indicate compounded words, and the Bengali tendency to write* anusvāra *for the letter* m *at the end of* pādas *is overlooked. After transliteration, any texts that don’t match count as variants.*
